iṅṅaṉam or uttiyōkatar orukāl tamatu karutti oru kaḻukuvaṭiviṉ mēl niṟuttiṉār. uṭaṉē ak kaḻukiṉ uyarnta vaṭivam am melliya takaṭṭilē pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟiyatu. maṟṟoru takaṭṭil oru kutiri mukattiṉ orupakkattu niḻal kāṇappaṭṭatu. eṉṟu pāraṭak eṉṉum am makāpaṇṭitar kūṟukiṉṟār. tām vittirukkum am melliya takaṭukaḷilē ciṟcila camayaṅkaḷil mukaṅkaḷ pala vantu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, orumuṟi oru tāyiṉ niṉiviṉāl ōr iṟanta kuḻantiyiṉ vaṭivam tōṉṟalāyiṟṟeṉṟum, avar moḻikiṉṟaṉar. orukāl cūkkumavuṭampiṟ ceṉṟu pārttataṉāl patinta ōr uruvattippaṟṟip piṉ varumāṟu avar kūṟukiṉṟār.
piyūkāreṭ eṉṉum ūriṟ pirapala vittiya cāttirapaṇṭitarāy irukkum āṭiyū eṉpavarāl, oruvar ōriṭattil niṉitta niṉivu tolivilirunta piṟitoru takaṭṭiṟceṉṟu patintu tōṉṟiṉa viyappāṉa nikaḻcci terivikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. toliviṟ ṟōṉṟum uṇarvu nikaḻccikaḷi ārāyntaṟivatil viruppam vittamiyālē avarum avartam naṇparāṉa iṭrāṭi paṇṭitarum, an nikaḻccikaḷip paṭam piṭittup pārkkum ārāycci muṟiyāl, oru niṉiviṉ uruvattit toliviṟ cittañ ceytu vikkappaṭṭa melliya takaṭṭiṉmēṟ patiyavikkak kūṭumā eṉpataṉi uṟutip paṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉat tīrmāṉañ ceytārkaḷ. itaṉic ceytu pārppataṟkeṉṟu kuṟippikkap paṭṭa mālikkālam vantatu. āṭiyū paṇṭitar tām paṭukkikkup pōku muṉṉarē niḻaṟ paṭam eṭukkun tamatu karuviyit tamatu paṭukkiyiṉ pakkattē vittār. iṭrāṭi paṇṭitarō avari viṭṭup pirintu pōyp pala nūṟumil tūrattiṟku appāl iruntaṉar. ivar tam naṇparōṭu ceytukoṇṭa ēṟpāṭṭiṉpaṭi tām tūṅkaccellum muṉpē, piyūkāreṭ eṉṉum ūril tam naṇparāṟ cittañ ceytu vikkappaṭṭirukkum melliya takaṭṭiṉ mēl tamaturuvam paṭumpaṭi niṉittut tamatu niṉivi orumukap paṭuttiyiruntār. maṟunāṭ kāliyil avar viḻitteḻunta piṟaku, tamatu kaṉavil ataṉi uṇmiyeṉṟu kaṇṭamiyālē tamatu muyaṟci niṟivēṟiyateṉat teḷintār. uṭaṉē avar tammiruvarukkun terinta maṟṟoru naṇparukku ataṉi eḻutit terivikkavē, anta naṇpar āṭiyū paṇṭitar iruppiṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟu, avar ammelliya takaṭṭiṟ patinta vaṭivam pulappaṭumāṟu ceyvikkum muyaṟciyilirukkak kaṇṭār. at takaṭṭiṉmēl tiruttamāṉa mūṉṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa; avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu mikat teḷivāyum uyiruḷḷatu pōṉṟum iruntatu. aḵtu iṭrāṭi paṇṭitar niḻaluruk karuvi* (photographic camera) yik kūrntu nōkkuvatāka viḷaṅkak kāṭṭiyatu; ak karuviyiṉ kaṭici muṉiyāṉatu at tōṟṟattiliruntu varuvatupōṟ ṟōṉṟiya oruvakiyāṉa miṉṉoḷiyāṟ ṟulaṅkiyatu. iṭrāṭi paṇṭitar piyūkāreṭ eṉṉum ūrukkut tirumpivantapōtu āvivaṭivāṉa tamatu paṭattiṉ uruva voṟṟumiyiṉik kaṇṭu mikavum viyappaṭintār; antap paṭamō vaḻakkamāṉa muṟikaḷāl eṭukkappaṭum maṟṟi niḻaṟpaṭaṅkaḷiviṭa, avar mukattiṉ accu aṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷiyum ēṉic ciṟappuk kuṟikaḷiyum teḷivuṟak kāṭṭiyatu. iṅṅaṉam pirāñcu tēcattil pukaḻpeṟṟa paṇṭitarāy viḷaṅkum pāraṭak avarkaḷāl uṟutippaṭuttap paṭṭirukkum uṇmikaḷi uṟṟu nōkkum evartām aṟivukkum uruvam uṇṭeṉpati uṇarāmaṟpōvar?
iṉṉum ic cūkkuma oḷiyākāyattiṉ iyalpukaḷi iṉitu ārāyntu avaṟṟit tamatu aṉupavattiṟ koṇṭu vantirukkum jē. mākluṭ kirēk eṉṉum amerikka vittuvāṉ, ivvoḷi yākāyattiṉ ṟaṉmiyi virit turitta upanniyācam mikavum payaṉpaṭuvatākaliṉ, ataṉi iṅkē tamiḻil moḻipeyarttut tarukiṉṟām.
cūkkuma oḷiyākāyattil taṉṉit tōṟṟuvikka virumpum māṇavaṉ uḷḷatti orumukap paṭuttutalē ataṟkut tiṟavukōlāmeṉṟu aṟintukoḷvaṉ. nām namatuṭampi viṭṭuc ciṟitunēram pirintupōy, vēṟōr ākāyattil muḻutuṇarvōṭu tōṉṟa virumpuvōmāyiṉ, nām nammiyē naṉku vacappaṭuttik koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyami yātatākum.
cūkkuma oḷiyākāyattil yāṉ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya aṉupavam eṭṭoṉpatu varuṭaṅkaḷukku muṉ ciṭni eṉṉum ūril nikaḻntatu.
yāṉ eṉatuṭampiviṭṭup pōvatupōl uṇara lāṉēṉ; eṉṟālum, yāṉ ataṉōṭu oṭṭik koṇṭi ruppatu pōlavē avvuṇar viṟṟeṉpaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṟaku ōr imi koṭṭuvataṟkuḷ yāṉ iraṇṭiṭattil irukkak kaṇṭēṉ. oru nāṟkāliyiṉmēl uṭkārntirunta eṉconta ūṉuṭampiṉōṭu cērntāṟpōl yāṉ maṟṟoru nāṟkāliyiṉ mēl uṭkārntiruntēṉ. oru noṭippoḻutu yāṉ veruṭci aṭintēṉ; eppaṭiyō aḵtu eṉṉit tiṭukkiṭap paṇṇiyatu; ataṉpiṉṉar yāṉ eṉatuṭampiṉuḷ tirumpuvatu pōṟ ṟōṉṟiyatu.
ittakiya tōṟṟattil eṉakku viruppam uṇṭākavē itaṉi ārāyntu aṟitaṟkut talippaṭṭēṉ. uṇarvōṭu kūṭiya eṉatu aṭutta aṉupavam inta nakarattil yāṉ paravacappaṭṭa niliyiṟ piracaṅkam puriyuṅ kālattil tōṉṟuvatāyiṟṟu. yāṉ ākāyattil mitantu celvatāka uṇaralāṉēṉ; yāṉ neṭuntūram pirayāṇañ ceyvatupōṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu. oru maṇi nēram ōr upanniyācam ceytapiṟaku yāṉ tirumpavum uṇarvupeṟṟu viḻittu akkirācaṉari nōkki, yāṉ niyūsīlaṇṭil uḷḷa kriṭ carc eṉṉum iṭattiṟkuk pōyiruntēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiṉēṉ. ataṟku avar puṉcirippukkoṇṭu ‘aṅkē evarip pārttīrkaḷ?  eṉṟu kēṭṭār.
yāṉ oruvarip pārttēṉ; āṉāl avar mēṭi mēl illi. maṟṟu akkirācaṉarā yirukkak kaṇṭēṉeṉṟu viṭipakarntēṉ.
yāṉ colvatu putumiyākak kāṇappaṭiṉum, muṉ colliyatu nikaḻnta iraṇṭu vārattiṟkuḷ kriṭ carc eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu eṉakku iraṇṭu ceytikaḷ vantaṉa; muṟkūṟiya atē nāḷiṉ mālikkālattil teḷivuk kāṭci yuṭiya mūvar yāṉ aṅkē tēvālaya mēṭimēl niṟkak kaṇṭaṉarām.
accamayattil uḷḷapaṭiyē yāṉ eṉatuṭampi viṭṭup pōyirukka vēṇṭumeṉa uṇarntēṉ. cūkkuma carīrattil niṟkum āvēcam oṉṟu eṉatuṭampik kippaṟṟi ataṉ vāyilākat tāṉ pēcuvataṟku ataṉi upayōkap paṭuttu kiyil, yāṉ eṉatuṭampiṉ pakkattē muṉṉē pala taṭavi kaḷil niṟkak kaṇṭēṉ. āṉataṉāl, yāṉ kriṭ carc eṉṉum iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟu niṉivōṭu tirumpi vantatip paṟṟi viyappaṭiyavilli. āṉāl, atē camayattil yāṉ avarkaḷāl terintukoḷḷap paṭṭatupaṟṟi mikavuṅ kaḷi kūrntēṉ; eṉṉi aṅṅaṉam kaṇṭa perumāṭṭikaḷil iruvar maṉōtattuva nūlārāyccikku aminta eṉatu māṇavar kuḻāttiṟ cērntu tamatu maṉōcaktiyi mikutip paṭuttik koṇṭārkaḷākiyāl eṉatu makiḻcci piṉṉum mēṟpaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
iṉṉum itaṉi ārāyntu cellavē, uṭampi viṭṭu eḷitākavum, virumpiyapaṭiyum celluvataṟkuk kuṟippāṉa paḻakkam avaciyam vēṇṭumeṉpatu kaṇṭēṉ. orumuṟi, kriṭ carc eṉṉum iṭattil yāṉ paravacap paṭṭa niliyiṟ pēcikkoṇṭirunta poḻutu, eṉṉuṭampi viṭṭu uvelliṅṭaṉ eṉṉum ūrukkup pōy, niyūsīlaṇṭiṟkut tirumpi vantu koṇṭirunta eṉ maṉiviyiyum kuḻantiyiyum cūkkuma oḷiyākāyattiṟ cantikkakkūṭumā eṉṟu pārkka virumpiṉēṉ.
anta iravu paṭukkikkup pōṉavuṭaṉē, maṉavorumi yiṉālum mantira moḻiyiṉālum eṉatuṇarvic cūkkuma niliyiṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟu, oruvakiyāṉa aṟituyilil amarntēṉ; ataṉpiṉṉar, eṉatuṭampi viṭṭu uvelliṅ ṭaṉukkuc celvatākat teḷiya niṉivu kūrntēṉ. appāl yāṉ paṭakiṉuḷḷē ciṟṟaṟiyil iṟaṅki eṉ maṉiviyiyum makaviyuṅ kaṇṭēṉ; itu māttiram aṉṟu; akkāṭciyiṟ ciṟanta eṉ ciṟu piyaṉ taṉ tāyi nōkki, atō appā vantirukkiṟār eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ; eṉ maṉiviyum yāṉ taṉṉuṭaṉ meyyāka iruppatākavē uṟutiyāka uṇarntāḷ.
maṟunāṭkāliyil, iliṭṭilṭaṉ eṉṉum iṭat tiṟkup paṭaku vantavuṭaṉē ceṉṟu eṉmaṉiviyiṭattil iraṇṭoru coṟ colliyapiṟaku iṅkēyē iru, nāṉ kīḻē pōy nī irunta aṟi itutāṉ eṉṟu terintu koḷḷukiṟēṉā eṉṟu pār, eṉak kūṟiṉēṉ.  ataṉpiṟaku yāṉ nērē ōr aṟikkuc ceṉṟu, kaṇkāṇicciyip pārttu, ‘kirēk eṉpavar maṉivi irunta aṟi itutāṉā?  eṉṟu viṉāva, avaḷum āmeṉa viṭi pakarntāḷ.
itaṟku muṉṉāḷ iravil avarkaḷiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa nilimiyiyum, avarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam paṭuttiruntārkaḷeṉ patiyum, avarkaḷ irunta aṟiyiṉ iṭattiyum māṟāmal niṉivukūrntē ṉātalāl, maṟunāṭ kāliyil ataṉi uṭaṉē kuṟippiṭa vallavaṉāṉēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam itiṟ cittipeṟavē, iṉic camayam vāykkumpōtellām iṉṉum itaṉi ārāyntu aṟitaṟkut tīrmāṉittēṉ; cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ciṭni eṉṉum ivviṭattil eṉakku oru camayam vāyttatu; appaṭippaṭṭa camayam iṉi eṉakkuk kiṭittal aritu; allatu ataṉi yāṉ mikutiyāy virumpukiṟēṉeṉṟuñ collakkūṭavilli.
āṉmacaktiyi mikutippaṭuttuvataṟku oru ciṟu māṇavar kuḻāttiṉi vittup pōtittu naṭatti varukiyil māṇavaril oruvar eṉṉi nōkkit teṉṉāppirikkāvil uḷḷa eṉ makaṉippaṟṟi eppaṭiyāvatu nīṅkaḷ eṉakkuc ceyti koṇṭuvarak kūṭumāyiṉ - itu pōyar - caṇṭi nikaḻnta kālattil nērntatu - aḵtu eṉ maṉivikku āṉmatattuva ārāycciyil āvali uṇṭupaṇṇutaṟkuk kāraṇamā yirukkumeṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ, eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
ākiyāl, intac ceytiyit terintu koḷḷum poruṭṭu oru vicēṭa muyaṟci ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟu nāṅkaḷ ellārun tīrmāṉittōm. ovvoruvarum kikōttuk koṇṭu eṉṉōṭotta maṉaniliyiluḷḷavarkaḷāy eṉakku utavi ceyvā rāṉārkaḷ; ciṟitu nērattiṟkellām, yāṉ oru paravacappaṭṭa niliyi aṭintēṉ. appoḻutu eṉ uṭampip paṟṟiyirunta ōr āvēcamāṉatu aṅkirunta maṟṟavarkaḷi nōkki ivar iṟantavarippōṟ kiṭantālum, ivari nīṅkaḷ toṭappaṭātu. nāṅkaḷ neṭunēram iv viṭattiviṭṭup pōyiruppōm; appōtu ivvuṭampi yārun toṭutal ākātu eṉṟatu. ataṉmēl, avarkaḷ eṉṉi appoḻutu nāṟkāliyil vacatiyāka irukka vittārkaḷ; avvaḷavutāṉ atip paṟṟi yāṉ niṉivu kūrntatu.
ēṟakkuṟiya mukkālmaṇi nērattiṟkup piṟaku atē āvi maṟupaṭiyum eṉatuṭampip paṟṟikkoṇṭu aṅkiruntavari nōkki, um makaṉ teṉṉāppirikkāvil kāyappaṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟāṉ. ippōtu avaṉ umakkuk kaṭitam eḻutik koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ; iṉṉum iraṇṭu mūṉṟu vārattil avaṉiṭamiruntu umakkuc ceyti varum eṉṟu kūṟiyatu. iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntatip paṟṟi eṉakku yārum etuvun terivikkak kūṭāteṉṟu muṉṉamē kēṭṭuk koṇṭēṉ. avviravu yāṉ vīṭṭukkup pōṉapōtu, uyirppāṉa uṇmit tōṟṟattippōl, an nikaḻcci muḻutum eṉṉetirē kāṇappaṭalāyiṟṟu. kavarcci mikka āṭavar nālvar eṉṉic cūḻntiruntatum, avarkaḷ eṉṉiṭam ōr āvēcattik koṇṭuvantatum, pōr nikaḻuṅ kaḷattiṟku yāṉ avar kaḷōṭu kūṭicceṉṟatum, aṅku yāṉ aminta aṉupavattāl pōriṉ tikilāṉa nikaḻccikaḷi eṉṟum maṟavātapaṭi yāṉ uṇaralāṉatum mikavunteḷivāka niṉivukūrntēṉ. mikavum accan taruvatāṉa avvaṉupavattil āṭavarum peṇṭir cilarum appōtu tām tamatuṭampiviṭṭup pirintu pōyum, avarkaḷ ataṉi aṟiyāmal tām taṉatuṭampili ruppatākavē niṉintu oḷiyākāyattilum toṭarntu pōr nikaḻttutalik kaṇṭu, avarkaḷiṟ perumpālār tamatu tūlacarīratti viṭṭu vanta uṇmi ciṟitum terintilareṉa meyppaṭa aṟintēṉ. pōriṉālum pōriṉ koṭumika ḷiṉālum viḷiyum payaṉ iṉṉatu tāṉ eṉṟu muṉṉiliyilum ippōtu naṉku teḷiyap peṟṟēṉ. intak koṭumikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉ iṭiyil ōr aḻakiya tōṟṟattik kaṇṭu niṉivu kūrntēṉ; peṇkaḷ kūṭṭam oṉṟu, pōrāṭi alamarum iv vēḻi uyirkaḷukku avi tamatu nilavuṭampi viṭṭu vantataṉi aṟivuṟutti utava uḻippeṭuttalik kaṇṭēṉ; avarkaḷ aṇintirunta tūya veḷḷiya āṭikaḷi ṉālum avarkaḷic cūḻntirunta oḷivaṭṭattiṉālum avarkaḷ cūkkuma mēlulakaṅkaḷiliruntu vanta tēvatūtarkaḷē yeṉṟu yāṉ collamāṭṭuvēṉ.
pōr ceyyac cellum paṭivīrarkaḷi makiḻvuṟutti oruvari oruvar kolipuriyumpaṭi ēvum maṉitaṉ, cūkkuma oḷiyākāyattil yāṉ kaṇṭa aṉupavattit tāṉum kāṇpa ṉāyiṉ, avaṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kūvutali viṭuttu iṇakkam uṟuvikkum capikaḷiṟ cērntuḻikka muntuvaṉ.
āṉmatattuvap poruḷkaḷi iṅṅaṉam yāṉ toṭarntu ārāyntu celkiyil orumuṟi yāṉ ciṭni eṉṉum ivvūril oru putumiyāṉa aṉupavattik kaṇṭēṉ. mātar oruvar mikavum iṭar uṟṟavarāyp pēykuṭi koṇṭa vīṭṭippaṟṟi eṉakku ētēṉun teriyumāveṉṟu eṉṉik kēṭṭār. ataṟku yāṉ ētum teriyāteṉṟum, āṉāl, attakiya toṉṟi neṭuṅkālamākat tēṭik koṇṭiruk kiṉṟē ṉeṉṟum, ataṉi yāṉ pārkka vēṇṭumeṉṟum moḻintēṉ. ataṉmēl avar tam vīṭṭil oru ciṟu mantirak kūṭṭamiṭṭup pārkkumpaṭi eṉṉi vēṇṭik koṇṭaṉar. nilattiṟ kāṇappṭāta viyappāṉa kūkkural olikaḷ tōṉṟu vataṉālum, katavukaḷ tiṭīreṉṟu tiṟappataṉālum, mēcikaḷ taṅ kālkaḷ mēl viṟuviṟeṉṟu cuḻalvataṉālum, cāḷarak katavukaḷ caṭacaṭa veṉṟu ōciyiṭuvataṉālum tām mikavun tuṉpuṟuttap paṭuvatākak kūṟit tamatu vīṭu āvēca vaṭivaṅkaḷāṟ cūḻappaṭṭi ruppatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟa teṉavum moḻintār.
yāṉ iṉṉuñ cilarōṭu am mātarār vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu, aṅkē mantirac ciṟu kūṭṭamoṉṟu kūṭiṉōm. aṅṅaṉam nāṅkaḷ uṭkārnta māttirattil ētō oru vakiyāṉa tōṟṟam eṉ talimēl vara uṇarntēṉ. yāṉ terintu koṇṭapaṭi cūkkuma āvēcam oṉṟu uṭaṉē eṉtaliyiṉ piṉpuṟatti nerukkip piṭikkat toṭaṅkiyatu. eṉ taliyāṉatu eṉ tōḷkaḷiliruntu muṟukki yeṭukkap paṭuvatupōl uṇarntēṉ. eṉṉuṭiya aṉupavaṅkaḷil immuṟi tāṉ, yāṉ eṉvali koṇṭa maṭṭum taṭuttuṅ kūṭāmal yāṉ irunta nāṟkāliyi ṉiṉṟuṅ kīḻē iḻuttup pōṭappaṭṭēṉ. eḻuntu maṟupaṭiyum nāṟkāliyil uṭkārnta piṟaku. eṉ taliyiṉ ucci vaḻiyē yāṉ veḷip puṟappaṭṭup pōvatupōl uṇarntēṉ; piṉṉuñ ciṟitu nērattiṟ kellām avacamāṉēṉ. ataṉpiṉ eṉṉuṭampi āvēcam oṉṟu paṟṟikkoṇṭu tāṉ oru kappaṟkāraṉāka iruntaṉaṉ eṉṟum, kaḻuttu muṟipaṭṭu avac cākkāṭu eytiṉaṉ eṉṟum, kāṭlāṇṭu tēcattiluḷḷa taṉ makaḷ oruttikkuc ceyti aṟivikka virumpukiṉṟaṉaṉ eṉṟum mantirakkūṭṭattil uḷḷārkkuk kūṟiṉatu. avaṉ orukālattil iv vīṭṭil taṅkiyiruntaṉaṉām; ataṉāl, avaṉ iṅkē tirumpivantu iṅkuḷḷavar taṉ putalvikkuc ceyti aṟivikkumpaṭi ceyya niṉittaṉaṉām. ataṉmēl am mantirakkūṭṭatti naṭattun talimakaṉ ām, umatu iruppi iṅkuḷḷavarkkut terivikka vaḻakkamallāta im muṟiyi nīr kiyāṇṭatēṉ? nīr ivarkaḷi accuṟuttiyatu māttiram aṉṟu; avarkaḷ iv vīṭṭiyē muṟṟilum viṭṭuppōka virumpumāṟu ceytu viṭṭīrē. uṇmiyil ivviṭam pēy piṭittateṉap peyar peṟṟuviṭṭatu; iṅkuḷḷa kuṭikaḷ itaṉi viṭṭup pōy viṭuvārkaḷāyiṉ itu veṟumi yākavē irukkumpaṭi nērakkūṭumē eṉṟār. ataṟku avvāvi, yāṉ ivarkaḷukkuc ceytiyaṟivikkak kūṭiyavaṉāṉatu paṟṟi makiḻcci aṭikiṉ ṟēṉ; iv vīṭṭukkāra ammi āvēcaṅkaḷōṭu iyivu paṭutaṟkuriya cūkkuma iyalpu vāyntavareṉṟum, avaruṭiya nāṟkālikaḷiyum mēcikaḷiyum kilukilukkap paṇṇiṉa cūkkuma pirāṇa caktiyiṉi av vammiyiṉi ṭattiliruntē yāṉ iḻuttuk koṇṭēṉeṉṟum nīr av vammikkut terivittal vēṇṭum, eṉṟatu. āvēca vaṭivil niṉṟa avaṉ taṉ peyariyum taṉ makaḷiṉ vilācattiyum terivittāṉ. itu naṭanta mūṉṟu nāṉku nāṭkaḷukkup piṟaku, eṅkaḷ mantirak kūṭṭattilirunta oruvar eṉṉiṭam vantu, muṟceṉṟa iravil namakkut terivikkappaṭṭatu uṇmiyeṉak kaṇṭupiṭittēṉ. ōr ammiyōṭu yāṉ pēca nēriṭṭatil, anta ammi āvivaṭiviṟ ṟōṉṟiya av vāṭavaṉit tāṉ aṟivaḷ eṉṟum, avaṉ iv vīṭṭil taṅkiyiruntatu uṇmiyē yeṉṟum urittāḷ, eṉak kūṟiṉar. cila mātaṅkaḷ kaḻinta piṟaku, avvāvēca vaṭivil niṉṟavaṉ makaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭitam vantatu; atil avaḷ taṉ ṟanti eṅkiruntāreṉpatum, avar uyirōṭiruntaṉarō iṟantupōyiṉarō eṉpatum tāṉ aṟintilaḷ eṉat terivittāḷ; ēṉeṉṟāl, pala āṇṭu kaḷāka avaṉip paṟṟi avaḷ kēḷvippaṭavē illi. muṟceṉṟa nikaḻcciyil mikavum vinōtamāṉatu itu: pēypiṭitta vīṭu eṉpatippaṟṟi av vīṭṭukkāraṉ, kuṭittaṉakkārar vīṭṭiviṭṭup pōkāmip poruṭṭu vāṭakiyik kuṟittiruntāṉ. taṉṉi vantu kāṇum āvi taṉṉi viṭṭu nīṅkiṉataṉāl uvakiyuṟṟa am mātu, pēykkūccal niṉṟupōṉa vakiyi vīṭṭukkāraṉukku eṭuttuc collavē, avaṉ vīṭṭu vāṭakiyi uṭaṉē uyartti viṭṭāṉ!
inta aṉupavamāṉatu, pēypiṭitta vīṭukaḷ eṉpaṉa maṉavaliyillāta āṭavarkaḷum āṇṭiṉ mutirnta kiḻavikaḷum koṇṭa vīṇ eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷ alla veṉpatum, avikaḷ uṇmiyē yākumeṉpatum, taṅkaḷ vīṭukaḷil kūkkuraloḷikaḷāl alikkappaṭupavarkaḷ āṉmatattuva iyalpil tiṟami vāynta oruvaruṭiya tuṇiyip peṟamāṭṭuvārkaḷāyiṉ āviyuruvil niṟkum evarō oruvar tammuṭaṉ ētō aṟivikka nāṭukiṟāreṉṟu terintu koḷvareṉpatum, nuṇṇuṭampil ulavum āviyāṉatu taṉatu iruppi aṟivikka im muṟiyik kikkoḷkiṉṟa teṉpatum eṉakkuc ciṟitēṉum aiyam illiyāmpaṭi nāṭṭiyatu. tamakku mikavum iṉṟiyami yātatāṉa ētō oru ceytiyit terivikka virumpum āṭavarum peṇṭirumē avarkaḷ eṉṟu nīṅkaḷ uṇmip paṭuttik koḷḷuṅkāl avvāviyiṉ varukiyil iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa tīmi ētum illi eṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkum. tām ip paruvuṭampi viṭṭup pōyiṉum, iv vuṭampōṭu kūṭiyirukkum ēṉiyōrkku ētō aṟivikka vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppam iṉṉum av vāvi yuruvil niṟpavarkaḷpāṟ kuṭikoṇṭirukkiṉṟatu.
māṇavakaṉukku iṉpam payakkum āṉmatattuvak kāṭcikaḷ iṉṉum pala irukkiṉṟaṉa. māṉata ākāyattip paṟṟi eṉakku mutaṉ mutal uṇṭāṉa aṉupavaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟil oṉṟu kumpalāṉa oru nāṭaka cāliyil nikaḻalāyiṟṟu. naṇparkaḷ cilarōṭu yāṉ melpōrṇ eṉṉum iṭattil naṭipeṟṟa naṭaṉaviruntiṟkuc ceṉṟiruntēṉ. appōtu caṭutiyilē eṉṉetiriṟ ṟōṉṟiya oru māṉatak kāṭciyi uṇaralāṉēṉ. eṅkaḷ conta vīṭu eṉakku nēr etirilē niṟpatāka uṇarntēṉ. yāṉ ataṉi mikavun teḷivākap pārkkak kūṭiyatā yiruntatu; piṉṉum ataṉi yāṉ uṟuttup pārttapōtu ataṉ cuvariṉ ūṭē eṉ aṉṉiyiṉ aṟiyi nērē kaṇṭēṉ; piṉ ataṉuḷḷē eṉ aṉṉiyār iṟantupōṉāṟpōṟ paṭuttuk kiṭakkak kaṇṭēṉ. ōr arimaṇi nēram variyil it tōṟṟatti eṉṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟap pārttēṉ; āyiṉum muṭivil ataṉi neṭunēran tāṅkakkūṭātavaṉ āṉēṉ; ēṉeṉil aḵtu avvaḷavu uṇmiyāyt tōṉṟiyatu. yāṉ anta naṭaṉa viruntiṟku aḻittuc ceṉṟa naṅkikku ittōṟṟattik kuṟittuc coṉṉapōtu, avvammi iḵtu appaṭi yiruntāl, yāṉum ummuṭaṉ varuvatu nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. uṭaṉē nāṅkaḷ vīṭṭukku virintu ceṉṟōm; ceṉṟatum yāṉ mettimēṟ pāyntēṟi, eṉ aṉṉiyār aṟikkuṭceṉṟu, avar nilattiṉ mēṟ paṭuttukkiṭakkak kaṇṭēṉ. aṉṉiyār mūccup piṭippu nōyāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭuk kiṭṭattaṭṭa iṟantavar pōliruntār. yāṉ uṭaṉē oru vittiyari aḻittuvara, avar ‘ummuṭiya tāyār nōykoṇṭiruppatāka nīr eṅṅaṉam aṟintīr?   eṉṟu eṉṉi viṉaviṉār. naṭaṉa cāliyil iruntapōtu ic ceyti mikavun tiṇṇamākat tōṉṟiyataṉāl vīṭṭukkup pōy aṅku ētēṉum uṇṭā eṉṟu pārkkat tīrmāṉittē ṉeṉṟum avarukku eṭutturittēṉ. ataṉmēl avar yāṉ nallanērattil vantēṉeṉṟu maṟumoḻi pukaṉṟār: yāṉ aṅṅaṉañ ceytatupōla uṭaṉē tirumpi vantilēṉāyiṉ, eṉ aṉṉiyār mūccu aṭipaṭṭup pōyiruppa reṉpatu tiṇṇamēyām.
nām namatu āṉmacaktiyi veḷippaṭuttum pōtu intap paruvuṭampi viṭṭuppōvatu eḷitilē ki kūṭuvatākum; mutaṉ mutal avvuṇarcci putumiyuḷḷatāyt tōṉṟum; nīṅkaḷ uṅkaḷ uṭampiviṭṭut taṉittu niṟpatu pōlavum, uṅkaḷiyum uṅkaḷ paruvuṭampiyum iyippataṟku melliya oru pirāṇac caraṭu māttiram uḷḷatu pōlavum uṇarukiyil atu ciṟitu putumiyuḷḷa tākavē tāṉ irukkum; āṉāl, aṭuttaṭuttup paḻakiya piṟaku cūkkuma oḷiyākāyatti uṅkaḷukkuc conta iṭamākavē niṉikka nērum; av vuṇarvākāyattiṟ cuṟṟilumuḷḷa poruḷkaḷōṭu taṉṉuṇar vuṭaṉ poruntap peṟṟataṟku makiḻcci aṭivatu māttira maṉṟu, aṅkē nīṅkaḷ ñāṉattiyum aṭikiṟīrkaḷ. uṟaṅkuṅ kālaṅkaḷil tammi āṉmacakti uṭiyavarkaḷeṉṟu terintu koṇṭōr māttiramaṉṟu, maṟṟum palarum tamatuṭampi viṭṭuc celkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, nām kāṇum kiḷarcciyuḷḷa kaṉavukaḷ palavum veḷip pārvikku nammuṭampu tūkkatti liruppatupōṟ ṟōṉṟa nām nammuṭampukaḷi viṭṭup puṟañceṉṟu aṉupavitta aṉupavaṅkaḷiṉ niṉivukaḷē eṉṟum nāṉ uṟutippaṭuttik koṇṭirukkiṉṟēṉ. iṅṅaṉam yāṉ kaṇṭa mika ariya aṉupavaṅkaḷil piṉvaruvatu oṉṟu:
ēṟakkuṟiya āṟu mātaṅkaḷukkumuṉ yāṉ niyūsīlaṇṭu eṉṉum ūril piracaṅkam ceytu koṇṭirunta poḻutu āvivaṭivil niṟkum māntar palarōṭu poruntap peṟuvē ṉāyiṉēṉ. teḷivāṉa uḷḷa nilimi yuṭiyēṉāy, in nilavulakatti aṭuttuḷḷa maṇṭalattik kaṭantu iṉṉum uyarnta ulakattiṟkuc cellavēṇṭu meṉṉum kaṭiciyāṉa niṉivōṭu yāṉ uṟaṅkac ceṉṟēṉ. yāṉ eṉatuṭampi viṭṭup puṟappaṭṭatum eṉatuṇarvu turitappaṭutali uṇarntēṉ; attatikayatāṉa cūkkuma āṉma uṇarcciyi itaṟkumuṉ yāṉ eṉṟum kaṇṭaṟiyēṉ. uyarntatōr ulakattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa ōr aṉupavattip peṟap pōkiṉṟēṉ eṉpatu eṉakku uṟutiyāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu; piṉ collap paṭuvatu uṇmiyāka nikaḻntatākum.
āviyiruviluḷḷa makkaḷ palarik kaṇṭēṉ; eṉṉic cuṟṟi oru vaṭṭamāy avarkaḷ uṭkārntārkaḷ; avvākāyattiṉ ūṭē nāṅkaḷ mitantu celvatupōṟ ṟōṉṟiyatu; aṅṅaṉam acintu celvatit teḷiya uṇarntēṉ; aṅkuḷḷavaṟṟiṉ nilimi mikavun tūyatāyum teyvattaṉmi yuṭiyatāyum iruntatu; eṉakkup putiyatōr aṟivum āṟṟalum varuvatākavum, mikavum teḷivāṉa pārvi koṭukkap paṭuvatākavum kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṟaku uṟṟuk kēṭpatu pōl niṉṟukoṇṭirunta ōr āyirampēri naṉṟāyp pārttēṉ; āṉāl, ciṟitu nēram vēṟoruvariyum yāṉ kāṇakkūṭavilli. eṉatu pārvi varavarap peritāṉa vuṭaṉ, oru pīṭattiṉ mēl mikavum vayatu mutirnta aḻakiya āṇmakaṉ oruvar niṟkavum, kiḷaroḷivaṭṭam oṉṟu avaratu taliyic cūḻntirukkavum pārttēṉ. avar veṇmiyāṉa nīṇṭa niliyaṅki oṉṟu uṭuttiyiruntār; avar aṅkē kuḻumiyirunta māntari nōkkip pēcutalit teḷivāyk kēṭṭēṉ. aṅkuḷḷavarkaḷōṭu yāṉ mikutiyāyp paḻakiyapiṟaku, inta āciriyar ivvāṇ peṇ makkaḷukku āṉma tattuva uṇarcciyi eḻuppa muyaṉṟu koṇṭiruntaṉa reṉavum, iṉṉum mēlāṉa ulakattiṟkuc cellum poruṭṭu avarkaḷ muyalum vaṇṇam avarkaḷi ūkkik koṇṭiruntaṉa reṉavum teriyap peṟṟēṉ.
intap paruvuṭampil irukkiyil mikavuñ ciṟappākap pēcakkūṭiya palar pēccukkaḷik kēṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ; āṉāl, ivar pēcumpōtō ivaruṭiya coṟkaḷ teyvacakti niṟintuvarak kaṇṭēṉ; tamatu āṉma caktiyiṉiyum tammuḷ niṟinta teyva caktiyiṉiyum vaḷarac ceytavarāyum, yāṉ ituvariyil etirppaṭāta mika uyarnta maṇṭalattil iruppavarāyum uḷḷa oruvarōṭu iṇaṅki avaruṭaṉ kūṭiyiruppatupōl eṉ uḷḷam eṅkum uṇaralāṉēṉ. avaric cuṟṟilum aḻakiya ōr oḷiyiṉ katirkaḷ virintu erivatupōl tōṉṟiṉa; avaratu taliyic cūḻap pala niṟaṅkaḷik kaṇṭēṉ. iṉṉum eṉatu pārvi teḷi vaṭiyavē, kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa veḷḷiya āṭikaḷ uṭuttirunta patiṉintu allatu irupatu āṭavarum peṇṭirum avaruṭaṉ iruttalit terintukoṇṭēṉ; avarkaḷ tamatu āṉma caktiyiṉālum ataṉ viḷakkattiṉālum ulakapālaṉañ ceyyum oru kūṭṭattavareṉṟum, uyarnta maṇṭalaṅ kaḷiliruntu kīḻuḷḷa maṇṭalaṅkaḷukkuc ceyti koṇṭu varupavar kaḷeṉṟum eṉakkup pulappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉṉar eṉṉiṭam vantu eṉakku āvivaṭivil niṉṟē ceyti aṟivitta tēvatūtaṉ oruvaṉāl, ac ciṟanta periyavar ulakapālaṉak kūṭṭam oṉṟōṭu mēlulakaṅkaḷ oṉṟiliruntu vantu kīḻē mutal ulakattil irukkum makkaḷukku āṉmacaktiyi nirapputaṟ poruṭṭum, mēluḷḷa māṉata ulakattiṟku avarkaḷ tāmē cellumpaṭi taṉmuyaṟci ceyya vēṇṭum avaciyatti avarkaḷukku vaṟpuṟuttik kāṭṭutaṟ poruṭṭum pēcik koṇṭiruntaṉareṉa aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭēṉ.
ivvaṉupavattiṉ piṉṉar eṉatu tūlatēkattiṟkut tirumpiyavuṭaṉē yāṉ oru teyvacaktiyāl nirampap peṟṟēṉ; piṉ nāṭkaḷilellām yāṉ vēṟu maṉitaṉā yiṉēṉ eṉṟuṇarntēṉ. itteyvacakti attaṉi valiyuṭiyatāyt tōṉṟiṉamiyāl āṉma tattuva ulakaṅkaḷ oṉṟil yāṉ aṭinta iv vaṉupavamāṉatu eṉakku aḷaviṟanta naṉmiyic ceytateṉa uṇarntēṉ. mēlaṟi vuṭiya tēvarkaḷōṭu nām oṉṟu cērkkappaṭṭakkāl atu nammit tappāmal valimimikka āṭavarum peṇṭirum āka ākkukiṉṟatu; ēṉeṉṟāl, āṉmatattuva ulakaṅkaḷil nīṅkaḷ iyaṅka vallavarāku muṉṉamē, uṅkaḷic cūḻntirukkum āṟṟal mikka cūkkuma carīravācikaḷukkuk kīḻ aṭaṅkiṉa niliyil nīṅkaḷ uṅkaḷi maṉavorumiyiṉāl vittuk koḷḷun tiṟami uṭiyavarkaḷ ākavēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam āṉapiṉ, nīṅkaḷ uṅkaḷ uṭampiviṭṭup puṟappaṭumpōtu, aṟintō aṟiyāmalō ivvulaka vāḻvil etaṉip peṟum nōkkattōṭu muyaṉṟu vantīrkaḷō ataṟku icinta māṉata ulakattiṟkuc celvīrkaḷ. āṉmatattuva vulakaṅkaḷilēṉum oḷiyākāyattilēṉum tamakku aṟivu viḷaṅkutalic ceytupārkka virumpuvōr yāṉ colvaṉavaṟṟip piṉpaṟṟi naṭakkavēṇṭum. itaṉic ceytu pārppataṟkeṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa mālik kālattiṟkumuṉ pakalmuḻutum uṇavu koḷḷāmal irukkavēṇṭum; nila ulakattip paṟṟiya ellā niṉivukaḷiyum aṟavē oḻittuviṭal vēṇṭum; ellā māntarkaḷiṭattum irakkamum aṉpum mikka niṉivōṭu amitiyākap paṭuttukkoḷḷal vēṇṭum; ataṉ piṟaku uṅkaḷ uṭampiviṭṭup pōyc cūkkuma ulakaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭa aṉupavaṅkaḷit tirumpavum innilavulakat tiṟku varumpōtu niṉivukūrtal vēṇṭumeṉṉum vēṭki yōṭu kūṭiya niṉivi uṅkaḷ uḷḷattil eḻuppik koṇṭē aciviṉṟi amitiyāka uṟaṅkac celluka; iṅṅaṉañ ceytāl nīṅkaḷ paṭṭa varuttattiṟkellām īṭukaṭṭuvataṟku mēlāṉa eṇṇaṅkaḷip perumpālum eytappeṟuvīrkaḷ eṉṉum uṟuti yuṭiyēṉ.
āṉāl, itaṟkum itupōṉṟa maṟṟa ellā viṣayaṅkaḷukkum vēṟoru tītāṉa pakkamum irukkiṉṟatu. nām oḷiyākāyattil veḷippaṭumpōtu nallavarkaḷ kūṭṭat tiyē nām ennēramum poruntumpaṭi vāyppatilli. orucamayattil yāṉ aṭinta aṉupavamāṉatu palanāḷkāṟum eṉṉit tuyaramāṉa nilimiyil vittatu.
yāṉ eṉatuṭampiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭēṉ; in nilavulakattil eṉakkuḷḷa nēcarippōlavē oḷiyā kāyattilum eṉakku naṇparāyuḷḷa tēvatūtaṉ oruvarōṭum yāṉ aṟivoḷi peṟāta uyirkaḷ uḷḷa iṭattiṟkup pōka niṉittēṉ. aṅkuḷḷa āṭavar māṉata ākāyattiṉ iruḷil taṭavikkoṇṭu pōtalip pārttēṉ. avarkaḷiṟ palar tām nilavuṭampiviṭṭu vantatākavē terintu koḷḷa villi; ataṉit terintu koṇṭavarkaḷuṅ kūṭa aṟivu vaḷarap peṟātavarkaḷāyum vaḷarāta niliyiluḷḷavarkaḷāyum iruntaṉar; āvivaṭivil niṉṟa ivvuyirkaḷ palavaṟṟic cūḻa uḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ nilimikaḷip pārttu, nuṇṇu ṭampil niṟkum oru keṭṭamaṉitaṉ, ōr aṟivillātavaṉ, nilavuṭampil niṟkum oruvaṉik kāṭṭilum mikavum keṭṭavaṉāvaṉ eṉṉum uṇmi muṟṟum uṇarappeṟṟēṉ; ivviṣayattik kuṟittu pēcum ivviṭattē maraṇa taṇṭaṉiyiṉ koṭumiyippaṟṟi vaṟpuṟuttic colla virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
yāṉ āṉmatattuva uṇarcci yuṭiyavaṉā yiruttalāl, namataraciyal vitippaṭi koli puriyappaṭṭut tamatuṭampip pirintu pōṉa āṭavar palari yāṉ cantittatiliruntu, avarkaḷ tamatu uṭampiviṭṭup pōṉatum toliviluḷḷa ētēṉu mōr aḻakiya ulakattiṟkuc cellāmal inta nila ulakattiyē cuṟṟikkoṇṭu tuṉpattiṉālum piḻipaṭa naṭantataṉāl uṇṭāṉa maṉak kalakkattiṉālum paṟṟappaṭṭu māṉata iruḷil tām irukkak kaṇṭaṉareṉpati yāṉ terintukoṇṭēṉ; avarkaḷiṟ palar paḻivāṅkum eṇṇam uṭiyōrāy melliya iyaṟki yuṭiyavarip piṭittuk koṇṭu avarkaḷip picaki naṭakkumpaṭi ēvukiṉṟaṉar.
iḵtuṇmiyeṉṟu nāṉ aṟivēṉ; āṉataṉāl yāṉ piṉvāṅkāmaṟ colvatu itu: uṭampōṭirukkum maṉavali yillāta āṭavariyum peṇṭiriyum tamakku iriyākkik koṇṭu avarkaḷip paḻicceyalukkut tūṇṭumpaṭi, koliyāḷikaḷik koṉṟu cūkkuma ulakattiṟku aṉupputalip pārkkilum avarkaḷ inta nilavulakattilēyē tārāḷamāyt tiriyumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭutal nallatu.
aticayamāṉa inta ulakam namakku aṇṭiyil iruppati meyyāka uṇarntu, aṟivu viriyāta ettaṉi āṭavarum peṇṭirum namakku evvaḷavu arukiliruk kiṟārkaḷeṉpati aṟiyuṅkāl, nām urappāṉa tūya niṉivukaḷi niṉikka vēṇṭuvatu ettuṇi avaciyam eṉpati nām pārāṭṭip pēcuvōm. vacīkara muṟiyi uṟṟu nōkkiṉāl nām nammiṭam varuvikkum uyirkaḷ nam eṇṇaṅkaḷōṭu otta iyalpuṭiyaṉa vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa; ākiyāl, oḷiyum utaviyum uyarcciyum vāynta niṉivukaḷi niṉippatilēyē nām karuttāyiruntu, nammiṭam nallavikaḷē aṇukumpaṭi iḻukka vēṇṭum.
mikac ciṟantatiyē peṟa vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppatti uruvēṟṟi, uṅkaḷukku utavi ceyyakkūṭiya variyē uṅkaḷiṭam iḻukka nīṅkaḷ muyalumvariyil āṉmatattuva vāḻkkikkuriya uyarnta maṇṭalaṅkaḷilēṉum oḷiyākāyatti lēṉum ciṟitum apāyamilli; ēṉeṉil, aṅkē uṅkaḷ valiyuḷḷa māṉata ākāyamē uṅkaḷic cūḻntiruttalāl, atuvē uṅkaḷi eppōtum pātukākkum.
yāṉ niyūsīlaṇṭil vittu naṭattiya mantira vakuppiṉ kaṇ orukāl eṉakkoru putiya aṉupavam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. appiyācam muṭintapiṟaku av vakuppu māṇavakaril oruvar tamakku aṉṟiravu viyappāṉa ōraṉupavam uṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟu coṉṉār. ataṉmēl avar ceṉṟirunta iṭam etuveṉṟu viṉaviṉēṉ; ataṟkavar tām kaṭikāra kōpurattic cuṟṟik koṇṭiruntatākak kūṟiṉar. avar āṉmacakti peritum mikap peṟṟavar eṉpataṉi yāṉ aṟivēṉ; āṉāl, avar itaṟku muṉ tām uṭampiviṭṭuc ceṉṟatākac coṉṉatē yilli. aṅkē evariyēṉum pārttatuṇṭā veṉa avarik kēṭṭēṉ; tām iruvarik kaṇṭatākavum avarkaḷ peyar iṉṉavi eṉṟum avar maṟumoḻi pukaṉṟār. piṉṉar yāṉ vicāraṇi ceytatil avviruvarum picakillāmal vivarittuc collappaṭṭaṉa reṉṟum, avar cuṭṭic colliya nērattil avviruvarum avviṭattik kaṭantupōyk koṇṭiruntaṉa reṉṉum kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ. ākavē, cūkkuma oḷiyā kāyattiṉkaṇ aṟivu nikaḻap peṟutal kuṟippiṭṭa cilarkku māttiramē yuḷḷateṉṟu kūṟuvātākā teṉavum, ēṟakkuṟiya ellāvuyirkaḷumē cila kālattil tam āṉmatattuva aṉupavaṅkaḷit tammuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūrntu varuvaṉavākumeṉavum aṟiyap peṟukiṉṟōm. iḵtu uṅkaḷukku āṉma ñāṉattiṟku utaviyāvatu māttiram aṉṟu; intap paruvuṭampi viṭṭuc cellumāṟu allatu enta maṇṭilattiṟkēṉum uṅkaḷ maṉañcellumpaṭi ataṉi uṇrvōṭu celuttumāṟu uḷḷatti oruvaḻi vikkap paḻakiṉāl inta nilavulakattilēyuṅkūṭa uṅkaḷukku nēril illāmaṟ pallāyiramil tūrattiṟku appāl uṭampōṭu kūṭiyō allatu ataṉōṭu kūṭāmalō irukkum uṅkaḷ naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ kalantupēcutaṟkun tiṟami vāykkap peṟuvīrkaḷ.
eṉṟu itukāṟum pōnta mākluṭ kirēk eṉpavariṉ uṇmiyaṉupava upanniyācattāl oḷiyā kāyattiṉ iyalpukaḷum āṇṭu nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷum ivvuṭampi liruntapaṭiyē atiṟ cellumāṟum piṟavum iṉitu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka. iṉṉum akakkaṇ tiṟakkap paṭṭu avvoḷi yākāyattiluḷḷa ulakaṅkaḷik kaṇṭavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu avaṟṟi varuṇittuc colli yirukkiṟārkaḷ.

16. oḷiyākāya ulakaṅkaḷ
innilavulakattup parupporuḷkaḷ uṇmiyāka iruttal pōlavē oḷiyulakaṅkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷum aḻiyā viḻuppam uṭiyaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. av vuṇmi yulakaṅkaḷilētāṉ uṅkaḷukku eṉṟumuḷḷa kuṟivukaḷ niṟivu ceyyappaṭum. tamatu naṟumaṇatti eṅkum nirampac ceyyum koḻuviya malarkaḷum ceṭikaḷum cūḻnta aḻakiya māḷikikaḷāl alaṅkarikkap paṭṭuḷḷa iṉiya nilat tōṟṟaṅkaḷ kaṇṇik kavarat takkaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. poṉ mayamāṉa ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷiyil kuvaṭukaḷum pakkaṅkaḷum muḻukappeṟṟa malikaḷum aṅkuḷḷaṉa. veṇmiyāṉa aruvikaḷ ōṭappeṟṟaṉavum vaḷañciṟanta pacumpul nilaṅkaḷāl uṭukkappaṭṭaṉavumākiya paḷḷattākkukaḷum aṅkuḷḷaṉa. tamakku mēl uḷḷa teḷinta vāṉattit tammiṭattē kāṭṭum mēṟparappum, puṟkaḷum malarkaḷum niṟinta kari ōraṅkaḷum elliyaṟṟa aṉpiṉ uṇarcciyi iṉitāka muṇumuṇut tōṭum teḷipaḷiṅkaṉṉa taṇṇīrum poruntap peṟṟa yāṟukaḷum aṅku viḷaṅkā niṟkum.,  parimaḷamāṉa iḷaṅkāṟṟuk kaṉṉattiṉmēl vīcuvatum uḷḷiḻukkun tūyakāṟṟum āṉmāvip paravacappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. mēlāṉa nīlaniṟam vāynta vāṉamum aṅku nilavum: kīḻulakattuḷḷa nallavarkkuk kaṉinta ceḻuṅkatiric coriyum ñāyiṟṟiṉ pala tiṟappaṭṭa vaṇṇaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya paḷappaḷappāṉa katirkaḷ tōyntu kiḷaroḷimikku viḷaṅkum mukiṟ kuḻāṅkaḷ kaṟṟikkaṟṟiyāka avvāṉilē mitantu cellā niṟkum. mikavuñ ceḻumiyāṉa tōkikaḷuṭiya paṟavikaḷ amiḻtam pōṉṟa iṉiya pāṭṭukkaḷi miḻaṟṟik koṇṭirukkum. nammic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa eḻil nalaṅkaḷip parukuvārpōṉṟu kaḷikkum paruvam mutirnta āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālarōṭum kūṭikkoṇṭu kaḷḷam aṟiyā makiḻcci tuḷumpum iṉiya maḻalic coṟkaḷi moḻintu aḻakiya iḷaṅkuḻavikaḷ viḷiyāṭṭayarum; aṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ viḷiyāṭuṅkāl, iṭiyiṭiyē tamatu pēriṉpattiṉ poruṭṭāka ittaṉi ariya poruḷkaḷiyum tamakku amittuk koṭutta aṉpumikka tantikku naṉṟiyum vaṇakkumuñ celuttuvōrāy iruppar. ammēlāṉa nilam ellārum kaṭiciyākac cellum iṭamāyirukkiṟatu. cilar ataṉit tām ceyta nalviṉikaḷāl eytukiṉṟaṉar; vēṟucilar mikunta tuṉpattiyum iṭariyum aṉupavittu ataṉpiṉṉar ataṉi aṭikiṉṟaṉar; maṟṟuñ cilar kaḻivirakkattiṉāl tūymi ceyyappaṭṭu ataṉip peṟukiṉṟaṉar; aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum ellārkkum atu ciṟappum nalamum niṟinta uṇmiyāṉa pukaliṭamē yākum.
iṉi amerikkā tēyattu aikkiya mākāṇattiṉ kaṇ niyāyātipatiyāyirunta eṭmaṇṭ eṉṉum periyōr tām cūkkuma carīrattil niṉṟavāṟē mantira cakti mikka oruvarmēl āvēcittu ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarattil oru peruṅ kūṭṭat tārkkup purinta upanniyācam cūkkuma oḷiyulaka vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpukaḷi naṉku pulappaṭuttutaliṉ ataṉiyum iṅkē moḻipeyarttu eḻutukiṉṟām.
17. cūkkuma oḷiyulaka vāḻkki
vēṟoruvar uṭampi iravalākap paṟṟik koṇṭu putumātiriyāka iṉṟu nāṉ iruvaki yulakaṅkaḷiṉ vāḻtturikaḷōṭum vantirukkiṟēṉ. eṉatu uyir vāḻkkiyil yāṉ aṭinta peruveṟṟiyiṉ - maraṇattic ceyittataṉ - varalāṟṟiṉi uṅkaḷukku eṭuttuc colla vallavaṉā yirukkiṉṟēṉ.
eṉatu uṭampu taṉiyē viṭṭu viṭappaṭṭatu; āṉāl eṉatuyirō uṅkaḷ naṭuvil nāṉ irunta kālattu iruntatu pōlavē tirumpavum putitāṉa cuṟucuṟuppōṭum ellāc caktikaḷōṭum muḻumiyākavē irukkiṉṟatu. ippōtu yāṉ putitākak kaṇṭa inniliyil uṇṭāṉa maṉakkiḷarcciyi ṉālum ūkkattiṉālumē niṟintirukkiṉṟēṉ. iṟappu eṉṟu collappaṭum nilimāṟutaliṉ vaḻiyāka yāṉ oruciṟitum tuṉpam uḻavāmalē nilavāḻkkiyik kaṭantuvantēṉ. eṉṉuṭampu melintupōṉatu uṇmitāṉ; ceṉṟa cila āṇṭukaḷāka valiviṉmiyiṉālum taḷarcciyiṉālum yāṉ varuntik koṇṭiruntatum uṇmitāṉ; eṉṟālum, oru toṭarpāṉa upatēcaṅkaḷik kēṭṭataṉālum, cūkkuma carīrattiluḷḷa eṉ naṇparkaḷōṭu aṭikkaṭi kalantuṟavāṭiṉa taṉālum cākkāṭeṉpatu añcattakka taṉṟu eṉa nāṉ aṟintukoṇṭēṉ. eṉatu nilavāḻkkiyiṉ kaṭici nāṭkaḷil, nilavulakattit tuṟantu celvataṟkuc cila vāraṅ kaḷukku muṉṉiruntē ōr aṉpuḷḷa āvi eṉṉuṭaṉiruntu koṇṭu eṉakku utavi ceyvati uṇaralāṉēṉ. inta āvi, eṉatu nilavāḻkkiyiṉ iḷamparuvattil eṉakku naṇparā yiruntavarētām; in naṇpar iṟantataṉāl eṉakkuṇṭāṉa tuyaramē iṟantavarkaḷiṉ āvi enta maṇṭilattiṟkuc cellakkūṭumeṉpataṉit terintu koḷḷum viruppatti eṉpāl eḻuppi viṭṭatu. yāṉ kalviyaṟivu peṟṟuvanta āṇṭukaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum innaṇpar eṉakkup piriyāt tuṇiyuṭaṉiruntu vaḻikāṭṭi vantār. eṉ vāḻnāḷ elli aṇukutalum avaḷ eṉakku āṟutali uṇṭu paṇṇiṉāḷ; avaḷ eṉṉi varavēṟṟuc cūkkuma ulakattiṟkuc cellum vaḻiyiyum eṉakkuk kāṭṭiṉāḷ. avaḷuṭiya aṉpāṉa utaviyiṉāl accattiyum maraṇattiyum paṟṟiṉa ellā niṉivukaḷiyum yāṉ aṟavē oḻittuviṭṭēṉ; āvivaṭivil eṅkaḷi maṟupaṭiyum oruṅkukūṭṭum nēratti mikunta kaḷippōṭum etirnōkki vāḻttiṉēṉ.
tuṉpam iṉṟiyē ivvuṭampik kaṭantu ceṉṟēṉ; tuṉpattippaṟṟi yāṉ uṇaravum illi; āṉāl, eṉṉuṭampō iṉitāka iḷippāṟik kiṭantatu; ataṉi ṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭa eṉatuyirō, paḻutāyppōṉa oru caṭṭi yip pārppatupōla, ataṉmēl antarattil niṉṟu koṇṭu ataṉip pārttatu. oru noṭip poḻutēṉum kaḻintu pōvati yāṉ uṇarntilēṉ. eṉatuḷḷam uṟaṅka villi. eṉ āṉma uṇarvukaḷil etaṉiyum ōr imip poḻutēṉum yāṉ iḻantu viṭavum illi. yāṉ iṟantu pōkap pōkiṟēṉ eṉpatu eṉakkut teriyum. eṉatuyir paruvuṭampippaṟṟi yirunta niliyip paṭippaṭiyāka viṭṭu varum ovvōr imippoḻutiyum nāṉ aṟintēṉ. eṉakkup pakkattilē yiruntu eṉṉip pārttukkoṇṭirunta aṉparkaḷ evvāṟu irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kāṇum poruṭṭum yāṉ cellukiṟa putiya vāḻkkiyik kuṟittu avarkaḷ añcukiṟārkaḷā eṉṟu nōkkum poruṭṭum, paṟ pala āṇṭukaḷāka nāṅkaḷ oṉṟucērntu kaṟṟaṟinta poruḷ kaḷiliruntu avarkaḷi itaṉic cevviyākap poṟuttuk koḷḷukiṟārkaḷā veṉṟu teriyum poruṭṭum, nīṅkaḷ viṭṭup puṟappaṭa vēṇṭiya oru vīṭṭic cuṟṟip cuṟṟic pārppatupōla, iṭiyiṭiyē eṉatuṭam pākiya kuṭiyiruppiṉuḷ yāṉ nuḻintēṉ. avarkaḷ maṉavuṟuti yōṭum aṉpāṉa urukkattōṭum kaṭavuḷi vēṇṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar; pirintupōkum uyiriṉ paruvuṭampip paṟikoṭuppatil ōr aruvaruppu māttiram - aṉpāka nēcikkappaṭṭa naṇpariṉ uṭampic cārntirukka vēṇṭu meṉṉum miku viruppam māttiram, iṉṉum avarkaḷiṭattil maṟivā yirunta teṉpatu uṇmi.
uṟakkamāvatu kuṟinta uṇarcciyāvatu iṭi nikaḻak kaṇṭēṉilli; pala āṇṭukaḷākac caṅkiliyiṟ kaṭṭuṇṭu kiṭantavaṉ ak kaṭṭu aṟuntatuṅ kutittu eḻumāṟu pōlap putitu kāṇappaṭṭa eṉatuyir vāḻkkiyil yāṉum maṉakkiḷarcciyōṭu ceṉṟēṉ. eṉatu uyir vāḻnāḷiṉ piṟpakutiyil uṇṭāṉa nōy varuttattāl yāṉ vilaṅkiṭap paṭṭavaṉip pōl iruntēṉ. tāṅkuvataṟkuk kūṭāta taḷarcci uṇṭāṉami yiṉālē, iv vūṉuṭampākiya caṅkiliyāṟ piṇikkappaṭṭuk kiṭantē ṉeṉavē eṇṇiṉēṉ. ciṟiyil aṭikkappaṭṭiruntu veḷippaṭṭa oruvaṉ iṉiya kāṟṟil vantu amiḻntuvatu pōlavum, poṉ mayamāṉa kaṭaliṟ pāyvatu pōlavum yāṉum aṅkuk kaḷippāṟ ṟuḷḷiṉēṉ. eṉakkuc contamāyirunta iḷamiyum valimiyum āṇmiyum ākiya ellām tirumpavum eṉṉiṭam varak kaṇṭēṉ. eṉṉip paṟṟiṉa maṭṭil itaṉiṉum iṉṉum mēlāṉa cilavaṟṟi uṇaralāṉēṉ: cūkkumatattuva uyir vāḻkkiyil yāṉ mikunta aṉupava muṭiyavaṉāyiruntum, āvi vaṭivil niṟpōr palari yāṉ eṉṉiṭam varak kaṇṭum avarkaḷōṭu yāṉ kalantuṟavāṭiyum, eṉṉi nilavulakattōṭu piṇittuvitta kaṭṭuk kaṭiciyāka aṟupaṭṭa piṉṉar allāmal cūkkuma vāḻviṉ iyalpi itaṟku muṉ uṇmiyāka uṇarntēṉilli. eṉaturuva māṉatu iḷamiyum valivum uṭiya uruvamāka muṟṟum putukkap paṭṭataṉik kaṇṭu aḷaviṟanta viyappaṭintēṉ. itaṟkumuṉ eṉakku aṟimukamā yiruntavarkaḷiyum eṉakkut terinta naṇparkaḷiyum yāṉ kaṇṭu, avarkaḷ ovvoruvarum iḷiñarāyum, tamakkup piṟpāṭu cērnta cūkkuma vāḻkkiyiṉ aṉupavattāl aṟiñarāyum viḷaṅkutalit terintēṉ. appōtu tāṉ mutaṉmutal kuraloliyum allāmal, vēṟōr ōciyum allāmal, oru collum allāmal uyirk kalappāy māttiram uḷḷa āvi yoliyiṉ uṇarvāṟṟalit teḷintukoṇṭēṉ. eṉṉic cuṟṟiyuḷḷārkku eṉ niṉivu ovvoṉṟum teḷivāyt tōṉṟiyatōṭu, eṉṉuṭiya coṟkaḷum naṉku pulappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. puṟampēyuḷḷa oru poruḷip paṟṟi nīṅkaḷ pēcikkoḷvatu pōlavē, yāṉ appuṟappaṭuttivanta uṭampip paṟṟiyum pēcik koṇṭōm. eṉakkuppiṉ eṉ uṭampōṭu viṭappaṭṭa aimpoṟikaḷip paṟṟiya uṇarcciyāl yāṉ itaṟku muṉṉellām ciṟitu aṟivu mūṭappaṭṭirukka, ippōtō yāṉ āṉma caktikaḷ muḻutum niṟiyap peṟṟa āviyākak kuṟippiṭṭup pēcappaṭṭēṉ. eṉakku mutalil uṇṭāṉa niṉivu: yāṉ eṉ putalvikaḷōṭu pēcakkūṭumā? eṉpatētāṉ; āṉāl, aḵtu eṉṉāṟ kūṭavilli; aḵtāvatu cevippulaṉākum oli aṅkirukka muṭiyātu; āyiṉum, avarkaḷ uḷḷattil yāṉ eṇṇiyavaṟṟit teḷivākavum iṉitu viḷaṅkumāṟum patiyavikkakkūṭum. eṉ uṭampu aḻintu pōyiṟ ṟeṉṟum, uyirākiya tīppoṟi avintu pōyiṟṟeṉṟum tāṉ terintiruntālum eṉ kaṭikkōṭi makaḷ yāṉ taṉ etiriliruppati naṉku aṟintiruntāḷ.
itaṉi aṭuttu eṉakkuvanta āṉma uṇarcciyāvatu iyakkattip paṟṟiyatēyām. kutirikaḷiṉ mēlāyiṉum, allatu vēṟu vaḻakkamāṉa ūrtikaḷilāyiṉum cūkkuma ulakattiṉ ōriṭattiliruntu piṟitōr iṭattiṟku yāṉ koṇṭupōkap paṭutalik kaṇṭēṉ. ippōtu yāṉ putitu tōṉṟiya āṉma uṇarcciyākiya icciyiṉ valivit teḷiyalāṉēṉ. eṉ tōḻar, nām ippōtu namatu cūkkuma ulaka viṭutiyip pōyp pārppōm eṉṟu eṉakkuk kūṟiṉār. ēṟiccelvataṟku ētēṉum ōr ūrti kiṭikkumā veṉṟu cuṟṟi nōkkiṉēṉ; iv viruppam eṉmaṉattil ēṟiyavuṭaṉē, mutalil metuvākavum, piṉṉar eṉatu viruppam mikutippaṭutalum mikunta virivōṭum, kaṭiciyāka iṭiyē poruḷkaḷ iruntālum yāṉ avaṟṟik kāṇa ēlāta attaṉi virinta celavōṭum yāṉ cavāri pōtali viyappuṭaṉ kaṇṭēṉ. nāṅkaḷ virintu ceṉṟa ac celavu ōr imippoḻutu tāṉ irukkum. nāṅkaḷ aḷavupaṭāta iṭiveḷiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟatākat tōṉṟiyatu. cila vēḷi ulakaṅkaḷ pala emmik kaṭantu pōyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ; maṟṟuñ cilavēḷi tattam ellikaḷiṟ cellum kōḷkaḷiṉ teḷivāṉa ōcikaḷi yāṉ kēṭṭatākavum eṇṇiṉēṉ; vēṟu cila vēḷi tolivil olikkum iciyiṉ ōciyi yāṉ ceviyēṟṟatākavum eṇṇiṉēṉ. oḷiyum niḻalum oruṅku kalantu paraviyuḷḷa viḷakkamāṉa vāyil oṉṟiṉuḷḷē nāṅkaḷ ippōtu vantu niṉṟōm. cūkkuma ulakattiṟkum tūla ulakattiṟkum naṭuvil uḷḷatāṉa nuḻivāyil itutāṉ eṉṟum, itaṉ vaḻiyāka āṉma tattuva uṇmi yulakukku yāṉ cellappōkiṟēṉ eṉṟum collakkēṭṭēṉ. muṉṉē eṉakkut tōṉṟiya kāṭcikaḷil yāṉ aṅkē ceṉṟirunta tuṇṭu. muṉṉellām maṟṟiyōr utavikoṇṭu yāṉ ac cūkkuma ulakaṅkaḷip pārttuvantēṉ; āṉāl ippōtō yāṉē eṉ āṉmacaktikaḷik koṇṭu pārkkalāṉēṉ. muṉṉar eṉakku nērnta pārviyiṉum ippōtu nērntatu muṟṟilum vēṟāṉatāyiruntatu. ippōtu nāṅkaḷ ceṉṟa ovvōri ṭattum putiya putiya aḻakukaḷik kaṇṭēṉ. eṉ akak kaṇṇāṉatu cuṟṟilumuḷḷa poruḷkaḷik kāṇattakka cuṟucuṟuppuṭiyatāṉatōṭu, apporuḷkaḷiṉ āṉmāk kaḷiyum naṉku kāṇalāyiṟṟu; ovvoru vaṭivamum iṅkuḷḷa cuvarkaḷippōlat toṭakkūṭiyatā yiruntālum, uḷḷum puṟampum naṉku viḷaṅkit teḷivāyiruntatu; yāṉ kaṇṭa ellāp poruḷilum oru cīvatātu ūṭuruvit tōṉṟiyatu.
iṉi, ak kaṭṭiṭattiṉ taṉmiyippaṟṟi vicāraṇi ceyyalāṉēṉ. yāṉ pukunta vāyiliṉ amippāṉatu yāṉ cintikkakkūṭiya ellavāṟṟip pārkkilum mikap parumaṉāṉa kōpuravāyili allatu oru tēvālayattip peritum ottatā yiruntatu, palatiṟappaṭṭa niḻalum pala aḷavāṉa niṟaṅkaḷum nirampap poruttamākavum teḷivākavum kalantu kūṭṭappaṭṭa aḻakiya uruvaṅkaḷōṭu ak kaṭṭiṭam cērntu niṉṟamiyālē ataṉi uyirōṭu kūṭiya ōr uruvamākavē yāṉ nampakkūṭiyatā yiruntatu. eṉ tōḻar eṉatu karutti uṇarntu intap poruḷ nilattiṉ mēluḷḷa ellāp poruḷkaḷiṉum vēṟeṉpatu muṟṟilum uṇmitāṉ; calavikkalliṉ tuṇṭukaḷālum, uyarntu maṇikaḷālum amikkappaṭṭiruppatu pōl tōṉṟiṉālum itu cīvatātu niṟinta niṉiviṉ vaṭivēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. nīr ippōtu nuḻintirukkiṉṟa ulakattiṉ cīvamaṇṭilamēyaṉṟip piṟitaṉṟu. ivvaḻiyē cellum ovvōr uyirum iv vāyiliṉ aḻaki mikutippaṭuttutaṟku vēṇṭum nalattiṉic ciṟitu koṭuttuc celkiṉṟatu, eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. tuṟaviyic cūḻntuḷḷa katiroḷi vaṭṭattip pōla veṇmiyum nīlamuṅ kalanta vāyumaṇṭilam eṉṉic cuṟṟiyirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. inta nīlavāyu maṇṭilam eṉṉic cūḻat tiruttamāṉa vaṭiveṭuttatu; uṭaṉē av vāyiliṉ ūṭē oru vaḻi teṉpaṭṭatu; atil yāṉ ceṉṟēṉ. piṉṉar ataṉit tirumpip pārttēṉ; maṟṟōr uyir annilattiṟ pukuntamikku aṭiyāḷamāka av vāyil aṅṅaṉam irukkumpaṭi viṭappaṭṭatu. appoḻutu avvāyiliṉ vaḷivukaḷum, avaṟṟiṟku alaṅkāramāy aminta urukkaḷum, avaṟṟil eḻutappaṭṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷum varavarak kural oli kāṭṭuvaṉapōṟ ṟōṉṟip, piṟaku teḷivāṉa iciyil eṉ uyiri nalvaravēṟṟaṉa. itaṟkumuṉ yāṉ kēṭṭa iciyuḷ itu cērntatāyilli; icikkaruviyiliruntu eḻum oli pōliruntatu. cevippulaṉāl allāmal uyiriṉ aṟivāl māttiram kēṭkappaṭuvatāyuḷḷa mutirnta niṉiviṉ icipporuttattiyē atu peritum ottiruntatu.
eṉ orē tōḻarum yāṉumākap piṉṉuñ ceṉṟōm; ēṉeṉil yāṉ pārtta maṟṟa āvikaḷ ellām ippōtu maṟintu pōyiṉa. piṉṉum yāṅkaḷ appāṟ ceṉṟōm. iv vēḷiyil akaṉṟa ōr aticayamāṉa iṭam eṉ kāṭcikkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu. iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷilum periya malivīḻ āṟukaḷ paḷḷattākkukaḷiṟ cuḻaṉṟu ceṉṟaṉa; aḻakiya aruvikaḷ malip pakkaṅkaḷil tuḷḷik kutittaṉa. cūkkuma ulakattiluḷḷa ematu iruppiṭattiṟku itu nuḻivāyi lākumeṉṟu niṉivu kūrntēṉ. iraṇṭu malittoṭarkaḷukkum naṭuvē ūrti ētumillāmalē nāṅkaḷ vāy pēcātu miku virivāyc ceṉṟu, oru tiṟantaveḷiyiṟ cērntōm. cūkkuma ulakattil nāṅkaḷ iruppataṟkākat terinteṭukkappaṭṭa iṭam iṅkētāṉ iruntatu. ōr aṟikkuc cellum vāyilip pārkkilum akaṉṟatallāta ōr oṭukkamāṉa pātiyil nāṅkaḷ pukuntapōtu, uratta pāṟiyeṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭa oṉṟiliruntu putumiyāṉa pala pāvikaḷum paṭivaṅkaḷum maṅkalāyt tōṉṟuvati yāṉ kuṟippāyp pārttēṉ. ip pāvikaḷ paḻakkamāṉa mukaṅkaḷum uruvaṅkaḷum uṭiyaṉavā yiruttalum, maṉattiṟkup pulaṉāyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyum eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum pōṟ ṟōṉṟutalum kaṇṭēṉ. eṉatuyir vāḻnāḷil uṇṭāṉa ovvoru niṉiviyum ovvoru ceyaliyum yāṉ pārttēṉ. avikaḷiṟ cila niḻaluḷḷaṉavā yiruntaṉa; vēṟu cila mika iṉpamāṉa uruvaṅ kaḷuṭiyaṉavā yiruntaṉa; iṉṉuñ cila pakimiyuṅ koṭumiyum vāynta cāyalu ṭiyaṉavā yiruntamiyāl avaṟṟi maṟantu viṭuvatil viruppamuṭiyēṉāyiruntēṉ; piṉṉuñ cila ikaḻccikkum ciṉattiṟkum iṇaṅkiṉa vāyiruntaṉa; āṉāl, iṉṉum muṉṉē yāṉ ceṉṟapōtu mēlāṉa aṉpuḷḷa mukaṅkaḷum vaṭivaṅkaḷum nirampavum mikutiyāyirukkavum, yāṉ uyiruḷḷa āviyāy at tiṟantaveḷiyiṟ ceṉṟatum aṉpōṭum irakkattōṭuṅ kūṭiyavaṟṟi allāmal vēṟuruvaṅkaḷ illātirukkavum, makiḻcci niṟintavaṟṟi allāmal vēṟolikaḷ illātirukkavuṅ kaṇṭēṉ.
oru maṅkiyiṉ kaḻuttaṇiyil taṉittaṉiyē patikkap paṭṭa muttukkaḷippōla ovvoru tuḷiyum attaṉi teḷivāka vuḷḷa oru nīrōṭṭattil yāṉ iṅkē amiḻntiṉāṟpōṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu. ittuḷikaḷ ov voṉṟum cila aṉpāṉa niṉivi allatu teḷivāṉa cāratti uḷḷaṭakki niṟpatupōl tōṉṟiyatu; inta nīrōṭṭattil yāṉ amiḻntiṉatum, maṇṇulakattil uṇṭāṉa kaṟikaḷ cilavaṟṟi eṭuttuviṭuvatupōla ovvoru tuḷiyum eṉ nuṇṇuṭampil koṭṭiṉatu. an nīrōṭṭattil neṭu nēram yāṉ irukka irukka ataṉit tāṅkik koḷvatu eṉakku eḷitāyiṟṟu. mutalil atu neruppip pōṟ cuṭṭuk koḷuttuvatākak kāṇappaṭṭatu; piṉṉar atu mēlum mēlum iṉiyatāki ovvoru tuḷiyum eṉ uḷḷattiṟ cila uṇmikaḷit terivittu eṉṉuṭaṉ pēcak kaṇṭēṉ. kaṭiciyāka ataṉ maṟṟap pakkattil yāṉ mēleḻuntavuṭaṉē, eṉ tōḻarāl puṉcirippōṭu varavēṟkappeṟṟēṉ. iḵtu umatu maṇṇuṭampiṉ kaṭiciyāka uḷḷa kaṟikaḷi nīkkukiṉṟatu; āyiṉum, nīr maṇṇulakiṟ ceyta ellāp piḻikaḷiṉ muṭivāṉa payaṉkaḷiyum itu nīkkukiṉṟilatu, eṉṟu avar moḻintār. anta ammiyārukkum eṉakkum uḷḷa cila vittiyācam eṉ uṇarvukkut teṉpaṭalāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum, avvammiyiṉ nilimiyi yāṉ iṉṉum muṟṟum aṭiyavilli yeṉṟum, āṉālum anta iṉiya nīrōṭṭattil muḻukiyatiliruntu yāṉ mikunta aṟiviyum mikunta kalviyiṉiyum eyti eṉakkirunta kuṟipāṭukaḷ paṭippaṭiyē nīṅkappeṟṟēṉeṉṟum terintukoṇṭēṉ.
inta nilattiṉmēṟ peyar aṟiyappaṭāta malarkaḷāl irupuṟattum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ōr aḻakiya koṭip pantarukku avvammiyār eṉṉip piṉṉar vaḻi naṭattik koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. nīṅkaḷ pārttup paḻakkap paṭṭirukkiṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷum uruvaṅkaḷum avikaḷ alla; avikaḷiṉ maṇaṅkaḷē cevikaḷukku iṉiya ōcikaḷit tarukiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum niṟaṅkaḷumō cila eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum, vaḻutturikaḷiyum, uyarnta nāṭṭaṅkaḷiyum terivittaṉa. eṅkaḷ iruppiṭattiṉ vāyilukku iṉṉum arukil avvammiyār eṉṉi aḻittuc ceṉṟaṉar. ataṉ vaṭiviyum uruviyum yāṉ pārkkak kūṭiyatāyiruntatu. ataṉ cuvarkaḷiyum vāyiliyum yāṉ uṅkaḷukkut teḷivāyc collik kāṭṭa muṭiyum. ataṉ uruvamippil yāṉ kaṇṭa ovvoru vaṭivilum, eṉṉuṭaṉirunta avvammiyāriṉ iyalpukaḷi yāṉ terintukoḷḷak kūṭiyatā yiruntateṉṟu colluvatit tavira maṟṟoṉ ṟiyuñ colli uṅkaḷi iṅkē vīṇē niṟuttivikka māṭṭēṉ. avvammiyār iṅkirunta kālattilum cūkkuma ulakattilum avar tamakku vaḻakkamāyuriya eṇṇaṅkaḷāl aḵtu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭirunta teṉpati yāṉ kāṇak kūṭiyatāyiruntatu. iṟivaṉi vaḻuttiya ovvoru collum ceyalum, nalnāṭṭamum, urukkamāṉa aṟac ceykiyum ivvuṟiyuḷil uruvu koṇṭiruntaṉa. eṉṉuṭiya eṇṇaṅ kaḷuṅkūṭa aṅku iṭiyiṭiyē piṉṉappaṭṭiruttalik kaṇṭēṉ. yāṉ koṇṭa nalleṇṇaṅkaḷum, vēṇṭukōḷ moḻikaḷum, nalnōkkaṅkaḷum, neṭunāḷikku muṉṉamē yāṉ maṟantu pōṉa ceykikaḷum aṅkē uyirōṭu aḻakāy viḷaṅkiṉavē yallāmal vēṟutūṇākavum pāvi yākavun tōṉṟavilli. eṉatuyir vāḻkkiyiṟ kiḷitta niṉivukaḷum vāḻt turikaḷum nalnāṭṭaṅkaḷum eṉakketiril oru variciyāka oḻuṅkupaṭuttap paṭṭiruttalik kaṇṭēṉēyaṉṟi eṉṉuṭiya kuṟipāṭukaḷik kaṇṭēṉilli; ākavē, avari nōkki : iḵtu eṉṉi? inta namatu iruppiṭattil eṉ nalleṇṇaṅ kaḷi māttiraṅ kāṇkiṉ- ṟēṉē yallāmal eṉ kuṟi pāṭukaḷik kāṇavilliyē? eṉṟu uṭaṉē viṉaviṉēṉ. ataṟku avvammiyiṉ niṉivu ‘nam āṉmākkaḷ irukkum cūkkuma uṟiviṭattil kuṟipāṭukaḷ irukkamuṭiyā; avaṟṟi nīr vāyiliṉiṭattē kaṇṭiruppīr; umatu vaḻineṭuka nīr avikaḷip pārttiruppīr; uyirt taṉmi vāynta cūkkuma uṟiviṭattil kuṟivillātaṉavē muṭivāṉa uruvum vaṭivum eṭukkakkūṭum? eṉṟu viṭi pakarntatu.
yāṉ evvaḷavu kuṟipāṭu uṭiyavaṉ eṉpati appōtutāṉ kaṇṭēṉ; yāṉ takutiyaṟṟavaṉ eṉṉum uṇarcciyāṉatu avviṉpa nilaṅkaḷiliruntu eṉṉip piṉvāṅkic cellumpaṭi ceyyavallatā yiruntatu; āṉāl, avvammiyār eṉṉi accamēṉum naṭukkamēṉum aṭiyalākāteṉṟu kaṟpittār. ēṉeṉil, maṇṇu lakattip paṟṟiya ovvoru niṉivum kuṟṟamum eṉatu muyaṟciyiṉālēyē nīkkappaṭa vēṇṭumātaliṉ eṉpatu. ivviṭattē niliyākat taṅkuvataṟku ēṟṟa taṉmi iṉṉum ummiṭattil muṟṟum uṇṭākavilli; umatu illam itutāṉ; umatu muyaṟciyiṉālum, kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭi ṉālum, ovvoru nāḷum ovvoru nāḻikiyum vaḷarum aṟiviṉālum cūkkuma ulakiṉkaṇ uḷḷa ivvīṭṭiṉ kaṇ tārāḷamāy makiḻcciyum taṉṉuṇarvum mikuntu nīr irukkappeṟuvīr, eṉṟu avvammiyār moḻintār.
ataṉpiṟaku tāṉ mutaṉ mutal yāṉ kaḷippaṭiyak kaṇṭēṉ. ip putiya uṟiyuḷiṉ pēroḷiyum, uḷḷat teḻunta kaḷippum, tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭappeṟṟa uṇarcciyum eṉṉik kaḷippaṭiyac ceytaṉa. yāṉ ōr uyireṉṟum, iṉi yāṉ eṉ uṭampiṟ pukutalākāteṉṟum uṭaṉē nāṉ terintu koḷḷak kūṭavilli. muntirik koṭikaḷālum malarkaḷālum piṉṉalāyuḷḷa oru pantariṉāl ivvuṟiyuḷiṉ maṟṟap pakutiyiṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭṭatupōṟ ṟōṉṟiya oru taṉiyaṟikku avvammiyār eṉṉi naṭattic ceṉṟār. itaṟkuṭceṉṟu ivvaḷavu nēram eṉṟu aṟiyāmal neṭunēram aṅkē uṟaṅkiṉēṉ; yāṉ maṟupaṭiyum viḻitteḻuntatum eṉ āṉmacaktikaḷellām putukkap paṭṭatupōl uṇarntēṉ; eṉṉi mutaliṟ kaḷippaṭiyac ceyta makiḻciṟanta āṉmatattuva eḻil nalaṅkaḷ ippōtu eṉṉi mikavum valivaṭiyap purintaṉa. eppōtum eṉ pakkattilirunta avvammiyāri nōkki, ippōtu nāṉ āyattamāy irukkiṉṟēṉ; ic ciṟanta uyirvāḻkkiyi iṉṉum mikutiyāka eṉakkuk kāṭṭuṅkaḷ, eṉṟēṉ.
mutalil metuvākavum vēṇṭāveṟuppākavum iruntatupōl allāmal ippōtuṭaṉē eṅkaḷ vaḻiyāṉatu tiṟantu kāṇappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu; eṉakketirē tūrattiliruntālum teḷivākap pulappaṭṭa āvikaḷiṉ kūṭṭam oṉṟik kaṇṭēṉ. maṇṇulakattil eṉakku mikunta paḻakkamum aṟimukamum uḷḷavarāyiruntavarkaḷi arukiṟ kaṇṭēṉ. av āvēcak kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum vantu eṉṉi mutaṉmutal vāḻttit tām aṅku vantiruttalit terivittaṟ poruṭṭu mikavum paraparappōṭu muyaṉṟavar niyūyārk nītimaṉṟam eṉṉum pattirikkikku muṉṉē āciriyarā yirunta hore kirīli eṉṉum eṉ nēcarētām. ivar cila kālam āṉmatattuva vicāraṇi ceytavarāyiruntālum, veḷikku avvāṟu tōṉṟiṉār allar. ummi vāḻttip, pala āṇṭukaḷāka nītiyiṉṟirunta niliyi māṟṟik koḷḷa virintu vantēṉ, eṉṟu avar kūṟiṉār. ah‹ ‘m~J v‹id? eṉṟu viṉaviṉēṉ. ataṟku avar nīr intap putiya uyir vāḻkkiyip paṟṟi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉa cāṉṟukaḷi nāṉ kuṟivāka matittēṉē; ippōtu iḵtu uṇmiya ṉupavamā yiruttalik kaṇṭu iṅṅaṉam kaṇṭa uṇmiyi umakku eṭutturittaṉāl maṉaamiti peṟṟirukkiṉṟēṉ, eṉṟu avar maṟumoḻi pukaṉṟār. cūkkuma ulaka vāḻkki yip paṟṟi avar tamakkuc ciṟitun teriyā teṉpataṉi yāṉ ennēramum avarukkuc colli vantēṉ; nāṅkaḷ ellārum oruṅku cērntu cūkkuma tattuva ārāyccik kūṭṭam iṭṭu uṭkārnta kālaṅkaḷilellām, avar aracāṅka kāriyaṅkaḷic cīrtiruttañceyyum muyaṟciyil tamatu karuttic celuttuvār; yāṉō āṉmatattuva ārāycciyiṟ karuttāyiruntēṉ. avar aṅṅaṉam viṭṭuc colliyatik kēṭṭu makiḻntēṉ; aḵtu eṉakku āṟutali uṇṭupaṇṇi valiyit tantatu. āṉmatattuva ārāycciyil eṉakkutavi yāyiruntavarum eṉakkuc ciṟanta paḻiya naṇparum āṉa mēp paṇṭitari yāṉ piṉṉar etirppaṭṭēṉ. uṭaṉē avar eṉṉi nōkki, ā! nāṉ nilavulakattiliruntapōtu caṭap poruḷiṉ vēṟākac cittup poruḷukkuḷḷa āṟṟalkaḷip paṟṟi eṉakkuc ciṟitun teriyātu; āṉāl ippōtō nīr kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷellām peritum uṇmiyeṉṟē kāṇkiṉṟēṉ,  eṉṟu moḻintār. eṉṉi varavēṟṟu vāḻttum poruṭṭu ivvoḷi yulakattil oruvar piṉ oruvarāy vantavar kaḷiyellām yāṉ uṅkaḷukku variciyāyccolla vēṇṭuvatilli. varavēṟkappaṭṭa uyirkaḷi vāḻtta vēṇṭumeṉṟu av āvikaḷ tamakkuḷ kūṭip pēcik koṇṭārkaḷeṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu; eṉṟālum, avarkaḷ oṉṟu kūṭiyatu ataṉ poruṭṭākavē aṉṟu. avarkaḷ aṅkē tamatu vaḻakkappaṭi kuḻumiyatu uyirkaḷiṉ iruppip paṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷi ārāyntu pēcutaṟ poruṭṭētāṉ. avarkaḷ kumpu kumpāy oḻuṅkākak kūṭiyiruntatupōṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉar; ovvoru kumpiṉ naṭuvilum talivar oruvar iruntār. oṉṟaṉ naṭuvil pirāṅkliṉ eṉpavarik kaṇṭēṉ; avar tammic cūḻa iruntōrkku ippōtu maṇṇulakattil uṇṭākum tōṟṟaṅkaḷippaṟṟic cila cūkkuma paricōtaṉi ceyyumpaṭi avarkaḷukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭikkoṇṭiruntār. pūtaputika nūlārāycciyiluḷḷa ellā viṣayaṅkaḷilum avar talimivāynta pēraṟiviṉar. avar cūkkuma ulaka vāḻkkiyiṟ pukuntatu mutal miṉcāra viḷakkaṅkaḷiyum atupōṉṟa caktikaḷiyum paṟṟiya ārāycciyē avarukkup piratāṉa viṣayamā yiruntatu. avar putiyaṉavākak kaṇṭu piṭitta uṇmikaḷē caṭattiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷ eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamakkup piriyamāṉa avviṣaya ārāycci yiṉālum paṭippiṉālum avar māttiramē atē cūkkuma ulakattiluḷḷa maṟṟavarkaḷutaviyōṭu parupporuḷ acivukaḷiyum iyakkaṅkaḷiyum ōcikaḷiyum maṇṇulakattiluḷḷa mantira caktiyuḷḷōr vaḻiyē veḷippaṭum āvikaḷiyum paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷi uṇmip paṭuttik kāṭṭik koṇṭiruntār. avarit talimiyākak koṇṭa āvēcak kūṭṭattavar avariṭattiṉiṉṟum pala uṇmikaḷit terintu piṟaku maṟṟa āṉmākkaḷukku avaṟṟi aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. ippōtu iv āvikaḷ im maṇṇulaka meṅkum paravic ciṟcatti, caṭacattiyi āḷavallatām uṇmiyi māntarukku veḷiyiṭṭu meyppaṭuttik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. iv viḷakkaṅ kaḷiṟ pulaṉāki avartam vicēṭa maṉaviyaṟkiyōṭu poruttamuṟṟu niṟkum kuṟikaḷiyum iṭaṅkaḷiyum paṟṟic colvataṟku eṉakku nēramum illi; iḵtu avaciyamum aṉṟu. eṉiṉum, parupporuḷil uṇṭām tōṟṟaṅkaḷ ali aliyāy eḻukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, ivikaḷ oru miyattiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟi innilavulakattil neṭuntūrattil uḷḷavarkaḷum ic cattikaḷiṟ ciṟitu peṟum aḷavukkuk kaṭalalikaḷ pōla im maṇmēṟ paravic celkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum nīṅkaḷ teritalvēṇṭum. cūkkuma āvi vaṭivaṅkaḷ kaṭpulaṉākum eṉṉum uṇmiyāṉatu mutaṉmutal amerikkā nāṭṭiṟ ṟōṉṟi ippoḻutu iṅkilāntu nāṭṭiṉ mēṟ ṟāviccelkiṉṟatu. aḵtu iṅkum vantu kiṭṭiyatu; iṉi atu tūratēcaṅkaḷukkuñ ceṉṟu cērum; kaṭiciyāka, iṉṉun tōṉṟāta piṟitōr aliyiṉāṟ piṉṟoṭarappaṭum. ākavē, iḵtu oḻukkanūl muṟi yātalōṭu putika nūl nuṭpaṅkaḷit terintu koṇṭa oru mēlāṉa ēṟpāṭākavum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. nilattiṉ mēluḷḷa putikanūl vallār palariviṭac cūkkuma ulakaṅkaḷil uḷḷōr ip putiya cattikaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyit terintukoḷvatilum ārāy vatilum nirampavuṅ karuttāyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
māṉata āvēcat tōṟṟaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya ārāycciyil tamatu valiyiyum nērattiyum celutti niṟpavarāṉa maṟṟoruvarip piṟitōr iṭattiṟ kaṇṭēṉ. avarum kalviyiṉ mikkavarāyiruntār! yōka nittiriyip paṟṟit tām kaṇṭupiṭitta kōṭpāṭukaḷāl putikanūṟ carittira kālattil oru periya vicēṣatti viḷivitta mecumar (Mesmer) eṉpavarip paṟṟiyē yāṉ kuṟippiṭṭuc collu kiṉṟēṉ. avar ippōtu āvēcaṅkaḷi vacappaṭuttum vaciya nūlārāycciyil taliyiṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. avarukku māṇavakarum kūṭa viruntu utaviceyvōrum uḷar; ivarkaḷ ōreṇṇa muṭiyavarāy oṉṟupaṭṭu mantiracakti vāyn tōri āvēcittum, avarkaḷ mēlōṅkutaṟku utavi ceytum, tam naṇparkkuc ceyti aṉuppumāṟu tammic cūḻntirukkum āvēcak kūṭṭattārkku ottāci purintu koṇṭu maṇṇulakattiṉmēl iyaṅkukiṟārkaḷ. uṅkaḷ iruppiṭaṅkaḷukku vantu katavit taṭṭutalāṉum āvēcam ēṟi āṭutalāṉum nīṅkaḷ aṟiyāta vēṟu vakiyāṉum tamatu iruppi uṅkaḷukku aṟivikkum vaṇṇam putitākak kaṇṭupiṭitta ivvuṇmi yip piḷḷikaḷippōṟ kaṟṟukkoṇṭu vanta kaṇakkillāta pallāyiram āvikaḷ taṉittaṉik kūṭṭamāka ivarkaḷic cūḻntirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. nīṅkaḷ uṅkaḷ naṇparkaḷukkuc ceyti aṟivikkumpaṭiyāṉa oruvaḻiyi allatu tantiyik kaṟṟukkoḷḷutaṟku vakuppukaḷiṟ cērvatupōla, āṉmatattuva vāḻkkiyit terintu koḷvataṟkuriya ic capikaḷiṟ cērum viruppam uḷḷa uṅkaḷ naṇparkaḷ in nilavulakat tuḷḷār palarkku uṇṭu: ippōtu nilavulakatti acittuvarum av vāṟṟalip peṟum poruṭṭum, maṉitarkku āvēcaṅkaḷiṉ uṇmiyit terivittaṟ poruṭṭum avikaḷ avaric cūḻntu niṉṟaṉa.
iṉip pala tēca māntariṉ carittiraṅkaḷip paṟṟik karuttāyp pēcikkoṇṭirunta vēṟōr uyarnta kūṭṭattārik kaṇṭēṉ; in nilavulakattit tuṟantu ceṉṟa rājatantira nipuṇarkaḷiṉ mukaṅkaḷiyum uruvaṅkaḷiyum yāṉ ak kūṭṭattiṉ iṭiyē teḷivākat terintu koḷḷak kūṭiyatā yiruntatu. oru kūṭṭattiṉ iṭiyē uvāṣiṅṭaṉ eṉpavar niṟkak kaṇṭēṉ. tamatu irattaveṟi aṭaṅkap peṟṟamiyāl ippōtu tēcamāntariṉ vaḷattiyum cukattiyum kōrikkoṇṭu maṟṟak kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvē niṟpavarāṉa neppōliyaṉ eṉpavariyum cīcar eṉpavariyum kaṇṭēṉ. oḷiviḷaṅku mukaṅkaḷum katir viḷaṅkum puruvaṅkaḷum uṭiyōrāy, aikkiya mākāṇap peruṅkaḻakattil cutantira uṇarcci naṭipeṟṟāṟpōla avvat tēcak kuṭimakkaḷiṉ caṭṭa nirumāṇak kūṭṭaṅkaḷil iyaṅkutaṟku viruppam vāynta periya āvēcak kūṭṭat tārkku avarkaḷ kaṟpittuk koṇṭiruttalik kaṇṭēṉ. cutantira viruppam vāyntamiyāl iliṅkaṉ eṉpavar āviyuruvil eḻuntulavu taliyuṅ kaṇṭēṉ; katir viṭum av voḷḷiya kūṭṭattil māntariṉ tiruttattiṟkuriya iṉṉum periya ēṟpāṭu kaḷellām ceyyappaṭutaliyuṅ kaṇṭēṉ. muṉṉē iṟantu pōṉa cārla camnar eṉpavar āvi yulakattil eḻuntu iṅkē pōla aṅkum aṭimikaḷiṉ cutantirattip paṟṟi vaḻakkiṭak kaṇṭēṉ.
ippōtu yāṉ uṅkaḷukkut teriyappaṭuttak kūṭāta iṉṉum palariyuṅ kaṇṭēṉ; avarkaḷ eṉakku aṟimukam uḷḷavarkaḷē; āṉālum avarkaḷuṭiya uṇarvum eṇṇamum, eṉakkup putiyavāyt tōṉṟiṉa. eṉ uṭkaruvi kaḷiṉ aḷavukku mēṟpaṭṭaṉavāy iruttali yaṟintēṉ. eṉṟālum, yāṉ it tōṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ paḻakappaḻaka, ettaṉiyō ūḻik kālaṅkaḷāka aṟivil mutirntu vanta āṉṟōr tiruvaṭikkīḻ iruntu upatēcaṅ kēṭṭuvarum kūṭṭat tāriṭiyil yāṉ cēraccēra, yāṉ iṅkirunta kāliyiṟ ceyya māṭṭāmalirunta oru peru muyaṟciyic ceyyavallavaṉāvēṉ eṉṟu collakkēṭṭēṉ; ataṉi yāṉ im maṇṇulakattiṟ ceyya māṭṭā maliruntatu ūṉuṭampiṉ valiviṉmiyāṉum variyaṟippaṭṭa karuvi kaḷiṉ iyalpiṉā ṉumēyām; āyiṉum, yāṉ am muyaṟciyil mikunta āval uṭiyēṉā yiruntēṉ eṉpati nīṅkaḷ ellōrum aṟivīrkaḷ. eṉatu vēli iṉi mēṟṟāṉ toṭaṅka vēṇṭuvatāy irukkiṉṟatu. iṉi eṉṉuṭiya muyaṟcikaḷum eṇṇaṅkaḷu mellām yāṉ aṟivu peṟutalilum, yāṉ peṟṟa av vaṟivi aṟiyāmiyiṟ kaṭṭuppaṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟa varkaḷukku eṭuttup pukaṭṭuvatilum kuvintu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru maṉitarukkuṅ koṭukkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟa aṟiveṉṉum peruṅ koṭiyāṉatu ellāvaṟṟiyum arittu varum kālattiṟkum akappaṭāmal piḻittiruttalum, kālamum, uṭampum aḻintupōkat tāṉ aḻiyāmal taṉakkuriya āvi maṇṭilattil taṉṉiruppi liruttalum evvaḷavu viyappākavum teyvattaṉmi vāynta tākavum kuṟivaṟṟatākavum irukkiṉṟa teṉpati yāṉ uṇarntēṉ. iṉṉum, ō! yāṉ viṭṭuvanta māntarkaḷ tamatu akakkaṇ tiṟakkappeṟṟu yāṉ kāṇpatupōlak kāṇpār kaḷāyiṉ yāṉ ippōtu aṟivatip pōlat tāmum aṟivarāyiṉ, ettaṉiyō ūḻik kālaṅkaḷākak kavintu koṇṭu varum maraṇa payattiṉiṉṟum nīṅki evvaḷavu pēriṉpaṅkaḷi aṭi vārkaḷ! avarkaḷ nittiya vāḻvi ṉiyum amirutattiyum nērukku nēr kāṇparallarō! eṉṟu uṇarvēṉ āṉēṉ.
āṉāl, iṅkē muḻutum iṉpanukarcciyē uḷḷataṉṟu. maṟupaṭiyum eṉ kuṟipāṭukaḷi uṇaralāṉēṉ; ivvuṇarcci tōṉṟiyatum eṉ uḷḷam kiḷarcci kuṉṟit tuṉpa muṟalā yiṟṟu. avarkaḷ evvaḷavutāṉ eṉṉi varavēṟṟu makiḻn tālum, ‘nāṉ eṉṉa kuḻantiyā yirukkiṉṟēṉē! eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ. ūḻi ūḻiyāka aṟivumikappeṟṟa āṉṟōr naṭuviliruntu koṇṭu iṅṅaṉam yāṉ uṇaravē oru ciṟu piḷḷiyip pōlak kūccam eyti eṉ uḷḷattiṉ veṟun taṉmiyi niṉintēṉ. ataṉpiṟaku yāṉ muṉṉē kaṇṭaṟi yāta ētō ōriṭattiliruntu uruvaṅkaḷ pala veḷi vantu eṉ etirē maṇṭiyiṭṭu, āṉmatattuva vāḻkkiyip paṟṟit tāṅkaḷ eṉakku mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṟpittīrkaḷ; maṇṇulakatti liruntapōtu mutaṉ mutaṟ ṟaṅkaḷiṭattiruntē uyiriṉ iruppippaṟṟi yāṉ aṟivukūṭap peṟṟēṉ, eṉṟu ovvoṉṟāyc collikkoṇṭē eṉ aṭikaḷiṉmēl ovvoru malari iṭṭaṉa. pukaḻp pāṭṭukaḷum vēṇṭu kōḷ moḻikaḷum vantatupōlavē, ap pūkkaḷōṭu naṟiya cāmpirāṇip pukiyum vantatu. in nivētaṉaṅkaḷi eṉṉuḷḷam uṟiñci yuṭkoḷḷavē, yāṉ vīriyam mikap peṟutal ōrntēṉ; ataṉāl eṉ uruvamum valivaṭintatu. yāṉ ivarkaḷukku utavi ceytatip paṟṟik kaḷikūrntēṉ. avar tamatu aṉpiṉāṟ celuttiya kāṇikkikaḷiṉāl yāṉ cūḻappeṟṟirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. tāṅkaḷ itaṉi emakkuc ceytīrkaḷ! yāṅkaḷ nilavulakatti viṭṭu varutaṟku muṉpē emakku maṟumiyippaṟṟit terivittīrkaḷ; ākavē ippōtu ematu kāṇikkiyit taṅkaḷiṭam koṇṭuvantu cērppik kiṉṟōm eṉṟu avarkaḷ kūṟiṉārkaḷ. ataṉ piṟaku yāṉ oḷi mikappeṟṟēṉ pōl tōṉṟiyatu; yāṉ muṉṉē nōkkikkoṇṭirunta āṉṟōr aṇintirunta uṭipōla eṉatu uṭiyum kāṇappaṭṭatu; putitu putitāka varuṅ kāṇikkiyiṉāl naṉṟiyaṟitalippaṟṟi eṉ uḷḷam niṉivu mikuntu valivaṭintatu; kaṭiciyāka yāṉ ākāyattiṟ paṟantu ceṉṟu, eṉakku aṟimukam uḷḷavarkaḷ naṭuviṟpōy amarntēṉ. ippōtu nīr eṅkaḷil oruvarāyiṉīr, eṉṟu avarkaḷ eṉṉi nōkkik kūṟiṉārkaḷ.
paḻakkappaṭṭa niṉivukaḷum acaṭṭiyāṟ pukunta eṇṇaṅkaḷum ēṟi uyiril uṇṭāṉa kaṟikaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭu yāṉ taṉiyē yirukkiṉṟēṉ. eṉṟālum, yāṉ kāṇkiṉṟa āṉṟōr palar aṭintirukkum uyarnta niliyi yāṉ aṭitaṟku iṉṉum pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻiya vēṇṭu meṉa uṇarap peṟṟēṉ. kāṇṭaṟkum ēlāta attaṉi paḷa paḷappuḷḷa āṉmākkaḷ cila irukkum oḷiyulaka vāḻviṉ urimiyi nāṉ peṟutaṟku muṉ yāṉ eṉṉi vayappaṭuttu taṟku neṭuṅkālam uḻippeṭukka vēṇṭum. cila vēḷikaḷil eṉ aṉpuḷḷa tōḻar eṉ pakkattirukka nāṅkaḷ iruvēmum aravamiṉṟit taṉittirukkum eṅkaḷ cūkkuma iruppiṭattiṉ kaṇṇē amarntiruntu. mēṉili yiluḷḷa cāṉṟārōṭu kalantu pēcukiṉṟōm; avarkaḷil oruvariṉ uṇmiyip paṟṟiyum niṟinta aṉpippaṟṟiyum māntar naṉkuṇarn tirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, am māntar avar ceṉṟa vaḻiyē ciṟitum celkiṉṟārkaḷilli; avarutaviyip peṟṟu avarkaḷmēl uyarvatumilli. iṅṅaṉam kūṭiyirukkum āvēcak kūṭṭaṅkaḷukku nirampavum mēlē, uyarvum aṟivum naṉmiyum mikka āṉṟōr kuḻāttiṟku mikavum mēlē maṇṇulakattārāl urittaṟkum eḻututaṟkum aritāṉa tulakkamum aḷavaṟṟa aṉpum katir virikkum vaṭivamum vāynta oru viḷaṅku moḷiyi yāṉ kāṇakkūṭiyatā yiruntatu; at tiruvuruvattiṉ kaṭṭaḷikaḷi ippōtu yāṉ uṅkaḷukku eṭuttup pukalkiṉṟēṉ. nīṅkaḷ oruvari yoruvar nēcikkak kaṭavīrkaḷ eṉpatē atuvām. im moḻikaḷāṉavi tēvatūtarkaḷ āvēcaṅkaḷ mutaliyōr uḷḷaṅkaḷiṟ pukuntu av vaḻiyē uṅkaḷ neñcaṅkaḷilumkūṭa nuḻintu, accam paki vayiṟ ṟericcal pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi yellām ōṭṭi inta noṭiyiliruntē uṅkaḷi iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷōṭu oṉṟupaṭuttā niṟkum.
eṉṟitukāṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya uṇmikaḷāl cūkkuma oḷiyākāyattiṉ iyalpukaḷum, ataṉkaṇ ulavum cūkkuma carīravācikaḷiṉ pala tiṟappaṭṭa nilikaḷum iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum. aḵtu aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya carittira utāraṇaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa mēl nāṭukaḷil vacikkum veḷḷikkārar tamakkuriya aṉupavaṅkaḷā yirutta liṉ, maṟumiyulakaṅkaḷ eṉṟu īṇṭeṭuttu viḷakkappaṭṭavi aṉittum veḷḷikkārar āvikaḷ māttiram niṟintaṉa vāyirukkum pōlum eṉṟu karuti aiyamuṟuvārkku avviyam nikaḻāmip poruṭṭu atippaṟṟiya uṇmiyiṉiyum oru ciṟitu teḷivuppaṭuttik kāṭṭuvām.
18. iṅkuḷḷapaṭi aṅkum
ivvuṇmiyi naṉkuṇara vēṇṭuvōr innila vulakattiṉ iyaṟkikaḷic ciṟitu ārāyntu pārpparāyiṉ atu cevviyākap pulappaṭum. im maṇṇulakattiṉ mēṟparappāṉatu pala nāṭukaḷāka vakukkap paṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟatu; avvan nāṭukaḷil uḷḷa makkaḷiṉ mukaṅkuṟi kural cāyal mutaliyaṉavellām palatiṟappaṭṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; aṅkuḷḷa paṟavikaḷum vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷuṅ kūṭap palvēṟu taṉmi yuṭiyaṉavāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa; āṅkāṅkuḷḷa marañceṭi koṭikaḷuṅkūṭa vēṟuvēṟu iyaṟkiyuṭiyaṉavākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa; ivvuyirp poruḷ kaḷōṭu oṉṟupaṭṭu iyantu niṟkum nila amippukaḷuṅkūṭa vēṟupaṭṭa iyalpiṉavākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. airōppiya nāṭṭil vacikkum veḷḷikkārar evvaḷavu veṇmiyāṉa niṟamum aḻakum uyarnta tōṟṟamum uṭiyarāyirukkiṉṟaṉar! āppirikkā nāṭṭil vacikkum nikirōvar evvaḷavu karumiyāṉa niṟamum parutta utaṭum nīṇṭa mōvāyum curuṇṭa mayirumuḷḷavarāyp pārkkap pāntamiṉṟi yirukkiṉṟaṉar! cīṉatēcattil uḷḷa māntar akaṉṟa mukamum cappi mūkkum paḻuppāṉa niṟamum uṭiyavarā yirukkiṉṟaṉar; nam intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷavarkaḷō tiruttamāṉa avayavaṅkaḷuṭi rāyiṉum cilar kariya niṟamum vēṟu cilar poṉṉiṟamum maṟṟuñ cilar ivviraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉa niṟamum uḷḷavarāy irukkiṉṟaṉar; amerikkā tēcattiluḷḷavarkaḷō taṭitta uṟuppukaḷōṭu cevēleṉṟa cenniṟamum vāyntu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉar; iṅṅaṉam palatiṟappaṭṭa iyaṟkikaḷuṭiya makkaḷōṭu otta iyalpiṉavāka avarkaḷic cūḻntuḷḷa pirāṇikaḷum marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷum ēṉip poruḷkaḷum iruttali ūrp pirāyaṇañ ceytavarkaḷ naṉkuṇarvārkaḷ. iṉṉum ivvāṟu palavēṟu vakiyākak kāṇappaṭum makkaḷuṭiya camayakkoḷkikaḷum, avvac camayaṅkaḷil vaṇaṅkappaṭum teyvaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟi vaḻipaṭum muṟikaḷum, naṭiyuṭi pāvaṉikaḷum aḷaviṟanta pētaṅkaḷ uṭiyaṉa vāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. ittaṉi pētaṅkaḷiyum tāmē nēriṟ kaṇṭāyiṉum, allatu nūlkaḷiṟ pārttāyiṉum ulaki yalaṟivu mutirappeṟṟavarkaḷukkē yām iṅkē kūṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷiṉ mikuti pulappaṭā niṟkum. tāmirukkum iṭattiyaṉṟi vēṟetaṉiyum evvāṟṟāṉum aṟintu koḷḷātavarkku ivvuṇmi oru tiṉittuṇiyum pulappaṭātu. avarkaḷ kiṇaṟṟut tavaḷiyiyē oppar. avarkkut tāmirukkum nāṭē oppuyarvaṟṟatu; vēṟu ciṟanta nāṭukaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟintōr colliṉum, avar ataṉi ēṟṟukkoḷḷamāṭṭār; orukāl ēṟṟuk koṇṭālum avi makkaḷum piṟavuyirkaḷum illāta vaṟaṭṭu nilamākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉippar; aṉṟi ētō oru vakiyāy piṟa nāṭukaḷilum makkaḷum maṟṟi uyirkaḷum irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇa nērntālum, am makkaḷ mirukatti ottavarāy iruppārkaḷē yallāmal tammippōl nākarika vāḻkki uṭiyavarāy irukkakkūṭum eṉṟu avar niṉikkamāṭṭār. piṉṉar orukāl tamakkuḷḷa ciṟitu nalviṉi vayattāl, maṟṟi māntarum ciṟitu nākarika muṭiyavartām eṉṟu karuta nērntālum, avarkaḷ tammiṉum aḻakiṟ kuṟintavarē yāvareṉavum, avarkaḷuṭiya naṭi yuṭi pāvaṉikaḷ tammiṉum mikat tāḻntaṉavē yāku meṉavum, avarkaḷ kikkoṇṭuḷḷa camayak koḷkikaḷ payaṉaṟṟa veṟum poykaḷēyāmeṉavum, avarkaḷ maṟumiyil narakattiyē aṭivareṉavum, tāmum tam camayamum tam teyvamumē uyarntaṉavā mātalāl tām maṭṭumē maṟumiyil tuṟakka vāḻvaṭivatu tiṇṇameṉavum kūṟic cerukkuvar. tammuṭiya aṟivum aṉupavamum uyarntaṉavō eṉṟu oppiṭṭu ārāyntu pārttaṟkum avar oru ciṟitum oruppaṭamāṭṭār. iṉṉa taṉmi vāyntavar, ayalnāṭṭavarkku avar tam mata iyalpukaḷukkiyinta tuṟakka naraka ulakaṅkaḷum maṟumiyil uḷavāy irukkumeṉṟu eḷḷaḷavum uṇara māṭṭār. ākavē, avviyalpiṉari viṭuttu virinta nōkkamum tammōṭu piṟari oppiṭṭup pārkkum ārāycciyum vāyntavarik kuṟittē ivvuṇmiyi iṅku variyappōkiṉṟām.
mēlē kāṭṭiyavāṟu palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa nāṭukaḷum āṅkāṅkup palvēṟu vakiyāṉa māntarkaḷum in nilavula kattil iruttalpōlavē, itaṉik kaṭantuḷḷa eṇṇiṟanta kōṭi yulakaṅkaḷilum avaravar taṉmikkum camayakkoḷ kikkum ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷ aḷavillāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa māntarkaḷ in nilavulakatti viṭṭu vēṟu mēluḷḷa ulakaṅkaḷukkuc celvatiyē iṟattal eṉṟu collukiṟōm. tamiḻil iṟattal eṉṉuñ collukkup poruḷ kaṭattal allatu ōriṭatti viṭṭu vēṟōr iṭattiṟkup pōtal eṉpatēyām. inta nilamāṉatu taṉṉōṭotta poruḷkaḷit taṉpāl iḻuttuk koḷḷum āṟṟalvāyntatāy iruttaliṉ, nām iruttaṟku iṭamāyuḷḷa inta nam maṇ uṭampōṭu kūṭi nām mēl ulakaṅkaḷukkuc cella iyalātavarkaḷāyirukkiṟōm. inta maṇṇuṭampu parupporuḷāyi ruttalil itu taṉpōṟ parupporuḷāyuḷḷa in nilattāl iḻuttuk koḷḷap paṭukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu ivvuṭampu in nilattik kaṭantu mēṟcellamāṭṭāmip paṟṟiyē, itaṉuḷḷirunta uyirtāṉ mēṟ cellutaṟku icinta nuṇṇiya vuṭampōṭu kūṭi itaṉi viṭṭup pōkiṉṟatu. itaṉiyē maraṇam eṉṟum cāvu eṉṟum iṟappu eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṟārkaḷ. iṉi veḷḷikkārar tām vacikkum nāṭṭiṉkaṇ ōr ūriliruntu piṟitōr ūrukkuc celvatupōlavum, nam intiyar tām vacikkum ivvintiya nāṭṭiṉkaṇ ōr ūriliruntu maṟṟōr ūrukkup pōvatu pōlavum ic cātiyār tamakku uṟiviṭamāyuḷḷa in nilavulakattik kaṭantu celluṅkālum tattam iyalpukku otta mēlulakaṅkaḷukkē ēkukiṉṟārkaḷ. iṉi ūkkamum uḻippum mikuntu aṟivāṟ ciṟantavarkaḷ tām vacikkum nāṭukaḷiṉ elliyuṅ kaṭantu toliviluḷḷa ēṉi nāṭukaḷukkuñ celvatupōla, mēlulakaṅkaḷilum ōrulakattiṟkuriyavarkaḷiṟ cilar ēṉiyōr ulakattiṟkuñ celvatuṇṭu. āyiṉum, iḵtu iṅku nikaḻvatik kāṭṭiṉum mēlukaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟupāṉmiyē nikaḻukiṉṟa teṉpatu āvi vaṭivaṅkaḷil niṟpōr kūṟum urikaḷik koṇṭu tuṇiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. veḷḷikkārar āvi vaṭivaṅkaḷ tām ulavum ulakaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum, tammiṉattavarip paṟṟiyum tām toḻutukoṇṭu vanta teyvattip paṟṟiyum kūṟukiṉṟaṉavē yallāmal, vēṟu nāṭṭavarāṉa nam intiyariṉ āvikaḷip paṟṟiyum avi yulavum maṇṭilaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum avar vaḻipaṭṭa teyvaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum urippak kāṇkiṉṟilēm. ip paratakaṇṭa vācikaḷāṉa nammavar āvikaḷum aṅṅaṉamē tammulakaṅkaḷip paṟṟip pēcak kaṇṭaṉamē yallāmal, vēṟu ayalnāṭṭavar aṉupavaṅkaḷiyum nilikaḷiyum paṟṟi oru ciṟitum urippak kāṇkiṉṟilēm. ākavē, im maṇṇulakattil ōriṭattiruntu maṟṟōriṭattiṟkuc celvatik kāṭṭilum, mēlē oruvaki yulakaṅkaḷil uṟipavar piṟitoruvaki yulakaṅkaḷukkuc celvatu arumiyāka variyaṟukkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭu meṉṉum uṇmi uyttuṇarappaṭukiṉṟatu. iḵtu ētu kāraṇattāl eṉṟāl, ippōtu oru nāṭṭil uṟipavarkaḷ vēṟu oru nāṭṭil uṟipavarkaḷit tammiṉuṅ kīḻppaṭṭavarāka niṉittalālum, oruvar camayak koḷkikaḷiyum teyvattiyum ulaka naṭikaḷiyum maṟṟavar poruntātaṉa veṉṟum poyyeṉṟuṅ karututalālum avar ciṟupāṉmi aṟivum paḻakkamum uṭiyavarā yirukkiṉṟaṉar. inta nilimiyiluḷḷa ivarkaḷ mēlulakaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟaviṭattu aṅkum ic ciṟṟaṟivum ciṟu paḻakkamumē mēṟpaṭṭavarā yiruttalāl, avarkku ēṉi yulakaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum aṅkuḷḷa āvikaḷip paṟṟiyum terintukoḷḷum āṟṟal illāmaṟ pōkiṉṟatu. ellā vuyirkaḷum aṟivu uṭiyaṉavē yāmeṉṟu, tam aṟivi maṟitta aṟiyāmi nīkkattiṟku ēṟpa avi palavaki yuṭampukaḷiṟ pukumāṟu cērppittu avaṟṟi oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ēṟṟamāṉa piṟavikaḷiṉ mēṟkoṇṭu cellum iṟivaṉ oruvaṉē yeṉṟum, makkaḷ tamatu palvēṟu vakiyāṉa taraṅkaḷukku ēṟpa palavaṟṟit teyvamāka vaṇaṅkuvarāyiṉum avaṟṟiṉkaṇ ṇellām niṉṟu avar ceyyum vaḻipāṭukaḷi ēṉṟukoṇṭu avar tam takutikkēṟṟapaṭi yellām aruḷ vaḻaṅkuvāṉ avviṟivaṉ oruvaṉē yallatu vēṟu piṟar illi yeṉṟum, avviṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷip peṟutaṟ kuriyarāṉa ellāvuyir kaḷum ōriṉamātalāl avar tamakkuḷ iruviṉi vayattāl nērnta vēṟupāṭukaḷi nōkkātu orē tantikkup putalvarām iyipi niṉintu uḷam uruka nēcittu oruvark koruvar utaviyāy niṉṟu oḻukutalē ceyaṟpāla teṉṟum, niliyillāta ivvulaka vāḻkkiyil viṉi vayattāṟ piṟanta niliyillāta vēṟupāṭukaḷiyē periyaṉavākak karuti oruvari yoruvar maṉam vākkuk kāyaṅkaḷāl oru ciṟitēṉum tāḻtta muyalutal peruṅ kuṟṟamāmeṉṟum iṅṅaṉamellām virinta nōkkattōṭum pēraṟivōṭum mikak kuḻinta pēraṉpōṭum tamatuyiri im maṇṇulakil irukkiyilēyē tūyatākkik koṇṭa cāṉṟōr cilar māttiramē itaṉik kaṭantu mēlulakaṅ kaḷukkuc ceṉṟa vaḻiyum ellām valla oru pēr iṟivaṉi nēr mukamākak kāṇappeṟṟu ellā maṇṭilaṅ kaḷiyum ammaṇṭilaṅkaḷil ulavum ellā vakiyāṉa āvi vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum kāṇkuvar. ēṉiyōrkku ivvarum perum pēṟu vāyātu. itu niṟka.
19. vēṟu pala ulakaṅkaḷ

iṉi innilavulakattit tavira vēṟu pala ulakaṅkaḷ uṇṭeṉpataṟkup piramāṇam eṉṉi yeṉiṉ; muṟkālattil varāka mikirar mutalāṉa vāṉanūl vallār eḻutiya uṇmikaḷum, ikkālattu vāṉanūṟ pulavarkaḷ putiya putiyavāka ārāynturikkum meyp poruḷkaḷumē cāṉṟām. namakkup pakalil veyil veḷiccattiyum iravil nilavu veḷiccattiyum tarum cūriyamaṇṭila cantiramaṇṭila iyaṟkikaḷic ciṟitu āḻntu cintittup pārppavarkku vāṉiṟ cuḻalum ulakaṅkaḷiṉ taṉmi ciṟitēṉum teriyāmaṟ pōkātu. nam kaṭpulaṉukku nāṭōṟum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum ivviraṇṭiviṭa iṉṉum ettaṉiyō maṭaṅku periyaṉa vāṉa ulakaṅkaḷ paṟpala iruppiṉum avi maṉattiṟkum eṭṭāt toli tūrattil ulavutalāl avikaḷ nam ūṉak kaṇkaḷukkut teṉpaṭuvatilli; iruṭkālattiravil vāṉattil iṟikkappaṭṭa muttukkaḷ pōṟ ṟōṉṟum vāṉ mīṉkaḷil ic cūriya cantira maṇṭilaṅkaḷip pārkkilum evvaḷavō paṅku periyaṉavāyuḷḷa ulakaṅkaḷ kaṇakkil lāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟip paṟṟip piṉṉē pēcuvōm. aviyellām cūriya cantira maṇṭilaṅ kaḷiviṭa neṭuntūraṅkaḷiṟ cellutalāl, nammuṭiya kaṇkaḷukku muttukkaḷip pōl avvaḷavu ciṟiyaṉavāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. cūriya cantira maṇṭilaṅkaḷō namatu maṇṇulakatti viṭa mikap periyaṉavāy iruttalōṭu, maṟṟi vāṉ mīṉkaḷiviṭa arukiluñ cuḻalutalāl avi nam viḻikaḷukkut teḷivāyp pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
20. cūriyamaṇṭila iyaṟki
cūriya maṇṭilam namatu maṇṇulakattiṟku oṉpatu kōṭiyē muppatu laṭcam mil tūrattil uḷḷateṉṟu iññāṉṟi vāṉanūṟ pulavar kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. oru mil allatu iraṇṭu mil uyaramuḷḷa oru maliyiṉ ucciyil ōṅki vaḷarntirukkum ōr ālamaram kīḻiruntu nōkkum nammaṉōr kaṇkaḷukku evvaḷavu ciṟiyatākavum am maliyucciyil ēṟip pārppavarkku evvaḷavu periyatākavun tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. iṉi irupatu mil allatu muppatu mil uyaramuḷḷa oru malimēl av vālamaram iruppatāka vittuk koḷvōmāyiṉ atu kīḻiruntu nōkkum nam kaṇkaḷukkuc ciṟitum pulappaṭāteṉpatu evarum uṇarva raṉṟō? aṉṟip pulappaṭumeṉṟē eṇṇuvōmāyiṉum, aḵtu ataṟkut takka parimāṇam uṭiyatāy namakkut terintuḷḷa ālamaraṅkaḷiviṭa ettaṉiyō paṅku periyatāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu uṇarvōmaṉṟō? irupatu muppatu mil tūrattiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷē mikavum periyaṉavā yirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu namakkut tōṉṟu māṉāl, nūṟumil, āyira mil, patiṉāyira mil, oru laṭca mil, pattu laṭcam mil, oru kōṭi mil tūraṅkaḷiliruntu nam viḻikaḷukkut tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷ varavara evvet taṉi maṭaṅku periyaṉavā yirukkavēṇṭumeṉpati nām viṇṭu coṟṟalum vēṇṭumō? cūriyamaṇṭilamō oṉpatu kōṭiyē muppatu laṭcam mil tūrattiliruntum nammuṭiya kaṇkaḷukkuc cevvaṉē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum peruvaṭivam vāyntatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, atu nammuṭiya kaṇkaḷukkut tōṉṟukiṟapaṭi yaṉṟit taṉṉiyaṟkiyil mikap periyatoru kōḷamāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum uṇmi iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu; ataṉ aḷaviṉik kaṇakkiṭṭup pārtta vāṉanūl vallār aḵtu in nilavulakattiviṭa oru kōṭiyē iraṇṭu laṭcam maṭaṅku periyateṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. itaṉi iṉṉun teḷivāyk kāṇal vēṇṭiṉ, cūriyaṉi  iraṇṭaṭi yuyara muḷḷa oru pantākavum im maṇṇulakatti oru paṭṭāṇik kaṭaliyākavum pāvittu av viraṇṭiṉiyum oppiṭṭup pārttāl cūriyaṉuṭiya pēraḷavum inta nilattiṉ ciṟṟaḷavum karuttil naṉṟāyp patiyum. iṅkiruntu pārppavarkkuc cūriyaṉ naṉku pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟutalpōlac cūriya maṇṭilattiliruntu nōkkuvārkku in nilavulakam oru kaṭukaḷavu kūṭat tōṉṟātu. iṉic cūriya maṇṭilam aḷaviṭappaṭāta aḻal āviyāṟ cūḻappaṭṭirut taliṉ atu pēr oḷip piṇṭamāyt tikaḻkiṉṟateṉṟum, atiṟ pallāyira mil akala nikaḷamuḷḷa karumiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum iññāṉṟi vāṉanūlvallār tolivu nōkkik kaṇṇāṭi* (Telescope) yiṉ utavikoṇṭu terintu eḻutukiṟārkaḷ. atu mikavum neṭun tūrattiluḷḷa tākiyāl ataṉ taṉmikaḷiyum aṅkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyum virivākavum nuṇukkamākavun terintu koḷḷak kūṭavilli. eṉṟālum, vāṉanūl ārāycciyil mikavum pukaḻ peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya āṉṟōrāṉa herṣal* (Sir John Herschel) eṉpavar in nilavulakattuḷḷa paruvuṭampōṭu kūṭiya uyirkaḷ uyirvāḻtaṟku ēlāta attuṇipperiya aḻal nirampiya iyaṟkiyuṭiyatu cūriya maṇṭilamāyiṉum am maṇṭilat tiṉ iyalpukku otta uṭampōṭu kūṭiya uyirkaḷ aṅku vāḻumeṉpatu aṟiviṉ aṉupavattiṟku icinta tākum, eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṭa eḻutiyiruk kiṉṟārākaliṉ, ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattilum ataṟku icinta uṭampōṭulavum uyirkaḷ uṇṭeṉpatu tēṟṟamēyām. ivvuṇmi kāṇamāṭṭāta vāṉa nūṟ pulavariṟ palar ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattil uyirkaḷ illi eṉṟu tām avvuṇmiyi muṟṟun terintāṟpōṟ kūṟiṉār. mēlē kāṭṭiyavāṟu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉum ettaṉiyō paṅku mikac ciṟitākiya in nilavulakam uyirkaḷ uṟitaṟku iṭamāka amikkappaṭṭirukka, mikap peritāṉa cūriyavulakam māttiram uyir vāḻkkiyillāta veṟṟiṭamāy amintirukkiṟa teṉṉum uri aṟivukkum aṉupavattiṟkum poruntiya tāyilli. mēlum, makkaḷuṭampu nilipeṟa muṭiyāta koḻuntu viṭṭeriyum tī naṭuvil ētum varutta miṉṟi yirukkum cila pirāṇikaḷi iññāṉṟi uyirnūl* (Biology) vallār terinteṭutturikkak kāṇṭalāl, aḻal mayamāṉa ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattilum oḷimayamāṉa uṭampukaḷil uṟiyum uyirkaḷ uṇṭeṉpatu iṉitu peṟappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam oḷimayamāṉa nuṇṇiyavuṭampukaḷil uṟiyum uyirkaḷiyē tēvarkaḷ eṉṟu iṅkuḷḷōr vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. tēvaṉ eṉṉum collukkup poruḷ oḷivaṭiviṉaṉ eṉpatēyām. atukiṭakka.
21. cantiramaṇṭila iyaṟki
iṉic cantiramaṇṭilattippaṟṟi oru ciṟitu kūṟuvām. itu namatu nilavulakattiṟku iraṇṭu laṭcattu muppattēḻā yirattu munnūṟumil tūrattil uḷḷatu! itu cūriya maṇṭilattiviṭa namakku mikavuṅ kiṭṭa ulavukiṟateṉṟē collalām. ivaṟṟiṉ cēymi aṇmikaḷi iṉṉun teḷivākat terintu koḷḷa virumpuvārkku maṟṟumoru vaḻi kāṭṭuvām. orumaṇi nērattiṟku muppatumil tūram mēlē cellakkūṭiya oru vāṉavūrtiyil oruvar allum pakalum ōyātu pirayāṇañ ceyyakkūṭumāyiṉ avar cūriya maṇṭilattiṟ pōyc cērac ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya munnūṟṟim pattu nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ ākum. iṉi, avar atē ūrtiyil aṅṅaṉamē cantiramaṇṭilattiṟkuc cellakkūṭumāyiṉ caṟṟēṟattāḻa ōr āṇṭil aṅkē pōyc cēralām. ip pirayāṇak kāla aḷaviṉik koṇṭu namatulakattiṟku ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilam evvaḷavu cēymiyilirukkiṟa teṉṟum tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilam ataṉiṉum namakku evvaḷavu aṇmiyili rukkiṟateṉṟum naṉku pakuttaṟintu koḷḷalām. iṉi, namatu nilavulakam ciṟitu kūṭakkuṟiya eṇṇāyira mil kuṟukkaḷa vuḷḷatāyirukkat, tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilamō iraṇṭā yirattu irunūṟu mil māttiramē kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷatākak kaṇakkiṭap paṭṭiruttalāl, atu nilavulakatti viṭac ciṟiyateṉṟum ataṉāl atu nilattiṉ āṟṟalāl iḻukkappaṭṭu nilavulakattic cuṟṟic cuḻalukiṟateṉṟum iṉitaṟintiruk kiṉṟōm. iṉit tolivu nōkkik kaṇṇāṭiyiṉ utavikoṇṭu nōkkum vāṉanūṟpulavar tiṅkaḷ maṇṭi lattil āyirattukku mēl malikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉkiṉṟaṉar. inta malikaḷē nammuṭiya veṟuṅ kaṇkaḷukkuk kaṟikaṟiyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum itiṟ pāli nilaṅkaḷ pala im maṇṇulakattil iruppaṉa pōl irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvulakattil āṅkāṅku erimalikaḷ iruttalpōlat tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilattilum periya periya erimalikaḷum, erimali vāykaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iviyē yaṉṟip perumpaḷḷattākkukaḷum, malippiḷappu kaḷum, maliyaruvikaḷumkūṭa atiṟ ṟeṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. periya erimali vāykaḷiliruntu veḷḷikkampi pōṉṟa oḷi vaṭivaṅkaḷ kiḷampi neṭuntūram cellukiṉṟaṉa; āṉālum ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmi iṉṉateṉṟu viḷaṅkavilli. pacci niṟamāṉa kaṭalkaḷuṅkūṭa aṅkirukkiṉṟaṉa. uyirkaḷ uyir vāḻtaṟku vēṇṭum veppamum aṅkuḷḷatākat terinturik kappaṭutalāl* (cantira maṇṭilattiṟku 1971il ceṉṟuvanta amerikka viṇveḷi aṟiñarkaḷ aṅku uyirkaḷ illi eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar) cantira maṇṭilattilum uyirkaḷ uṇṭeṉpatu uyttuṇarap paṭuvatākum. atu niṟka.
22. cevvāy maṇṭila iyaṟki
iṉic cevvāy maṇṭilattippaṟṟic ciṟitu pēcuvām. namatu nilavulakattiṟkuc cantiramaṇṭilam mika arukil iruppatupōl illāviṭṭālum, cūriyaṉiviṭa namakkuk kiṭṭattiliruppatum perumpālum in nilavulakatti ottiruppatum cevvāy maṇṭilamē tāṉ. itu cūriyaṉic cuṟṟic cellum vāṉvaḻi nērvaṭṭamāy illāmal kōḻi muṭṭi vaṭiviṉatāy iruttaliṉ ītu iraṇṭu āṇṭiṟku orumuṟi mūṉṟarikkōṭi mil tūrattil in nilattiṟku aṇmiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu; maṟṟik kālattil itaṟku āṟukōṭiyē pattulaṭcam mil tūram akaṉṟu pōkiṉṟa tākaliṉ appōtu atu cevviyākak kāṇap paṭuva tilli. iṉi iññāṉṟi vāṉanūl āciriyarkaḷ nirampac ciṟanta tolivu nōkkik kaṇṇāṭikaḷāl nōkkic cevvāy maṇṭilattiṟ cemmaṇ nilamum pacci niṟamuḷḷa kaṭalkaḷum amintuḷḷaṉaveṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa maṇ civappā yiruttaliṉāṟṟāṉ, aḵtu irāk kālattil cevveṉṟa oḷiyi vīcukiṉṟatu; itu paṟṟiyē tamiḻilum cevvāy eṉṉum peyar ataṟku vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. iṉi, in nilavulakattiṉ vaṭakku muṉiyāṉa uttara turuvamum teṟku muṉiyāṉa taṭciṇa turuvamum kuḷirmikunta iṭaṅkaḷākaliṉ, aṅkēyuḷḷa nilamuṅ kaṭalum pallāyira mil tūram veṇmiyāṉa paṉippāṟikaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; itippōlavē cevvāy maṇṭilattiṉ vaṭamuṉi, teṉmuṉi iraṇṭilum āyirattu irunūṟu mil tūram variyil tūveḷḷiyāṉa paṉikkaṭṭi niṟintu paraviyirukkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam paravi yiruppatu paṉikkaṭṭitāṉ eṉṟu evvāṟu uṟutippaṭuttak kūṭumeṉiṉ; in nilavulakattiṉ av viraṇṭu muṉikaḷilum vēṉiṟkāla veppam mikumpōtu aṅkuḷḷa paṉippāṟikaḷ urukic curuṅkutal pōlavum, maṟittum kuḷirkālattuk kuḷir mikumpōtu ap paṉip pāṟikaḷ mikap peruttup paravutal pōlavum cevvāy maṇṭilattiṉ av viraṇṭu muṉi kaḷilum vēṉiṟ kālattil av veṇpaṉip piṇṭam ciṟuka vum, kuḷirkālattil atu perukavuṅ kāṇkiṉṟōm. iṉṉum ac cevvāy maṇṭilattil oṉṟiyoṉṟu ūṭaṟuttuc cellum akala nikaḷattil mikap periya periya yāṟukaḷ paṟpala ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. iv yāṟukaḷ ataṉ vaṭamuṉi teṉ muṉi variyil āṅkāṅkuḷḷa paṉippāṟikaḷōṭu toṭar puṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa; ap paṉippāṟikaḷ urukuṅkālattu av yāṟukaḷ nīr nirampic cevvāy maṇṭilattiṉ mēṟ parap peṅkum kuṟukkumaṟukkumāyp pāyntu ataṉ nilatti vaḷampaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa; ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu yāṟukaḷ iraṭṭi yāka neṭuka ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟṟāl, paṉikkaṭṭiyum nīrum yāṟum nilaṉum kaṭalum kālamum iṅkuḷḷatu pōlavē, cevvāy maṇṭilattilum amintirukkum uṇmi naṉku teḷiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṉṉum uyarnta malikaḷ palavum am maṇṭilattiṟ ṟōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. im maṇṭilam namatu nilavulakat tiṟ kāṟpaṅkutāṉ parimāṇam vāyntatu. itaṉkaṇ makkaḷi ṉuñ ciṟanta aṟivuṭiyarāṉa uyirkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉa reṉṟu ippōtu vāṉanūl ārāycci valla paṇṭitarkaḷ urik kiṉṟaṉar. atu kiṭakka.
23. putaṉ maṇṭila iyaṟki
iṉip putaṉ maṇṭilattippaṟṟi mikutiyāṉa vivaraṅkaḷ vāṉanūṟ pulavarāṟ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭavilli. atu namatu nilavulakattiṟku aintukōṭiyē muppatu laṭcam mil tūrattil cūriyamaṇṭilattiṟkum namatu maṇṭilattiṟkum iṭiyē cūriyaṉic cuṟṟippōkiṉṟa teṉṟum, namatu maṇṇulakatti viṭa atu ciṟiyatākumeṉṟum, atu taṉṉit tāṉē irupattintu maṇi nērattil cuṟṟuvatāl am maṇṭilattiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa orunāḷ namatu nāḷukkuc ciṟitēṟak kuṟiya ottatē yāmeṉṟum, aḵtu eṇpatteṭṭu nāṭkaḷil cūriyaṉic cuṟṟic cellutalāl aṅkuḷḷa ōr āṇṭu namatu āṇṭil nāṉkiloru pākamē ākiṉṟateṉṟum iñ ñāṉṟi vāṉanūṟ pulavarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. namatu maṇṇulakattiṉ niḻal cantiramaṇṭilattiṉmēṟpaṭuvatāl cantiraṉ vaḷarvatun tēyvatumpōṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamē putaṉ maṇṭilattiṉ mēlum ētō maṟṟoru maṇṭilattiṉ niḻal paṭuvataṉālē, atuvum vaḷarvatun tēyvatumpōṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. putaṉ maṇṭilattiṉ oḷi ciṟitu civappākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu; āṉāl ataṉ kāraṇam iṉṉateṉṟu pulappaṭavilli. paḻiya vāṉanūṟ pulavar oruvar putaṉmaṇṭilattil patiṉoru mil uyaramuḷḷa oru mali kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉak kūṟiyirukkiṟār. namatu nilamaṇṭilat tilirukkum malikaḷilellām mikap periyatu aintu mil uyarattiṟkumēl illi. putaṉ maṇṭilatti mēlē kavintirukkum āvi taṭippāṉatā yiruttalāl, iṅkiruntu nōkkuvārkku aṅkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ nili iṉṉa teṉṟu pulappaṭavilli. atu niṟka.
24. viyāḻa maṇṭila iyaṟki
iṉip pirukaṟpati ulakameṉap paṭuvatākiya viyāḻa maṇṭilam namatu nilavulakattiṟku aṇimiyiluḷḷa maṇṭilaṅ kaḷiṟ cūriyaṉit tavira ēṉi ellāvaṟṟiviṭa mikavum periyatākum. namatu maṇṇulakattip pōṉṟa āyirattu munnūṟu ulakaṅkaḷi oruṅku cērttut tiraṭṭiṉāl, aṅṅaṉan tiraṭṭappaṭṭa piṇṭam viyāḻa maṇṭilattiṉ aḷaviṉi oppatākum eṉa vāṉanūṟ pulavar oruvar kaṇakkiṭṭiruk kiṉṟār. itu cūriya maṇṭilattiṟku nāṟpat teṭṭukkōṭiyē muppatu laṭcam mil tūrattilum, namatu nilamaṇṭilattiṟku muppattoṉpatukōṭi mil tūrattilum cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟatu. iv viyāḻa maṇṭilattic cūḻa arik kacci pōṉṟa karukalāṉa paṭṭikkampi vaṭivaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa; ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu paṭṭikaḷ akalamākavum ivviraṇṭiṉ irupuṟattum uḷḷavi kuṟukalākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi ovvoṉṟum am maṇṭilattiṉ naṭuvē yōṭum varikkup pakkattē nērvaricikaḷākavē ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi eṉṉa veṉṟu ārāynta vāṉanūl āciriyar cilar im maṇṭilam taṭippāṉa mukiṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷāṟ cūḻappaṭṭirukkiṉṟa teṉavum, am mukilkaḷiṉ naṭuvēyuḷḷa piḷappukkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē tōṉṟum am maṇṭilattiṉ iṟukiya nilamē aṅṅaṉam kīṟṟuk kīṟṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉavum karutukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉic cūḻnta mukiṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ aṭikkaṭi nili māṟak kāṇṭalāl aṅkē perumpuyaṟ kāṟṟukkaḷ aṭikkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum uyttuṇarkiṉṟaṉar. puḷḷi vaṭivaṅkaḷ pala im maṇṭilat tiṉkaṇ aṭikkaṭi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 1878ām āṇṭiliruntu mikavuñ cenniṟamāṉa oru kaṟi vaṭivu pala āṇṭukaḷ variyil itiṟ kāṇappaṭṭu vantatu; ivvaṭivu irupattēḻāyiram mil nikaḷamum eṇṇāyiram mil akalamum vāyntatāyiruntatu; ataṉuruvam muṭṭiyi ottu viḷaṅkiṟṟu; nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ kāṟum ikkaṟiyiṉ cenniṟamum māṟāmalē tōṉṟi vantatu; ataṟkup piṟaku ataṉ vaṭivam appaṭiyēyirukka ataṉ niṟam māttiram māṟip pōyiṟṟu. āṉāl iv vaṭivattiṉ uṇmi yiṉṉateṉṟu pulappaṭa villi. iṉi, im maṇṭilattil ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭum kaṟi vaṭivaṅkaḷukkum, ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattil iṅṅaṉamē kāṇappaṭum kaṟi vaṭivaṅkaḷukkum ētō ōr oṟṟumi yirukkavēṇṭumeṉa vāṉanūlāciriyar karutukiṉṟaṉar; ivi oru maṇṭilattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiyil maṟṟiyatilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉi namatu nilamaṇṭilam oru nimiṭattukkup patiṉēḻu mil viḻukkāṭu taṉṉit tāṉē cuṟṟikkoṇṭupōka iv viyāḻa maṇṭilamāṉatu oru nimiṭattiṟku nāṉūṟṟu aṟupattāṟu mil viḻukkāṭu taṉṉit tāṉ cuṟṟik koṇṭu celkiṉṟatu. iḵtu iṅṅaṉam virintu cellutalāl im maṇṭilattil naṭuvari yōṭum iṭam pitukkamākavum vaṭamuṉi teṉmuṉikaḷ taṭṭi yākavum uruvamintirukkiṉṟaṉa. iḵtēṉ ittuṇi virivākac celkiṉṟateṉiṉ, itu ñāyiṟṟumaṇṭilattiṟku mikavum toliviliruttalāl aṅku niṉṟum namakku varum avvaḷavu veppam iv viyāḻa maṇṭilattuḷḷārkku varamāṭṭātu; ākavē, itu mikavuṅ kaṭuvirivōṭu cuḻalutali liruntu ak kuṟinta veppam niṟivu ceyyappaṭumāṟu mutalvaṉ aṅṅaṉam amippāṉāyiṉāṉ. iṉṉum iv viyāḻa maṇṭilam taṉṉiṭattiruntē vēṇṭumaḷavukku veppattip piṟappikka vallatā yiruttaliṉālēyē, aṅkuḷḷa nīr āviyāka māṟi mukiṟkūṭṭaṅkaḷ aṅkē uṇṭāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṉi ivviyāḻa maṇṭilattic cuṟṟi aintu cantira maṇṭilaṅkaḷ cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟaṉaveṉa iññāṉṟi vāṉanūṟ pulavar uyarnta tolivu nōkkik kaṇṇāṭiyāl kaṇṭurikkiṟārkaḷ. ivviyāḻa maṇṭilam pattu maṇi nērattil taṉṉit tāṉē cuṟṟuvatāl aṅkuḷḷārkkup pakaṟkālam aintumaṇi nēramum irākkālam aintumaṇi nēramumē yuḷḷateṉṟu aṟiyap paṭum; aintumaṇi nēramuḷḷa avirākkālamum aṭuttaṭuttu aintu cantira maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉāl viḷakkap paṭutaliṉ viyāḻa maṇṭilam eṉṟum oḷiyuṭiyatākavē viḷaṅkumeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. iṉi, itu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattic cuṟṟi varuvataṟkup paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ cellutaliṉ, namakkup paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭu koṇṭa oru kāla aḷavi viyāḻa maṇṭilat tuḷḷārkku ōr āṇṭu ākumeṉpatu aṟiyaṟ pālatām. itu kiṭakka.
25. veḷḷimaṇṭila iyaṟki
iṉic cukkiravulaka meṉappaṭum veḷḷi maṇṭilamum namatulakiṟkum cūriyaṉulakiṟkum iṭiyil iyaṅkuvatēyākum. itu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattic cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭu namatu nilavulaka mukamāy varukiyil itaṟkum namakkum iraṇṭu kōṭiyē aimpatulaṭcam mil tūram irukkiṉṟatu; itu nammi viṭṭuc cūriyaṉukku appakkamāyc cellum pōtu itaṟkum namakkum iṭiyil patiṉāṟukōṭi mil tūram ākiṉṟatu. ākiyāl, itu namatulakattiṉ pakkamāy varumpōtu itaṉi uṟṟup pārttu vivaraṅkaḷ terintu koḷvataṟku iṭamuṇṭāvatu paṟṟi, ac camayatti vāṉanūṟ pulavarkaḷ āvalōṭu nōkkukiṉṟārkaḷ. iv veḷḷi maṇṭilamāṉatu namatu maṇṇulakattiṉ parimāṇamē yuḷḷatu. tiṅkaḷ, putaṉ maṇṭilaṅkaḷil vaḷartal kuṟitalkaḷ kāṇap paṭuvatu pōlavē veḷḷi maṇṭilattilum avi kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. putaṉ maṇṭilattiviṭa veḷḷi maṇṭilattiṉ oḷi nirampavuṅ kiḷarcciyuḷḷa tāy oḷirukiṉṟatu. pala taṭavikaḷiṟ puḷḷikaḷum niḻalkaḷum veḷḷi maṇṭilattiṉ mēṟ ṟōṉṟiṉamiyāl avi aṅkuḷḷa malikaḷiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷā meṉṟu uyttuṇarap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. vāṉa nūṟ pulavar oruvar tām putaṉ maṇṭilattil mika uyaramāṉa maliyik kaṇṭatu pōlavē veḷḷi maṇṭilattilum attakiyavāṉa malikaḷ pala irukkak kāṇpatāka urikkiṉṟār. veḷḷi maṇṭilamum namatulakattip pōlavē caṟṟu ēṟattāḻa irupattunāṉku maṇi nērattil taṉṉit tāṉē cuṟṟukiṉṟatu; ēḻari mātattil ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattic cuṟṟivarukiṉṟatu. iv veḷḷimaṇṭilattiṉ teṉmuṉiyil oru puḷḷi veḷiccam aṭikkaṭi kāṇap paṭutalāl, aḵtu aṅkē mika uyarntuḷḷa mali mukaṭṭiṉ mēṟ paṭum ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷitāṉ eṉṟu uṟutip paṭuttap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. im maṇṭilattiṉ vaṭamuṉi teṉmuṉi iraṇṭum orē kālattiṟ kaṭpulaṉā kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, am muṉikaḷiṟ perum paṉippāṟikaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum maṟṟoru ciṟanta vāṉanūṟ pulavar kūṟutalāl, nammulakattum cevvāy maṇṭilattum uḷḷa vaṭaturuva teṉturuva iyaṟkikaḷē veḷḷiyulakattum uṇṭeṉpatu teḷiyap paṭum. ivvāṟṟal veḷḷi maṇṭilattil avvulakattiṟku iyinta uṭampōṭu kūṭiya uyirkaḷ uṇṭeṉpatum tuṇiyappaṭum. itu niṟka.
26. caṉimaṇṭila iyaṟki
iṉic caṉimaṇṭilattippaṟṟic ciṟitu kūṟuvām. collutaṟ kaṭaṅkāp pēr aḻakum pēroḷi viḷakkamum vāyntu kāṇappaṭum caṉimaṇṭilak kāṭci pēr aṟputam uṭiyatāy irukkiṉṟatu. itu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṟku eṇpatteṭṭuk kōṭiyē aṟupatu laṭcam mil tūrattilum, namatu nilavulakattiṟku eḻupattoṉpatu kōṭiyē muppatulaṭcam mil tūrattilum cuḻaluva toṉṟākiyāl, ittuṇi neṭun tūrattiliruntu nōkkum nam veṟuṅ kaṇkaḷukku aḵtu oru ciṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṉatākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. āṉāl mikap periyatum ciṟantatumākiya oru tolivunōkkik kaṇṇāṭiyiṉ vaḻi itaṉi nōkkuvārkku itu pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟum aṟputak kāṭciyi eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟuvēm! im maṇṭilam naṭuvil ōr uruṇṭi vaṭivamum, avvuruṇṭiyil mōtiram iṭṭāṟ pōṉṟa oru vaḷiya vaṭivamum amintu mikunta oḷiyōṭum tulaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉic cōtiyuruvāṉa oru civaliṅka vaṭivamākavē collalām. im maṇṭilattiṉ oḷiyiṉiyum vaṭiviṉiyum nōkkiya vāṉa nūlāciriyar evarum itaṉi viyantu pukaḻntu pēcāmaṟ pōvatilli. iṉi, mēleṭuttup pēciya viyāḻamaṇṭilattiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa arikkaccup pōṉṟa varivaṭivaṅkaḷ ic caṉimaṇṭilattiṉkaṇṇum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa;
