iṉi, mēṟcolliyavāṟāyk kīḻnōkkit taṭavutalum mēlnōkkit taṭavutalum muṟiyē oruvarkku aṟituyili viḷivittalum, piṉṉar ataṉiṉiṉṟu avari eḻupputalum yāṅṅaṉam ceykiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉ; avviyalpiṉiyum oru ciṟitu viḷakkuvām.ellāp piṟavikaḷilum uyir uṭampōṭu kūṭiyirukkuṅkāl, aḵtu avvuṭampil iḷippāṟat tuyilkoḷḷum iṭamum, tuyiṉṟu iḷipput tīrntapiṉ aṟivōṭiruntu muyaṟciceyyum iṭamum eṉa uyirkku iruvakiyiṭaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. kaḻuttiṉ kīḻiruntu kuṟivariyiṟ cellum nāṭiyiṉ pakutiyē uyir tuyiṉṟu iḷippāṟum iṭamām; kaḻuttiṟkumēl talivariyil amintirukkum annāṭiyiṉ mēṟpakutiyāṉa mūḷiyē aḵtu aṟivōṭiruntu muyaṟciceyyum iṭamām. atu taliyip paṟṟikkoṇṭu aṟivum muyaṟciyum uṭiyatāy niṟkuṅkāl, taliyum uṭalum nimirnta paṭiyāyirukkum. aṟivum muyaṟciyum viṭṭu atu kīḻ iṟaṅkit tuyilkoḷḷap pōkuṅkāl taliyum uṭalum nimirntirukkamāṭṭāmal nilattē kiṭiyāyk kiṭantu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṭampiṉ ivviruvaki nilikaḷum nammaṉōr kaṇkaṭketirē nāṭōṟum nikaḻak kāṇṭalāl, uyir taliyiviṭṭu iṟaṅkit tuyilumiṭam kaḻuttiṉkīḻ neñciliruntu kuṟiyiṉ aṭivariyil ām eṉṟu nām uṇarap peṟukiṉṟēm. oruvari aṟituyiliṟ celutta vēṇṭum maṟṟoruvar avaratu taliyiliruntu tamkikaḷāṟ kīḻnōkkit taṭaviya aḷavilē celuttuvōr niṉiviṉ vaḻiyē viralkaḷi liruntu pāyum miṉ āṟṟal avartam uyiri mūḷiyi ṉiṉṟu pirittuk kīḻē neñcattiṉkaṭ ṭuyil koḷḷumāṟu ēvukiṉṟatu. celuttuvōr ataṉpiṉ tam kikaḷi mēṉmukamāyt tiruppi avari eḻuppiviṭuṅ kuṟippōṭu mēlnōkkit taṭavavē aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟōr uyir neñcatti viṭṭu mēlēṟi mūḷiyip paṟṟikkoṇṭu viḻittu aṟivum muyaṟciyum uṭiyatākiṉṟatu. ikkālattu uṭampu nūlvallārum uyirkku iruppiṭam kaḻuttiṉkīḻk kuṟivariyiṟ cellum nāṭiyiṉ akamēyeṉṟu ārāyn turikkiṉṟār. uṇmiyivvāṟākaliṉ, taṭavutalukkum aṟituyilukkum uḷḷa iyipu itukoṇṭu naṉku teḷiyaṟ pālatām eṉka.
iṉi, iṭaṅkaḷit taṭavutal eṉṟavakiyil maṟṟoru muṟiyum paḻakikkoḷḷaṟ pālatāyiruttaliṉ ataṉiyum ciṟitu viḷakkuvām. uṭampiṉkaṇ ētēṉum ōr iṭattiṟku miṉāṟṟal ēṟaṟalvēṇṭumēṉum, allatu aṅkuḷḷa nañciṉi iḻuttu nōy akaṟṟa vēṇṭumēṉum avviṭattiṟku nērē viralkaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu paṭāmal nērākappiṭittu, avviṭattiṉmēṟ paṭāmal avaṟṟi oruviraṟkaṭi akala niṟutti, niṉivi orumukappaṭutti, ataṉāl untappaṭum miṉ āṟṟali avviral nuṉikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē pāyacceyka. aṅṅaṉam ceyyumpōtu avviral kaḷi naṭuṅkumāṟu acittalvēṇṭum. appaṭic ceytalāl anta miṉāṟṟal avviral nuṉikaḷil iṟaṅkic cevvaṉē pāyum. itaṉōṭu avviral nuṉikaḷiṉ mukamāy mēliruntu kīḻ nōkkiyapaṭiyāy vāyāl kāṟṟi ūtutalum ceytāl am miṉāṟṟal mikavuñ cevvaṉē pāyum. atu niṟka.
mēṟcolliyavāṟāy nēṇāyuṟṟa iṭattiṟ kiviralkaḷi nērē piṭittu aṅku miṉṉāṟṟalip pāycciyapiṉ, tūya melliya tuṇiyi nāṉkāy maṭittu avviṭattiṉ mēl vittu, ataṉmēl vāyivittu vetuvetuppu uṇṭākum paṭi ūtutal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceytalāl, avviṭattiṟ pāynta miṉṉāṟṟal atil naṉṟāyp paravi aṅkuḷḷa piṇiyi virivil akaṟṟum eṉka.
9. kaṭṭurittal
iṉi, mēlēkāṭṭiya muṟikaḷ ellām karutiya payaṉit tarutaṟku utaviyāka avaṟṟōṭu cērttuc ceyaṟpālatākiya maṟṟōr iṉṟiyamiyāta muṟiyum irukkiṉṟatu. aṟituyili varuvippōr ataṉi varuvittaṟkēṟṟa coṟkaḷit tiṟami yāy vaṟpuṟuttic collappaḻakutal peritum vēṇṭaṟ pālatoṉṟām. aṟituyiliṟ celvārkku ataṉi varuvippōr iṅṅaṉam vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutaliyē kaṭṭurittal (Suggestion) eṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. oru collip palakāl niṉivu muṉippōṭu tiruppit tiruppic coṉṉāl ataṉi uṟṟuk kēṭpōr accolliṉpaṭiyē naṭappar. ivvuṇmiyi maṉanūl ārāycciyil tērcci peṟṟa aṟiñarkaḷ cevviyāy āyntu pārttu nilināṭṭi yirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
tali nōyāl varuntum oruvarkku annōy nīkkal vēṇṭiṉ, avari vacatiyāka oru cāymāṉakkuṟicciyil amara vittu, nōy nīkkum tammuṭiya kaṇkaḷi avar uṟṟup pārtta paṭiyāyt tām collum coṟkaḷik karuttāyk kēṭkumpaṭi kaṟpittu `umakkut talivali illi, atu nummi viṭṭu akaṉṟatu, ippōtu nīr cemmiyāy irukkiṉṟīr, umatu maṇṭi nalla nilimiyilirukkiṉṟatu. eṉṟu iccoṟṟoṭar kaḷit tiruppit tiruppic collic ciṟitunērañ ceṉṟapiṉ avaratu taliyit toṭṭut taṭavi eḻuppi viṭṭāl, avarkkirunta ammaṇṭi vali uṭaṉē vilakippōtalip pārkkalām. niṉiviṉ vaṉmiyōṭu oruvar allatu palar aṭuttaṭuttuc colluñcoṟkaḷ poyyāy iruppiṉum avi piṟarāl nampappaṭutali muṉṉēyum viḷakki yirukkiṉṟōm. ulakattiṟ perumpālum naṭipeṟuñ ceyalkaḷellām piṟar colluvaṉavaṟṟi namputalālēyē nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. piṟarpāl nampikkiyi varuvittaṟkēṟṟa vallami cilariṭattu - avar kaṟṟavarā yiruppiṉum kallātavarāyiruppiṉum aṟivuṭiyōrāyiṉum atiṟ kuṟintavarāyiṉum, amintiruntamiyāl avar  colliya coṟkaḷē caṭṭamāka nampappaṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ payaṉāṉa ceyalkaḷē eṅkuṅ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. payaṉṟarum coṟkaḷi nampi naṭattal paḻutaṉṟu. payaṉṟarātavaṟṟiyum tīṅku payappaṉavaṟṟiyum nampinaṭattalē kuṟṟamām. kaṭṭurittuc collavalla cilarāṟ paraviya oru tītāṉa nampikkiyit tolippateṉṟāl aḵtu eḷitil muṭivatilli. orukāl oru peṇpiḷḷiyiṉ paṟkaḷil oṉṟu āṭṭam eṭuttu avaṭku mikunta nōyi uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu.
pal maruttuvattil tērnta oruvariṭattil avaḷ ataṉic ceṉṟukāṭṭa, avar antap pallip piṭuṅkiviṭat tīrmāṉittu, ataṉit terivittāl avaḷ ataṟku iciyāḷ eṉpatit terintukoṇṭu, avaḷi aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu nōy teriyāmal ataṉip piṭuṅki eṭuttuviṭṭār. piṟaku avaḷiyum eḻupviviṭṭu uṇmiyic collāmal `iṉi uṅkaṭkup palvali irātu eṉṟu maṭṭum colli avaḷi aṉuppiṉār. avaḷum vīṭuceṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḻintapiṉ kaṇṇāṭiyil taṉvāyit tiṟantu pārkka aṅkē vali uṇṭāṉa pakkattil oru pallik kāṇāḷ āyiṉāḷ. ataṉmēl arukilirunta oruvarkku ataṉit terivikka avar uṇmi terintu koḷḷāmal `āṭṭaṅkaṇṭa palli nī uṇaveṭukkumpōtu uṇavōṭu cērttu viḻuṅki yirukka vēṇṭum. appaṭi viḻuṅkiyiruntāl atu vayiṟṟu valiyi uṇṭupaṇṇumē!   eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. avvāṟu colliya coṟkaḷi avaḷ uṇmi yeṉṟu nampiya ciṟitu nērattilellām, avaḷ taṉ vayiṟṟil nōy uṇṭākak kaṇṭāḷ. avaḷ piṉṉum piṉṉum an nampikkiyāl tuyarappaṭavē avaḷukku vayiṟṟuvali mikutippaṭṭu enta maruntilum tīrātatāy avaḷit tuṉpuṟuttalāyiṟṟu. kaṭiciyāyp palvali tīrtta maruttuvariyē varuvittu avarkku annōyiṉ kāraṇattit terivippa, avar nakittu `ammā pal piṭuṅkuvatippaṟṟi coṉṉāl nīṅkaḷ uṭaṉpaṭamāṭṭīrkaḷ eṉṟu kaṇṭu, uṅkaḷi oruvakiyāṉa tūkkattiṟ pōkac ceytu, uṅkaḷukku nōy teriyāmal antappalli nāṉē piṭuṅki veḷiyē eṟintuviṭṭēṉ eṉṟār. ataṟku anta ammi `eṉ palli nīṅkaḷ piṭuṅkiyiruntāl eṉakku atu terintirukkumē! evvaḷavu tūkkamāyiruntālum, kocuku allatu mūṭṭuppūcci kaṭittāl uṭaṉē viḻittuk koḷkiṉṟōmē.
appaṭiyirukkap pallip piṭuṅki yeṟiyumvariyil nāṉ vali teriyāmal tūṅkiyiruppēṉā! nīṅkaḷ colvati nāṉ nampak kūṭavilli. nāṉ uṇavōṭu cērttuttāṉ ataṉi viḻuṅkiyirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu maṟuttu urittāḷ. `illi ammā nāṉ varuvitta tūkkam eppōtum nīṅkaḷ tūṅkum tūkkam aṉṟu. atu vēṟuvakiyiṟ cērntatu. atil uṅkaḷukku nōyē teriyātu eṉṟu piṉṉuṅkūṟiṉār. kūṟiyum avar coṟkaḷil avaṭku nampikki piṟakkavilli. atukaṇṭu anta maruttuvar `appaṭiyāṉāl nāḷi vantu uṅkaḷ vayiṟṟi naṉṟāy ārāyntu pārttuc collukiṟēṉ eṉṟa collippōy maṟṟi nāṭkāliyil avar tirumpi vantu, avaḷ vayiṟṟi ārāyvatu pōl māyañceytu piṟaku `ām, vayiṟṟil antap pal irukkiṟatupōlattāṉ aṟikiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu collik kakkutaṟku maruntu koṭuttār. ammarunti uṭkoṇṭa ciṟitu nērattil avaḷ vāyāl kakkiṉāḷ. uṭaṉē avar tamatu kiyil maṟittu vittirunta oru palli avaḷ kakkiṉa malattil avaḷukkut teriyāmal iṭṭuvittār. piṉṉar avaḷukkuk kakkiṉa kaḷipput tīrntavuṭaṉē, avaḷ kakkiṉa malattik kāṭṭi ati ārāyntu pārkkac ceytatil atil oru pal akappaṭṭatu. ataṉik kaṇṭapiṉ ammātu tāṉ viḻuṅkiṉa taṉatu pal veḷivantuviṭṭamiyāl iṉit taṉakku vayiṟṟunōy irātu eṉṟu nampi makiḻntāḷ. avaḷ nampiyapaṭiyē avaṭku annōyum aṉṟumutal illātāyiṟṟu. ivvuṇmi nikaḻcciyiliruntu piṟar coṟkaḷil vikkum nampikkiyiṉ āṟṟal iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
iṅṅaṉamē valiyuṟutticcollum kaṭṭuriyāl nallōrit tīyōr ākkutalum tīyōri nallōr ākkutaluṅ kūṭum. āṉatupaṟṟittāṉ teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
 maṉanalam naṉkuṭiya rāyiṉuñ cāṉṟōrk
  kiṉanalam ēmāp puṭittu
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytār. paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum,
 nallōr iṇakkamum niṉpūci nēcamum ñāṉamumē
  allātu vēṟu niliyuḷatō.
eṉṟu aruḷiṉār. ātalāl, tāñ cērntu niṉṟavar coṟkaḷ tammi evvaḻiyun tiruppumātalāl tīmiyi viḷivippār kūṭṭuṟavu peritum añci viṭaṟpālatām eṉka. atu niṟka.
aṟituyilip piṟarpāl varuvippavarkaḷ tām collum coṟkaḷ avarkku evvakiyālun tīṅku payavāmal pātukāttuc collutaṟku nirampavuṅ karuttāyp paḻakal vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉṟāl, aṟituyilil oruvar amarntirukkumpōtu avarukku etaṉic coṉṉālum uṭaṉē avar ataṉpaṭi ceyvar. orōvorukāl tappit tavaṟit tītāṉa oru colli appōtu colliviṭṭāl, uṭaṉē ataṉi māṟṟi nallatoṉṟi avarkkuc collal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, oṉṟik kaṭṭurittuc colluṅkāl tām collum col ovvoṉṟiluṅ karuttip patiyavittu āḻnta kuralil avaṟṟi nīṭṭi mellac collal vēṇṭum. tūkkatti oruvarkku varuvittaṟ poruṭṭut `tūkkam eṉṉuñ collac colluṅkāl accolliṉkaṇ uḷḷa ovvōr eḻuttōciyiyum nīṭṭit `tū-k-ka-m eṉṟu āḻnta kuralil viriyāmaṟ collap paḻakikkoḷka. mutalil tūkkatti varuvikkumpōtu iṅṅaṉam āḻamāy nīṭṭi mellac collavēṇṭuvatu kaṭṭāyamākum; āṉāl, ataṉi naṉṟāy varuvittapiṉ ivvāṟu collavēṇṭuvatilli. eḻut tōcikaḷi oṉṟu cērttuc ciṟitu aḻuttamāyp pēcuvatē pōtum. eṉṟālum, entac collic coṉṉālum niṉivic citaṟaviṭātu muṉikka niṟuttippēcutalē karutiya payaṉit taruvatākum eṉṟu kaṭippiṭikka.
10. aṟituyilukku ēṟṟakālam
iṉi, aṟituyil varuvittaṟkēṟṟa perumpoḻutu, ciṟupoḻutukaḷip paṟṟiyum ciṟitu terintukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. kaṭuveyil kāyum vēṉiṟkālattiṉ uccip pakaṟpoḻutu aṟituyili oruvarpāl varuvittaṟku ēṟṟakālam aṉṟu. ēṉeṉṟāl, appōtu puḻukkamum viyarviyum kaḷippum evarkkum uṇṭātal iyaṟki yātalāl, aṟituyiliṟ celvōrkkum atil avaric celuttuvōrkkum ayarvu mikutiyum uṇṭām. ayarvu vantapōtu niṉiviṉ valimiyum uṭampiṉ valimiyum curuṅkuvatāl appōtu atil muyalutal orukāl iruvarkkum tīṅkiṉiyum payakkum. ātalāl, akkālattil atu ceyyaṟka. āyiṉum, kuḷirnta cōlikaḷiṉ iṭiyē veyil veppam teriyāmal cilleṉṟirukkum uyarnta māḷiki vīṭukaḷilāṉāl, naṇpakaṟkālattum ataṉic ceytu pārttal paḻutākātu. mikavum iṭarāṉa nōykaḷi virintu nīkkavēṇṭi vantakkāl, appōtu uccip poḻutāyiruntālum ataṉic ceytal kuṟṟamākātu eṉiṉum, attakiya nēraṅkaḷiluṅkūṭa aṟituyil varuvip pōr tamakkuk kaḷippu uṇṭākātapaṭi oḻuṅku ceytu koḷḷal naṉṟu.
maṟṟu, vēṉiṟkālattiṉ kāli mālip poḻutukaḷum, maḻikāla paṉikkālaṅkaḷiṉ ellāp poḻutukaḷum aṟituyil varuvittaṟku icivāṉa kālaṅkaḷeṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. mēlum, uṇaveṭutta piṉṉum, paciyāṉa nēramum aṟituyilukku ēṟṟa kālaṅkaḷ alla. avviraṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa kālamē ataṟkup poruttamāṉateṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. atuvaṉṟiyum, kaḷippuṅ kavaliyum tuṉpamum koṇṭirukkuṅkālam iruvarkkum ākā tākiyāl, avi tīrntu maṉam amiti peṟṟu muyaṟciceytaṟku ēṟṟa  cuṟucuṟuppuḷḷa nēramē iruvarum  atil muṉittu muyalutaṟkut takkateṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
11. aṟituyilukku ēṟṟavar
aṟituyiliṟ cellutaṟkuriya icivu ellā uyirkaḷiṭattum  amintiruppiṉum, makkaḷ cilariṭattu ataṉi varuvippatu eḷitākavum, vēṟu cilariṭattu ataṉi varuvippatu aritākavuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa ātalāl, piṟarpāl itaṉi varuvittaṟku mutaṉmutaṟ paḻakuvōr eḷitāka ataṉi varuvittaṟku ēṟṟavarit terinteṭuttu ataṉi avarpāṟ ceytu pārttup paḻakutal vēṇṭum. ataṉi iṅṅaṉam palakāṟ paḻakip paḻakivarap piṟaku ettakiyōriyum aṟituyiliṟ celuttum āṟṟal  mikuntuvarum. ōriṭattiṟ kūṭiyirukkum ōr āyiram pēri orunoṭip poḻutil aṟituyiliṟ celuttat takka valimi vāyntavarkaḷ amerikkā tēyattil irukkiṉṟaṉar. kūṉ, kuruṭu, cappāṇi mutalāṉavark kellām ivvaṟituyili varuvittu ataṉ utaviyāl avarkkuḷḷa kuṟipāṭukaḷi nīkkum āṟṟalmikkavarkaḷum annāṭṭilirukkiṟārkaḷ. ākiyāl, itil naṉṟāyp paḻaki āṟṟal mikkavarkaḷ viyakkattakka arum peruñ ceyal kaḷellām eḷitiṟ ceyyamāṭṭuvārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟintu koḷka. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ mutalil eḷitāka aṟituyiliṟ cellat takkavarkaḷit terintu eṭukkumāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉṟāl ataṉiyuñ ciṟitu kāṭṭutum.
ēḻeṭṭup peyari ōriṭattil varuvittu mutalil oruvarit tamakku etirē niṟutti, avaratu neṟṟikku nērē tam kikaḷi nīṭṭi viralkaḷik kaviḻttapaṭiyāy nērākap paṭittu avartam kaṇkaḷic ciṟitunēram uṟṟu yāṉ eṉ kikaḷi muṉṉē iḻukkumpōtu eṉ kiyōṭukūṭavē nīr varuvīr. aṅṅaṉam varakkūṭāteṉṟu nīr muyaṉṟālum ummāl muṭiyātu, nāṉ virumpukiṟapaṭiyē nāṉ collukiṟapaṭiyē nīr eṉatu kiyōṭu kūṭa muṉvarakkaṭavīr eṉṟu nālintu muṟi tiruppit tiruppic colli, aṟituyil varuvippōr nīṭṭiya tamatu kiyi mellamella muṉṉē iḻuttuk koṇṭu piṉṉē cellaccella aṟituyiliṟ celvōrum avar kiyōṭukūṭavē varuvar. aṅṅaṉam varalākāteṉṟu avar eṇṇiṉālum avarāl muṭiyāmal akkiyōṭu kūṭavē varak kāṇalām. ivvāṟu muṉniṉṟu avarit toṭāmal iḻuppatupōlavē, avarkkup piṉ niṉṟu piṉṟalikkunērē kiyippiṭittu piṉṉukku iḻuppatuñ ceyyalām.
ivviraṇṭuñ ceytu pārttapiṟaku avarkiyi viṟikkac ceyvatākiya maṟṟoṉṟum ceytupārttal vēṇṭum. aḵteṅṅaṉa meṉṟāl; avari nērē niṟutti avaratu valatukiyit tūkki nīṭṭi, avar tam kaṇkaḷic ciṟitu nēram uṟṟunōkkiyapiṉ tamatu valakkiyāl avaratu valakkiyit tōḷiliruntu muṉkivariyiṟ ṟaṭavik koṇṭē piṉvarumāṟu colluka: umatu ki ippōtu mikavum viṟittup pōkiṉṟatu. itaṉi muṭakka ummāl ākātu. itu marampōl viṟittuniṟkum. nī evvaḷavu muyaṉṟālum itaṉi muṭakka ummāl ākātu eṉṟu tiruppittiruppic collip piṉ ataṉi avar maṭakkutaṟku muyalumpaṭi kēṭka. aṅṅaṉamē ataṉi avartāmākavē maṭakkutaṟku muyaṉṟālum atu ceyya muṭiyāmal niṟpar. ataṉpiṟaku ippōtu nīr umatu kiyi maṭakkakkūṭiyavar āvīr eṉṟu colli akkiyiṉmēl nōkkit taṭavuka. iṅṅaṉañcolliyapiṉ avar tamatu ki muṉpōl nīṭṭavum muṭakkavum vallavar āvār. itupōlavē, muṉṉiḻukkavum piṉṉiḻukkavuñ ceytapiṉ iṉi nīr umatu viruppampōl naṭappīr eṉṟu colli mēlnōkkit taṭavuka. iṅṅaṉañ ceyyā toḻiyiṉ, avar tamatu nili maṟantavarāyiruttaluṅ kūṭum. iṉik kiyi marakkacceytatu pōlavē kāliyum marakkac ceytu pārkkalām.
iṉi, mēṟcolliya muṟiyōṭu avaratu kaṇṇi mūṭac ceytu piṟaku avartāmē avaṟṟit tiṟakkamuṭiyāmal irukkumāṟum ceyyalām. eṅṅaṉameṉṟāl, avari oru nāṟkāliyil amaracceytu avar kaṇkaḷic ciṟitu nēram uṟṟunōkkiyapiṉ, avar kaṇkaḷit tamatu kiyāl mūṭi, avaṟṟiṉ irippimēl nālintu muṟi taṭavip piṉvarumāṟu colluka: ippōtu ummuṭiya kaṇkaḷ iṟukkamāy mūṭikkoḷkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟit tiṟakka ummāl ciṟitum ēlātu. nīr tiṟakkappārum, āṉāl, ummāl atu muṭiyātu. eṉṟu colliyapiṉ avar avaṟṟit tiṟakkamuyaṉṟālum atu muṭiyāmaliruppar. piṉṉar iṉi numatu viruppampōl numkaṇkaḷit tiṟakkavum mūṭavum vallavarāvīr eṉṟu colli mēl nōkkik kikaḷi vīcuka. iṅṅaṉañ ceytavuṭaṉē avar eppōtum uḷḷa niliyiṉi aṭivar. iṅṅaṉam eṭuttukkāṭṭiya inta nāṉku muṟiyālum aṟituyilil eḷitākac cellutaṟku ēṟṟavarkaḷit terinteṭukkalām. inta muṟikaḷiṟ coṉṉapaṭi naṭakkamāṭṭā tavarkaḷukku ataṉi varuvittal iṭarppaṭumātalāl, tuvakkattil avarpāl aṟituyili varuvittaṟku muyalalākātu. aṟituyiliṟ cellutaṟkēṟṟa takutiyuṭiyavarpāl ataṉi varuvittaṟku naṉṟāyp paḻakit tērntapiṉ, eḷitil aṟituyiliṟ cellamāṭṭāta varkaḷiyum atil viriviṟ cellumāṟu tūṇṭalām.
12. aṟituyiliṟ celuttum muṟikaḷ
iṉi, mēṟcolliya muṟikaḷiṉpaṭi ārāyntu terinteṭutta oruvari aṟituyiliṟ cellumāṟu ceytaṟkuriya palavakippaṭṭa muṟikaḷiyum aṭiyavē eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkuvām. aṟituyiliṟ celvōrit tuyilvōr eṉṟum, atil avaric celuttuvōrit tuyiṟṟuvōr eṉṟum kūṟuvōm. terinteṭutta oruvari oru nāṟkāliyil irukkacceytu, avar tamatu maṭimēl tamatu iṭatukiyi vittu avviṭatu kimmēl valatukiyi virittu vittu ataṉ uḷḷaṅkiyil ciṟiya oru veḷḷik kāci vittu ataṉi avar imikoṭṭāmal uṟṟuppārkkumpaṭi ceytal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu avar ēḻeṭṭu nimiṭaṅkaḷ uṟṟunōkkiyapiṉ, avaratu uccan taliyiliruntu muḻaṅkāl variyil toṭṭāyiṉum toṭāmalāyiṉum kikaḷāṟ kīḻnōkkiyapaṭiyāyp palamuṟi taṭavit tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu iṭatu kiyiṉ peruviralāl tuyilvōriṉ neṟṟiyiṟ puruvattiṉ naṭuviṭattiyum tamatu valakkiyiṉ peruviralāl avaratu valakkiyiṉ peruviraliyum mella aḻuttuka. iṅṅaṉam ceykiyil, avartam kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoḷḷum paṭi kaṟpittu āḻntu nīṇṭa kuralil tūk-kam, ayarn-ta tūkka-m, nal-la-tūk-kam eṉṟu tiruppit tiruppiccolli, niṉivi orumukappaṭutti avar tūṅkavēṇṭum eṉpatil niṟuttuka.
iṉi maṟṟorumuṟi tuyilvōri oru nāṟkāliyil vacatiyāka amaracceytu, tuyiṟṟuvōr avaretiril niṉṟukoṇṭu tam kaṇkaḷi avar uṟṟu nōkkumpaṭi ceytu, tāmum avaratu puruvattiṉ naṭuvil tamatu pārviyi niṟuttutal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam iruvarum aintu nimiṭaṅkaḷ uṟṟunōkkiyapiṉ, tuyiṟṟuvōr tuyilvōr pakkattil niṉṟukoṇṭu tamatu iṭatu kiyāl avaratu piṉṟaliyit tāṅka valatu kiyi avar tam neṟṟimēl vittuk kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoḷḷumpaṭi avarukkuk kaṟpittu, neṟṟiyik kīḻnōkki mellat taṭavikkoṇṭē tūkkam, nal-la-tūk-kam, ayarnta-tūkkam, iṉi-ya-tūkkam eṉṟu tiruppit tiruppic colluka. iṅṅaṉañ ciṟitunērañ colliya aḷavilē avar tūṅkat tuvaṅkuvar.
iṉṉum orumuṟi: tuyilvōri vacatiyāṉatōr irukkiyil amaracceytu, kāmpiṟkōtta oruciṟu paḷiṅ kuruṇṭiyit tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu kiyiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭu, ataṉit tuyilvōruṭiya kaṇkaḷukku nērē kāṭṭi avar ataṉi uṟṟuppārkkumpaṭi kaṟpikka. piṉṉar avvuruṇṭiyi mutalil mella vaṭṭamāyc cuṟṟuka. aṅṅaṉañ cuḻaṟṟuṅkāl tuyilvōr kaṇkaḷ av vuruṇṭiyip pārttapaṭiyākavē tāmuñ cuḻalutal vēṇṭum. tuvakkattil metuvākac cuḻaṟṟiya ataṉi varavara viciyākac cuḻaṟṟuka. ivvāṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nimiṭaṅkaḷ cuḻaṟṟiyapiṉ, tāḻnta orē vakiyāṉa kuralil uṉ kaṇ iṟippikaḷ aluppaṭikiṉṟaṉa - uṉ kaṇkaḷ ayarvaṭi kiṉṟaṉa - avi imikkat tuvaṅkukiṉṟaṉa - ippōtu nī teḷivāyp pārkka muṭiyātu - uṉ kaṇṇiṟippikaḷ mūṭikkoḷkiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟu uṟutti mellac colluka. ivvāṟu palamuṟi tiruppic coṉṉāl avar ayarntu uṟaṅkuvar. iṅṅaṉan tiruppit tiruppic colliyum avar uṟaṅkāviṭṭāl, uṉ kaṇkaḷi iṟuka mūṭik koṇṭu, ippōtu naṉṟāy uṟaṅku eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttic colluka.
iṉṉum orumuṟi: tuyilvōr oruvarit takkatōr irukkiyil amaracceytāyiṉum, nīḷap paṭukka vittāyiṉum, avartam iraṇṭu kikaḷum uḷḷaviralkaḷil naṭuviraliyum mōtira viraliyum tuyiṟṟuvōr tammuṭiya, kikaḷiṉmēl ēṟṟikkoṇṭu tammuṭiya peruviralkaḷāl avaruṭiya avviral nakattiṉ vērkaḷi aḻuttuka. aṅṅaṉam aḻuttuvatu cevviyāyum orē taṉmiyāyum iruttal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟaḻuttuvatum ovvoruvarukku ovvoru vakiyāy iruttal vēṇṭum. evvevarkku evvevvakiyāy aḻuttal vēṇṭumeṉpati naṉku teriya virumpiṉāl, ippaḻakkattiṟku mika icinta palari muṉṉē kāṭṭiyapaṭi terinteṭuttu avaruḷ ovvoruvariyum mellavēṉum ciṟitu kaṭukavēṉum palapaṭiyāy aḻuttip pārttu, evarkku etu nallatāyirukkiṉṟateṉṟu avaravar vāy moḻiyik kēṭṭē aṟintu koḷka. ippaṭiyākat terintukoṇṭa piṉ evari eṅṅaṉam aḻuttutal vēṇṭumō avari aṅṅaṉam aḻuttuka; iṅṅaṉam avartam naṭuviral nāṉkām viral nakavērkaḷi aḻuttiṉa aḷavilē avarkkut tūkkam varum; itiṟṟūkkam varuvatu tuvakkappaṭumāṉāl, avartaṅ kaṇkaḷi mūṭit tūk-kam, tū-k-kam, ayarn-ta-tūk-kam, nal-la-tū-kkam, i-ṉi-ya-tū-k-kam eṉṟu āḻnta kuralil toṭarpāy orē vakiyāy mellat tiruppit tiruppic colluka ippaṭic colliya ciṟitu nērattilellām avar ayarntu tūṅkuvar.
maṟṟum orumuṟi: tuyilvōri irukkiyil amarac ceytapiṉ avaratu mukattic ciṟitu muṉ iḻuttuk kaviḻac ceyka. avar tam kikaḷit tam muḻaṅkālkaḷmēl vittuk koḷḷumāṟum, tam aṭikaḷi oruṅku cērttuk koḷḷu māṟum ceyka. piṟaku tuyiṟṟuvōr avarkketirē yuḷḷa irukkiyil irukkumpōtu, ivar tammuṭiya muḻaṅkālil oṉṟu tuyilvōriṉ muḻaṅkālkaḷukku iṭiyilē irukkumāṟu amartal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam amartal muṟiyākāta viṭattu, avarit toṭāmalē ciṟitu akaṉṟum irukkalām. tuyilvōr toṭattakka varāyiruntāl, avaratu neṟṟimēl valatukiyi vittu, iṭatukiyi avaratu valatu tōḷmēl vikkalām; toṭakkūṭātavarāyiruntāl, aṅṅaṉaṅ kikaḷi avarmēl viyāmalē nēr iruntu, avaruṭiya kaṇkaḷi oru ciṟitu nēram uṟṟu nōkkuka. aṅṅaṉam nōkkiyapiṉ iruvar uḷḷamum orumittami terintu koṇṭu, avartam kaṇkaḷi mūṭik koḷḷumāṟu kaṟpikka. ivvāṟu orumippāṭu uṇṭāṉavuṭaṉē, tuyiṟṟuvōr collumāṟellām tuyilvōr ceyyat talippaṭuvar. iruvarkkum iv vorumippāṭu uṇṭāṉati uṟutiyāyt terintukoḷḷa vēṇṭumāṉāl, uḷḷatti ōṭāmal naṉku niṟutti, mūṭik koṇṭa avartam kaṇkaḷit toṭṭēṉun toṭāmalēṉuṅ kīḻ nōkki nālintu muṟi taṭavip piṟaku avari nōkki nīr evvaḷavu muyaṉṟālum ippōtu ummuṭiya kaṇkaḷi ummāl tiṟakka muṭiyātu; tiṟakka muyaṉṟupārum, ummāl atu muṭiyātu eṉṟu mūṉṟu nāṉku muṟi tiruppit tiruppic colluka. avarum kaṇkaḷit tiṟakka muyalvār; āṉāl, avar tuyiṟṟuvōr vayappaṭṭu niṉṟāl avarāl avaṟṟit tiṟattal muṭiyātu; avar vayappaṭṭu nillāviṭṭāl uṭaṉē kaṇkaḷit tiṟantu viṭuvār. itukoṇṭu, ava tuyiṟṟuvōruṭaṉ orumittu niṟpatum nillātatum tiṭṭamāy uṇarappaṭum. mūṭiya kaṇkaḷit tiṟavāmalirukkac ceytal pōlavē, tūkkiya kiyik kīḻviḻāmal viṟittu niṟkavum, mūṭiya vāyit tiṟantu pēcāmalirukkavum, irunta iṭatti viṭṭu eḻāmal irukkavum, nīṭṭiya kāli maṭakkamāṭṭāmal vikkavum, iṉṉum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavuñ ceytu tuyilvōr tuyiṟṟu vōruṭaṉ orumittu niṟpatum nillātatum eḷitil uṇaralām.
ivvāṟu immuṟikaḷuṭ palavēṉum oṉṟēṉum ceytu pārttu avar orumittu niṟṟali yaṟintapiṉ, iraṇṭu kiviralkaḷiyum virittu nīṭṭi, avaratu uccantaliyiliruntu kāṟkīḻpperuviral muṭiyat toṭṭēṉun toṭāmalēṉuṅ kīḻ nōkkit taṭavuka. iṅṅaṉam pattup paṉṉiraṇṭu muṟi taṭaviyavuṭaṉ, tuyiṟṟuvōr iḷippaṭintāl iṭi yiṭiyē nīḷat taṭavāmal, muḻaṅkāl iṭuppu mārpu kaṇ neṟṟi mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷ variyilātal taṭavalām. iṅṅaṉaṅ kīḻnōkkit taṭavum ovvoru muṟiyuṅ kikaḷi utaṟiviṭuvatōṭu, maṟupaṭiyuṅ kikaḷi uyarttuṅkāl avaṟṟip pakkac cāyvāyt tiruppit tuyilvōriṉ vilāppuṟamāka mēl uyarttutal vēṇṭum. taṭavutali muṭikkumpōtu kāliṉ peruviral nuṉivariyil nīḷat taṭavi muṭikka; aḵtu etaṉ poruṭṭeṉṟāl, iṭiyiṭiyē muḻaṅkāl iṭuppu mārpu mutalāṉa uṟuppukaḷ variyil taṭaviyiruntāl avvuṟuppiṭaṅkaḷ variyil vantu taṅki niṉṟa miṉcatti nīḷak kāṟperuviral muṭiya ōṭacceytaṟ poruṭṭēyāmeṉka.
ivvāṟu taṭavi muṭitta nērattil tuyilvōriṉ tali mella mellak kīḻnōkkit toṅkat tuvaṅkum. ituvum tūkkattiṟkuriya maṟṟa aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷum kaṇṭapiṉ, avar yōkanittiri eṉṉum aṟituyiliṟ celkiṉṟār eṉpataṉit terintukoḷka. ataṉit terintatun tuyiṟṟuvōr iṉṉum mikutiyāṉa nampikkiyōṭuṅ kiḷarcciyōṭum avarit tuyiṟṟutaṟkuc celvārāka. ceṉṟu piṉṉar avaratu mukam avartam muḻaṅkālil vantu kaviḻntukiṭakkumaḷavum avarit tuyiṟṟutaṟkuriya muṟikaḷi viṭātu ceyka. avaratu tali avartam muḻaṅkālil vantu poruntumaḷavum avarit tuyiṟṟiyapiṉ, avar ayarnta tuyilil irukkiṉṟār eṉpatit terintu koḷka. inniliyil avar iṉṉum āḻnta tuyiliṟ cellum poruṭṭu, avaratu piṭariyiṉmēl vāyiṉāl ūti, avartam piṉtalimēl tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu pārviyi niṟuttip piṟaku aṅkiruntu avaratu mutukiṉ naṭuvip palakāṟ kīḻnōkkit taṭavuka. aṅṅaṉañ ceytapiṉ, avar āḻnta tuyilil irukkiṉṟārā illiyā eṉṟu teritaṟkut tuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuppuvatupōl avari appuṟamum ippuṟamumāka acittup pārkkalām. avvāṟu acittum avar viḻiyāmal ayarnta tūkkattilēyē iruppārāyiṉ, avar āḻnta yōkanittiri allatu aṟituyilil irukkiṉṟāreṉpati uṇarntu koḷḷalām.
iṅṅaṉamāka oruvar āḻnta aṟituyiliṟ celuttap paṭuṅkālam avaratu uṇarviṉ cuṟucuṟuppip poṟut tirukkiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum perumpālum oru nāḻiki nēram oruvarit tuyiṟṟutaṟkup pōtumāṉatākum. orunāḻiki muyaṉṟum oruvarit tuyiṟṟutal muṭiyātāyiṉ, aṉṟikku ataṉi viṭuttu, aṭuttanāḷil avarit tuyiṟṟutaṟku muyalka. aṭutta nāḷilum atu muṭiyātāyiṉ ataṟkaṭutta nāḷilum, atilum iyalāviṭṭāl ataṟkaṭutta nāḷiṉumākat toṭarntu muyalka. iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ toṭarpāka muyaṉṟāl ettiṟattavariyun tuyilacceytal kikūṭum. mēlum, orumuṟi oruvari aṟituyil koḷḷac ceytuviṭṭāṟ, piṟaku avari ataṉkaṭ celuttutal mika eḷitu; avar tuyil koḷḷalvēṇṭumeṉa eṇṇiya aḷavāṉum, avarmēṟ cilamuṟi taṭaviya aḷavāṉum avar eḷitilē āḻnta aṟituyiliṟ cērvar.
mēlē kāṭṭiya terinteṭukkum muṟiyāl tuyilvōr oruvarit terinteṭuttu, avari etirē niṟuttikkoḷka. itaṉip paḻakum iṭattil ētōr aravamum, tuyilvōr karuttik kavarattakka ētoru poruḷum illāmal ellām amitiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum. mēṟcolliya muṟiyiṟ pōlavē avaruṭiya mukattiyum tōḷkaḷiyum muṉṉē ciṟitu iḻuttuk kuṉiyac ceytu, avartam kikaḷ vilāppuṟaṅkaḷukkuc ciṟitu muṉṉē tuvaṇṭu toṅkumāṟu viṭuka. iṅṅaṉañ ceytalāl tuyilvōrukku narampiḷakkam uṇṭām. aḵtu uṇṭākavē, avar tuyiṟṟuvōriṉ vaḻippaṭṭu niṟkum nili uṇṭām. uṭaṉē, tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu valatu kiyit tuyīlvōriṉ mutuku naṭuvil tōṭpaṭṭikaḷukku iṭiyilē vittu, iṭatu kiyi avaratu neṟṟi mēṟ puruvattiṉ naṭuvilē mella vikkakkaṭavar. ataṉ piṟaku, tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu iṭatukāli avarukku muṉṉē avartam iṇiyaṭikaḷi aṭukkavittut tamatu muḻaṅkālum avartam muḻaṅkālkaḷit toṭumpaṭiyākak kiṭṭac cērttal vēṇṭum.
ivvāṟṟal avviruvarkkum maṉa icivu uṇṭām. inniliyiṟ ciṟitunēram iruntapiṉ, tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu valatu kiyāl avaratu piṭariyiliruntu mutukiṉ aṭikāṟum taṭavutalōṭu, iṭatu kiyāl iṭiyiṭiyē avartam neṟṟiyiyuṅ kaṇkaḷiyuṅ kīḻnōkkik kaṉṉaṅkaḷvariyil taṭavuka; ataṉpiṉ avarukkup piṉṉēceṉṟu avaratu taliyiṉ piṉpuṟattē yiruntu naṭumutukiṉ neṭuka vetuvetuppu uṇṭāka vāyiṉāṟ kīḻ nōkki ūtuka. tuyilvōriṉ kaṇṇiṟippikaḷ kīḻ iṟaṅki arivāci mūṭutalum, avaruṭampil acivuṅ kāṇappaṭum variyil mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu ceyka; avviraṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭṭavuṭaṉē, tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu valatu kiyi mēṟcolliyavāṟē avaratu mutukiṉ naṭuvittu, avar tam kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoḷḷumāṟu kaṟpittut `tūṅkum eṉṟu kūṟuka. maṟupaṭiyum mēṟkāṭṭiyavāṟē muṉṉum piṉṉum taṭavutalic ceyyap pukuntu, avar niṟkamāṭṭātu taḷḷāṭum variyil aṅṅaṉañ ceyka. ataṉmēl, avar niṟkalāṟṟātu piṉṉēviḻat tuvaṅkuvarātaliṉ, avar kīḻē viḻuntuviṭāmaṟ piṉṉē tāṅkikkoṇṭu, avari nilattilāyiṉum allatoru kaṭṭiliṉ mēlāyiṉum paṭukka vittal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam avari mallākkap paṭukkavitta piṉ `tūṅkum `cevviyāyt tūṅkum eṉṟu iṭiyiṭiyē collikkoṇṭē avaratu puruvanaṭuvil melleṉa iṭatu kiyi vittu, valatukiyāl nīḷattaṭavuka. ivvāṟu ceyta ciṟitunēratti lellām avar ayarnta aṟituyiliṟ celvar.
iṉi orēkālattiṟ palarit tuyiṟṟutaṟku oruvar kikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟi yāteṉiṟ kūṟuvām. māntarkaḷiṟ palar palavēṟuvakiyāṉa iyalpukaḷ uṭiyavarāyiruttaliṉ avarkaḷit tuyiṟṟutaṟkuc ceyyum muṟikaḷum palavēṟu vakip paṭṭaṉavāy irukkumeṉpatu uṇarappaṭum. eṉṟālum, oruvar palavakiyāṉa muṟikaḷiyum aṭuttaṭuttuc ceytu paḻakivaruvarāyiṉ, avar appaḻakkattāl āṟṟal mikappeṟṟu orē oru muṟiyāṟ palariyun tuyiṟṟavallunar āvar. āyiṉum, cilanēraṅkaḷil avaruṅkūṭap palarit tuyilacceyyum poruṭṭup palavēṟu muṟikaḷik kiyāḷa vēṇṭi varumātalāl, avar perumpālum ammuṟikaḷellā vaṟṟiyun terintu paḻakik koḷḷutal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. iṉip, palarit tuyiṟṟutaṟku icinta ōr ariya muṟiyiṉi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟām.
aṟituyilil tuyiṟṟappaṭutaṟku icintuvanta palariyum oru variciyāka irukkikaḷiṉmēṟ cevvaṉē amarac ceyka. muṉṉum piṉṉuñ ceṉṟu avarkaḷit taṭavit tuyiṟṟutaṟku icivāka avarkaḷ etirilum piṉ puṟattilum vēṇṭumaḷavukku iṭam tārāḷamāy irukka vēṇṭum. appalariṉ muḻaṅkālkaḷum pakkaṅkaḷil oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭumāṟu irukkavittāl, avvellāriṭattum pāyum miṉāṟṟal avariṭattellām orē taṉmiyākap paravi oru muḻutāy niṟkum. avarkaḷuṭiya mukaṅkaḷiyellām ciṟitu muṉṉē iḻuttuk kuṉiyac ceyvatuṭaṉ, avar tam kikaḷiyum tam muḻaṅkālkaḷiṉmēl vittuk koḷḷumāṟu ceyka. avarkaḷic cūḻa uḷḷa ellām amitiyāyirukka, iviyellām ceytāṉapiṉ, avarkaḷ ellārun tamatu kaṭpārviyi oru muṉiyil niṟuttumāṟu kaṟpikka. iḵtu avarkaḷ tamatu mūkku muṉiyi nōkkutalāl eḷitiṟ ceyyappaṭum. kaṇviḻit tirukkamāṭṭāmal avarkaḷ tam kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoṇṭu tūṅka maṉam vantāl ataṉit taṭiceyyāmal avvāṟē ceyyumpaṭi kūṟuka. ataṉpiṉ avar ovvoruvariyum etirē muṭimutaṟ ṟuvaṅki muḻaṅkāl variyum nālintu muṟi iraṇṭu kikaḷāluṅ kīḻnōkkit taṭavuka. piṟaku avarkkup piṉpuṟattē pōy avar ovvoruvariṉ piṉṟali yaṭiyiliruntu mutuku neṭuka aṅṅaṉamē taṭavuka avaṟṟōṭu, iraṇṭoru muṟi avar ovvoruvariṉ puruvanaṭuvil valatu kiyi vittuc ciṟitu melleṉat taṭavuka. iṅṅaṉañ ceykiyil, avarkaḷiṉ talikaḷil āṭṭaṅ kāṇkiṉṟatā eṉa uṟṟu nōkkuka. avarkaḷil evarkkēṉum āṭṭaṅkaṇṭāl, tuyiṟṟuvōr tam kiviralkaḷi avar taliyucciyiṉ mēl vittut tamatu peruviralāṟ kīḻnōkki aḻuttikkoṇṭē, avar tam kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoḷḷumāṟu kaṟpittut `tūṅkum eṉṟu colli, avaratu taliyi nimirtti aḵtu avar nāṟkāliyiṉ mutukumēṟ cāyntirukkumāṟu vikka. piṟaku avartam muṭimēl vitta viralkaḷāl mellamellak kīḻnōkki neṟṟi, kaṇkaḷ mukammuṭiyat taṭavuka.
iṅṅaṉam ellām ceyyavē avarkaḷil uṇarvu nuṭpam iyaṟki yilē vāyntavarkaḷ tūkkamayakkam uṭiyavarkaḷāyk kāṇappaṭuvar; avarkaḷi maṭṭum mēlum pala ārāyccikaḷic ceytu pārttaṟkut takkavarkaḷāka iruttik koḷka; attūkka mayakkam illātavarkaḷi avvariciyiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟiviṭuka. piṉṉar aṟituyilukku ēṟṟavarāka iruttappaṭṭavarkaṭku mēlum mēlum tūkkam uṇṭākumpaṭi ippōtu muyalat toṭaṅkuka. ippōtu tīrmāṉattōṭum virivāy avarkaḷiṉmēl taṭavutalic ceytu, avarkaḷ ovvoru varāy āḻnta tuyiliṟ cellumāṟu kaṟpikka. avarkaḷ āḻnta aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟa aḷavāṉē, avarkaḷiṉ uṭampu tuvaṇṭirukkum, avarkaḷ orē ottaniliyiliruttal iyalātu, tammiccūḻa naṭappatu iṉṉateṉṟum aṟiyār. ivvaṭi yāḷaṅkaḷik koṇṭum, ayarnta tuyilukkuriya maṟṟak kuṟikaḷik koṇṭum avarkaḷatu tuyiliṉ nilimi naṉkuṇarappaṭum eṉka.
iṉi, nāṭaka araṅkiṟ kaṇkāṭci kāṇavarum palaruṭ cilarit terinteṭuttu, avarkaḷiyellām iṉ collōṭum iṉmukattōṭum nāṭaka araṅkiṉmēl varuvittu vittu, avarkaḷi aṟituyiliṟ pōkacceytu, avarkaḷ pāṟ palaviyappāṉa kāṭcikaḷiyellāṅ kāṭṭalām. āṉāl, nāṭaka araṅkil ivvaṟituyilip palarpāl varuvittup pala viyappāṉa nikaḻccikaḷik kāṭṭutal eḷitaṉṟu. aṟiyil āṭi ampalattil āṭu eṉṉum paḻamoḻikku iṇaṅka, mutalil vīṭṭiṉkaṇṇē ikkalvimuṟiyic cevvaṉē paḻakit tērccipeṟṟapiṉ nāṭaka araṅkil itaṉik kāṭṭutaṟku veḷivaral vēṇṭum. nāṭaka araṅkil vantēṟutaṟku icinta āṟṟalpeṟṟu ataṉkaṇ vantavar mutalil `yōkanittiri eṉṉum itaṉ mēṉmiyiyum itaṉāl viḷiyum payaṉkaḷiyuṅ kēṭpōr uḷḷattil naṉṟāyt tikkumpaṭi ciṟitu nēram eṭuttuppēcuka.
aṅṅaṉam pēci muṭittapiṉ, etirē avikkaḷatti liruppōrkkut tām ceytu kāṭṭappōkum muṟikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟic collikkāṭṭi, avaṟṟiṟku avarkaḷum utaviyāy niṟkavēṇṭumeṉpataṉi vaṟpuṟuttuka. piṉṉar aviyil uḷḷāril evarevar aṟituyiliṟ cella viruppam uḷḷārō avarellām nāṭaka mēṭimēl varukaveṉṟu aṉpākak kēṭṭukkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. ataṟkicinta palar nāṭaka mēṭimēl varuvar. nāṭaka araṅkiṉ orupuṟattē iṉṉicik karuvikaḷuṭaṉ iṉiya icippāṭṭukaḷ cilar pāṭik koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. mēṭimēl vantōri yellām arivaṭṭamāka iṭṭa nāṟkāli varicimēl aviyi luḷḷārkku etirmukamāka irukkumpaṭi amaracceyka. iṉi, etirmukamāka vaṉṟi, mēṭiyiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum irukkumāṟum ceyyalām. avarkaḷi avvāṟu iruttiyapiṉ, ovvoruvariyum ārāyntu pārttut, tuyiṟṟutaṟkut takutiyillātavareṉṟu kāṇappaṭuvōri yellām paṇivōṭum iṉitākac collit tirumpavum kīḻē aṉuppiviṭuka. mēṭimēl vantatum nāṟkālikaḷiṉmēl iruttappaṭṭōril evar tamatu oru kālmēl maṟṟoru kālit tūkkivittuk koṇṭu nimirntapaṭiyāyp piṉṉē cāyntu irukkiṉṟaṉarō avar iṟumāppu uṭiyavareṉat terintu, avar aṟituyilukkut takutiyillātavarātalāl avari uṭaṉē kīḻē pōkacceyka. aṅṅaṉamē, pukiyili tiṉpōriyum, pukiccuruṭṭup piṭippōriyum, mūkkup poṭiyiṭuvōriyum, cārāya nāṟṟam uṭiyōriyum itaṟkut takutiyillātavareṉa uṇarntu avariyum akaṟṟik kīḻē pōkacceyka. tuyiṟṟuvōr itaṟkumuṉ aṟituyiliṟ palakāṟ cellumpaṭi paḻakkappaṭuttiya cilariyum tammōṭu uṭaṉkoṇṭuvantu muṟkūṟiya putiyōr pakkattil irukkumpaṭi ceytalum naṉṟu.
ataṉ piṟaku, tuyiṟṟuvōr tām collukiṟapaṭi yellām avarkaḷ uṇmiyuṭaṉ naṭantukoḷḷumpaṭi kaṟpikka. avarkaḷi nōkkic colluvaṉa ellām iyaṟkiyāṉa kuralil tīrmāṉattōṭum teḷivākac collappaṭutal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ ovvoruvarum maṭimēl vitta tamatu iṭatu uḷḷaṅkiyiṉmēl tamatu valatukiyi mēlnōkki virittuvittukkoḷḷumpaṭi ceyka. piṉṉarc ciṟiya veḷḷik kācukaḷiyēṉum, allatu avaṟṟippōla paḷicceṉṟirukkum vēṟuciṟu poruḷ kaḷiyēṉum varuvittu avi ovvoṉṟiyum ovvoruvar kiyiṉum iṭṭu, avarellārum tam kiyiluḷḷa ac ciṟuporuḷi imi koṭṭāmaṟ kuṉinta paṭiyāy uṟṟunōkkumpaṭi colluka. avar ellārum aṅṅaṉam uṟṟunōkkikkoṇ ṭirukkiyil, tuyiṟṟuvōr muṉṉamē itil paḻakkit tammuṭaṉ koṇṭu vantōril oruvariyum aṅṅaṉamē uṟṟunōkkumpaṭi ceytu avarit tuyiṟṟutaṟkup pukuka.
itil muṉṉarē paḻakkappaṭuttap paṭṭavarkku inta muṟiyil aṉṟi, veṟuñcollaḷavilē tuyili eḷitil varuvikkalāmeṉṟālum, maṟṟa ellārkkuñ ceyvatupōṟ ceytalāṟ, putiyarāy vantu amarntōr ataṉik kaṇṭu tuyiṟṟuvōrvaḻiyil eḷitil aṭaṅki niṉṟu tuyilvar. paḻiyavari nōkkit `tūṅkum eṉṟu cilamuṟi tiruppit tiruppic coṉṉavuṭaṉ avar naṉṟāyt tūṅkuvar. avarit tuyiṟṟiyapiṉpu, maṟṟip putiyōriṭañ ceṉṟu avaruṭ kaṇ iṟippikaḷ āṭappeṟṟārip pārttu `nīr num kaṇkaḷi iṟukamūṭikkoṇṭu tūṅkum, eṉṟu colliviṭṭu virintu avarkaḷiṉ kaṇ iṟippikaḷiṉ mēl toṭṭēṉum toṭāmalēṉum kīḻnōkkit taṭavuka. ituceytapiṉ valatukipperuvirali avar ovvoruvariṉ puruvanaṭuvil vittuc ciṟitu kīḻnōkki aḻuttikkoṇṭē avarkaḷiṉ valatukiyit tuyiṟṟuvōr iṭatukiyāl oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy melleṉap paṟṟuka iṅṅaṉañ ceykiyilēyē tīrmāṉamāṉa kuralil ippōtu - uṅkaḷ - kaṇkaḷi - nīṅkaḷ - tiṟakka - muṭiyātu eṉṟu aḻuttamāyc colluka. ivvāṟu colliyavuṭaṉē avarkaḷiṟ palar tūkkattiliruntālum illāviṭṭālum tamkaṇkaḷit tiṟakkamāṭṭāmaliruppar; cilar avvāṟaṉṟi uṭaṉē kaṇkaḷit tiṟantuviṭuvar; attakiyōr aṟituyilil eḷitilē pōtaṟkut takuti yillātavar ākiyāl, avarkaḷiyuṅ kīḻē aṉuppiviṭuka piṟaku, uṟaṅkutaṟkut takkavarāka mēṭimēl iruttik koḷḷappaṭṭavarkaṭku etirilē ciṟitu eṭṭaniṉṟu koṇṭu ellāriyum uccantalimutaṟ ṟuvaṅki aṭimuṭiya ovvoruvarāy iṭatukippakkat tuḷḷavariliruntu taṭavikkoṇṭu celka. muṉṉamē itiṟ paḻakkappaṭuttap paṭṭavarkaḷum iṭi yiṭiyē iruttalāl avarkaḷ eḷitilē ivaṟituyiliṟ celvar. maṟṟup putiyōr virivil tūṅkāviṭṭāl, ovvoruvariyum kaṇkaḷiṉ mēlum kaṉṉap poṭṭukaḷiṉ mēlum virintu kīḻ nōkkip palakāl taṭavuka. iṅṅaṉaṅ kālmaṇinēram viṭātuceytāl avarum ayarnta uṟakkattiṟku ēkuvar, toṭakkattil ovvoruvar kiyiṉum vitta veḷḷik kācukaḷi eṭuttukkoḷka.
tuvakkattil veḷḷikkācu mutalāṉa paḷicceṉṟa poruḷkaḷi vittu avaṟṟi uṟṟunōkkac ceytaliviṭa, ovvoruvarum tamatu iṭatu kipperuvirali valatu uḷḷaṅkiyil vittu iṟukap paṟṟiya paṭiyāyk kaṇkaḷi naṉṟāy mūṭik koḷḷumāṟum ceytal naṉṟu. iṅṅaṉa mellāñ ceytu cilariyēṉum palariyēṉum aṉṟi oruvariyēṉum tuyiṟṟutaṟku muyaluṅkāl tuyiṟṟuvōr tamatu niṉivip palamukamāyc citaṟa viṭātu, ellārkkun tūkkatti varuvittaliṉkaṇṇum, ataṉi varuvitta piṉṉarc ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟilum muṉittu oru mukamāyk kuvintu niṟkumāṟu ceytal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum ēṉeṉṟāl, ettakiya ariyaceyalum niṉivi orumukamāy niṟutti valivēṟṟutaliṉālēyē muṭikkappaṭukiṉṟatu tuyiṟṟuvōr iṭi yiṭiyē iḷippaṭintāṟ ciṟitu otuṅki niṉṟu tūyakāṟṟip palamuṟi mūkkāl uḷḷiḻutta lāṉum, pālēṉum pāṉakamēṉum parukutalāṉum av viḷippit tīrttukkoḷḷalām. iḷipput tīrttuk koṇṭapiṉ tirumpavum avarkaḷ pāṟceṉṟu, avarkaḷil uṟaṅkuvōrkkup piṉṉum avvuṟakkatti mikutippaṭuttal vēṇṭi mēṟcolliya muṟiyākavē piṉṉuñ ciṟitunēram muyalka.
ippōtu avarkaḷellārum naṉṟāy ayarntuṟaṅkat talippaṭuvar. avarkaḷ uṟakkattiṟkuc celkiyilēyē tuyiṟṟuvōr piṉvarumāṟu, `nīṅkaḷ - eṉ - coṟkaḷi yallāmal - vēṟu - etaṉiyum - kēṭka māṭṭīrkaḷ. nīṅkaḷ - ayarnta uṟakkattil - iruntālum - yāṉ - colluvaṉa vaṟṟik - kēṭṭu avaṟṟiṉpaṭiyellām - ceyvīrkaḷ. yāṉ - uṅkaḷi - eḻuppukiṉṟa variyil - nīṅkaḷ - naṉṟāy - ayarntu - uṟaṅkuvīrkaḷ. yāṉ - tirumpa - uṅkaḷi - eḻuppukiṉṟa pōtu - nīṅkaḷ - naṉṟāy uṟakkam nīṅki - eḻuntu viṭuvīrkaḷ eṉṟu aḻuttamāṉakuralil mella mellac colluka. ivvāṟu collit tuyiṟṟiya aḷavilē, tuyiṟṟuvōr collukiṟapaṭiyellām ceytaṟku icinta aṟituyilil avarellām cellāniṟpar.
13. mūvakittuyil
iṉi, oruvarkkō allatu palarkkō iṅṅaṉam varuvikkappaṭum aṟituyilāṉatu mūvakippaṭṭa nilikaḷi uṭiyatākiṉṟatu. avi: niṉivuttuyilum, niṉivaṟṟa tuyilum, naṭittuyilum eṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ niṉivut tuyilil iruppōrkku niṉivumāṟātu; attūkkattil naṭappaṉavellām atu nīṅkiyapiṉ niṉivukkuvarum. inniliyil iruppōrkkuk kaṇkaḷ tiṟantaṉāyiṉum mūṭiyāyiṉum arivāci tiṟantāyiṉum irukkum. ivarkaḷit tām ēviyapaṭiyellām naṭakkumāṟuceytal tuyiṟṟuvōrkku muṭiyātu; eṉṟālum ivarkaḷuṭiya mukam kikāl mutalāṉa uṟuppukaḷit tām virumpiya nilikaḷiṟ ciṟitunēram niṟkumāṟu tuyiṟṟuvōr ceytuvikkalām. ittuyilil irukkiyil uṭampil nōy uṇarcci irātu, ūciyāṟ kuttiṉālum, kattiyāṟ ciṟuka aṟuttālum nōytōṉṟātu. itaṉik kaṇṭaṟinta nuṇṇaṟivu mikka veḷḷikkāra maruttuvar cilar puṇkaḷ kaḻalikaḷāṟ poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpattiyaṭiyum nōyāḷikaḷi annōy teriyāmal avaṟṟi aṟuttut tīrkkum poruṭṭu ittuyiliṟ pōkac ceytu, avaṟṟi avarkku nōy teriyāmalē aṟuttut tīrkkiṉṟaṉar. murukakkaṭavuḷukku nērntukoṇṭu uṭampil alakēṟṟik kāvaṭi eṭuttuccelvōr ittakiyatoru niṉivut tuyiliṉ kaṇṇētāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉar. ittuyiliṉ payaṉi uṇmiyāy uṇarpavarkaḷ itaṉāṟ palanalaṅkaḷi yaṭiyalām. iravum pakalum uṟakkam iṉṟi nōyāl tuṉpuṟuvārkaḷi it tūkkattiṟ pōkac ceytāl avarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷa nōyiṉ tuṉpatti aṟiyārāy iṉitu uṟaṅkutalōṭu uṟakkamnīkki eḻuntapiṉṉum annōyiṉ koṭumi taṇiyap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉṟālum ittuyilil niṉivu māṟāma liruttalāl tuyiṟṟuvōr colvaṉavuñ ceyvaṉavum ellām tuyilnīṅki eḻuntapiṉ ēṟakkuṟiya niṉivukku varum eṉka.
iṉi, mēṟcolliya niṉivut tuyiliṉum āḻntu cellum tuyilil uyirukku niṉivu ciṟitum nikaḻāmiyāl ataṟkuppiṉ uṇṭām itu niṉivaṟṟa tuyil eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ittuyiliṉkaṇ iruppōrkkuc cuṟṟiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇarcci ciṟitum irātu; puṟattē tōṉṟum ōci evvaḷavu periyatāy evvaḷavu koṭumiyāṉatāy iruppiṉum ataṉi avar ciṟituṅkēḷār. tuyiṟṟuvōruṭiya coṟkaḷiyuṅ kuraloli yiyun tavira vēṟoṉṟaṉiyum avar uṇarār viḻikaḷ cuḻalāmal amitiyuṟṟirukkum. muṉṉē kāṭṭiya niṉivut tuyiliṟ ceṉṟavarō tiṭukkiṭattakkatu etaṉiyēṉuṅ kēṭṭapiṉ ataṉiṉiṉṟum uṭaṉē viḻitteḻuvar.
āṉāl, inniṉivaṟṟa tuyiliṟ ceṉṟavarō ettakiya ōciyāṉum tiṭukkiṭṭu eḻār. avaruṭiya mukaṅ ki kāl mutalāṉa uṟuppukaḷi enta niliyil tuyiṟṟuvōr vikkiṉṟaṉarō anta niliyilēyē avi niṟkum. avari nōkkit tuyiṟṟuvōr ētēṉum aṭuttaṭuttu viṉaviṉāl maṭṭum avar ataṉi arivāci yuṇarvōṭu kaṭṭāyattiṉāl viṭi kūṟuvar; aṅṅaṉam viṭi kūṟiyapiṉ maṟupaṭiyum avvāḻnta tūkkattiṉ kaṇṇēyē amitiyāyc ceṉṟiruppar. inniliyil uṭampiṉiṭattē uṇarvu ciṟitum illāmiyāl, uṭampil āḻamāka aṟuttuc ceyyavēṇṭiya muṟikaḷic ceytaṟku ituvē mikavum ēṟṟatoru niliyākum itaṟkumuṉ nikaḻum tuyilil niṉivu paḻutupaṭāmaliruttalāl ataṉkaṇ uṭampiṉ evviṭattiyēṉum āḻa aṟuttalāl uṭaṉē oruvar tiṭukkiṭṭu eḻuntiruttaluṅ kūṭum. maṟṟu itaṉkaṇ evvaḷavu āḻamāka uṭampi aṟuttālum, allatu ki kāl mutaliya uṟuppukaḷi veṭṭi yeṭuttālum ittuyiliṉkaṭ ceṉṟōr avaṟṟic ciṟitum uṇarār. ikkalvip paḻakkattāl nōyāḷikaḷ palarkkup poṟuttaṟ kariya nōykaḷ palavaṟṟi nōyiṉṟit tīrtta veḷḷikkāra maruttuvar oruvar (Dr. James Esdile, M.D.) nōyāḷi oruvaṉukku mika vīṅkipperutta vitikaḷ iraṇṭi nōyiṉṟi aṟutteṭuttu nalappaṭuttiṉa varalāṟṟiṉi iṅkē moḻi peyarttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟām.
paṉṟi viṟpaṉi ceyta oruvaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉi aṭittup paṭukāyappaṭuttiṉa kuṟṟattiṟkāka ēḻu āṇṭu kaḷukkuk kaṭuṅkāvalil vikkappaṭṭāṉ. avvāṟu vikkappaṭṭapiṉ cila kālattil avaṉukku iraṇṭu vitikaḷum mika vīṅkip peruttu avaṉ naṭattal iruttal eḻutal mutalāṉa evvaki naṭamāṭṭamum ceytaṟku iyalātapaṭi avaṉukkup peruntuṉpatti viḷivittaṉa. atukaṇṭu ciṟikkōṭṭat talivar avaṉi aṟamaruttuvak kaḻakattiṟku aṉuppiṉar. aṅkirunta veḷḷikkāra maruttuvar avaṉi oru nāṟkāliyil irutti, avaṉuṭiya vitikaḷil oṉṟik kutti nīri veḷiyē eṭuttuviṭṭu, ataṉuḷḷukku ēṟṟavēṇṭiya kāra marunti ēṟṟiviṭṭār. akkāramaruntu uṭceṉṟatum ataṉāl uṇṭāṉa erivu poṟukkamāṭṭāmal kaṇkaḷi iṟuka mūṭikkoṇṭu, kaṟi narampi iraṇṭu kikaḷālum piṭittu nerukkiṉapaṭiyāy mikavun tuṉpuṟuvāṉāṉāṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭu avaṉpāl irakkamuṟṟa avveḷḷikkārar taṅkīḻ aluval pārkkum vaṅkāḷi maruttuvari nōkki yōkanittiriyil celuttap paṭuvōrip pārttirukkiṉṟīrā? eṉṟu kēṭka avar maruttuvakkalvik kaḻakattil aḵtu ārāyappaṭṭatip pārttēṉ; eṉṟālum, aḵtu appōtu palikkavilli. eṉṟār. ataṉmēl avveḷḷikkāra maruttuvar akkalip payiṟciyip paṟṟi yāṉ nūlkaḷiṟ kaṟṟaṟintirukkiṉṟēṉ; āṉāl evarum atip paḻakap pārttilēṉ. ippōtu ataṉi ivaṉmēṟ ceytu pārkka mikavum virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
iḵtu eṉakkup paliyāmaṟ pōṉālum pōkalām. eṉṟu collik koṇṭē annōyāḷi nāṟkāliyil irunta paṭiyākavēyirukka, avaṉuṭiya iraṇṭu muḻaṅkālkaḷiyum tam iraṇṭu muḻaṅkālkaḷukku iṭiyē vittukkoṇṭu, tam kikaḷik kīḻnōkkiya paṭiyāy virittunīṭṭi avaṉ mukattiliruntu vayiṟṟiṉ koppūḻ variyil avaṉit toṭāmalē taṭavuvārāṉār. ivvāṟu arimaṇi nēram variyiṟ ceytapiṉ, avaṉōṭupēcip pārttatil avaṉ eppōtum pōlavē aṟivōṭu poruttamāyp pēciṉāṉ. ataṉmēl avaṉit tirumpavum amitiyāy irukkak kaṟpittu, iṉṉum kālmaṇi nēramvariyil muṉtaṭaviya paṭiyākavē taṭavip pārttār; piṉṉum avaṉ muṉṉirunta paṭiyē iruntamiyāl avar atu palikka villiyeṉṟu viṭṭuviṭṭut tām itil mikavuṅ kaḷittup pōṉamiyāl ōr irukkiyil amarntu iḷippāṟuvārāṉār. appoḻutu an nōyāḷiyō acivaṟṟu amitiyō ṭiruntāṉ; tuṉpattiṉ aṭi yāḷaṅkaḷāṉa mukaccuḷivukaḷ ippōtu avaṉiṭam kāṇappaṭavilli; kaṇṇit tiṟakkum paṭi kaṟpikkappaṭṭapōtu avaṉ `eṅkum pukicūḻntāṟpōl irukkiṉṟatu eṉṟāṉ.
accoṟkaḷik kēṭṭatum, avveḷḷikkāra maruttuvar piṉṉum avaṉi muyaṉṟu pārkkuṅ karuttuṭiyavarāy, avaṉatu talimēl vāyiṉāl ūti, avaṉatu piṉṟaliyiliruntu mukattiṉ mēlākak kiyiṉāl taṭavikkoṭu mēlvayiṟu variyil vantu ataṉi aḻuttiṉār. iṅṅaṉam avar ceytavuṭaṉē avaṉ taṉ ariyil aḻuttik koṇṭirunta kikaḷi appuṟam eṭuttu, ammaruttuvar kikaḷip piṭittu aḻutti `nīṅkaḷē eṉ tāyum tantiyum, eṉakku nīṅkaḷ tirumpavum uyirik koṭuttīrkaḷ. eṉṟu collip perumūcceṟintāṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭu ammaruttuvar piṉṉum maṉaveḻucci uṭiyavarāy, aṟituyiliṟ celuttum muṟikaḷi mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē piṉṉum viṭāmaṟ ceyvārāyiṉar. piṟaku orumaṇi nērattilellām, tāṉ tūṅkavēṇṭumeṉṟum taṉ uṇarvukaḷ kalintu pōyiṉaveṉṟum colli vāyit tiṟantu koṇṭu tūṅkalāṉāṉ. avaṉ toṭarpāka viṭi colla iyalavilli. kaṇkaḷit tiṟakkumpaṭi kaṟpikkap paṭṭapōtu tiṟantu, eṅkum pukiyāy irukkiṟateṉṟum, evariyum terintukoḷḷak kūṭavilli yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ; avaṉ kaṇkaḷ appōtu oḷiyiṉṟi iruntaṉa; avaṉ kaṇṇiṟippikaḷ mikavum varuttattōṭu tiṟantaṉa. tuṉpattiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ avaṉiviṭṭu ippōtu muḻutum akaṉṟaṉa; avaṉ kikaḷ ariyi aḻuttuvativiṭṭu avaṉatu mārpiṉmēṟ kuṟukkiṭṭuk kiṭantaṉa. avaṉ ippōtu amitiyāṉa nalla uṟakkattil irukkiṉṟāṉ eṉpati avaṉatu mukam viḷaṅkak kāṭṭiṟṟu. ammaruttuvar palavaṟṟippaṟṟi viṉaviyum, avaṉ peyaric colli urakkakkūviyum avaṉ ētoṉṟum aṟiyavilli.
ataṉmēl avveḷḷikkāra maruttuvar avaṉik kiḷḷip pārttum avaṉ aciyavilli; ataṉpiṉ oru kuṇṭūciyi varuvittut, tamakku utaviyāy niṟkum vaṅkāḷi maruttuvari uṟṟunōkkumpaṭi kaṟpittu, avvūciyi annōyāḷiyiṉ iṭuppiṉ piṉpuṟattē kutti nuḻittār. appōtum avaṉ ciṟitum aciyavilli. piṉṉar avvutavi maruttuvar avvūciyip piṭuṅki avaṉuṭampiṉ palaviṭaṅ kaḷiṉuṅ kuttikkuttip pārttār. appaṭic ceytum avaṉukkuc ciṟitum nōy uṇṭāṉatākat teriyavilli. avaṉ ivaṟṟi eḷḷaḷavum uṇarāmal nāṟkāli mutukiṉ kūrāṉa mēlōrattiṉmēl taṉ kaḻutti vittukkoṇṭu naṉṟāy uṟaṅkuvāṉāṉāṉ.
atip pārttatum, tammāl aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭṭu niṉivaṟṟut tūṅkum avaṉatu tuyiliṉ iyalpik kaṟṟār cilarkku meyppittuk kāṭṭum poruṭṭu atturimakaṉār tamatu toḻiṟcālikkup pōy aṟaṅkūṟuvōr iruvari aḻittuvantār. avar tirumpi vantapōtum, avaṉi avar muṉ viṭṭup pōṉa nilimiyilēyē avaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ kātaṇṭi pōy evvaḷavō urakkak kattiyum avaṉ viḻittu eḻuntilaṉ. piṉpu neruppik koṇṭuvantu avaṉ muḻaṅkāliṉmēl vittārkaḷ, ataṉiyum avaṉ uṇarntilaṉ. ataṉpiṉ, eṭukkiyilēyē taṅkaḷ kaṇkaḷil nīri varuvitta navaccāratti avarkaḷ ciṟitu nēram avaṉ mūkkiṟ piṭittārkaḷ; atilum avaṉ kaṇṇiṟippikūṭa aciyavilli. āṉāṟ ciṟitunērattiṟkuppiṉ avaṉ ciṟitu uṇarvuvarap peṟṟut tali acittatik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, ammaruttuvar `uṉakku ētēṉum parukutaṟku vēṇṭumā? eṉak kēṭṭaṉar.
ataṟkavaṉ vāyit tiṟantataṉāl, navaccāratti mikavuṅ kāramākak kalantatoru nīriṉi avaṉukkup parukakkoṭuttār. aṅkirunta maṟṟavarkaḷāṟ ciṟitu cuvittup pārttaṟkum ākāta kāram uṭiya annīriṉi avaṉ pāl kuṭippatu pōṟ kuṭittuviṭṭu, iṉṉum vēṇṭumeṉṟu vāyip piḷantāṉ. ataṟkup piṉṉar, mēṟkūriyi nōkkiya paṭiyāy irunta avaṉatu taliyi avar nimirtti nallatoru viḷakkiṉ oḷikku nērēvittu, avaṉṟaṉ kaṇkaḷi oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākat tiṟantuviṭṭār. āṉāl, aṅkuḷḷa avviḷakkiṉ oḷi kuruṭaṉ kaṇkaḷiṟ paṭuvatu pōliruntatēyallāmal, vēṟētu payaṉiyum avaṉ kaṇkaḷukkut tantilatu. ivvāṟellām ammaruttuvarum avaruṭaṉ iruntōrum ārāyntu pārkkavē, avaṉuṭiya aimpoṟikaḷum muḻutum uṇarvaṟṟa niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉpati avarkaḷ ellārum naṉku teḷintu koṇṭārkaḷ. ataṉmēl, avaṉi oru paṭukkimēl nīḷap paṭukka vittārkaḷ. kāli patiṉoru maṇikkut tuyiṟṟappaṭṭa annōyāḷi piṟpakal mūṉṟu maṇikku mukattilē taṇṇīr teḷittavuṭaṉ attuyil nīṅki eḻuntāṉ. avaṉ teḷinta mukattōṭu cemmiyāy iruntatik kaṇṭu ammaruttuvar, tammuṭaṉ irunta kaṟṟōr muṉṉiliyil avaṉip piṉvarumāṟu viṉavi viṭipeṟṟār.
`ippōtu eppaṭi irukkiṟāy? `mikavuñ cemmiyāy irukkiṉṟēṉ.
`toṇṭiyilāvatu vēṟeṅkāvatu nōvirukkiṉṟatā? `toṇṭiyilmaṭṭuñ ciṟitu varuttam irukkiṉṟatu, maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil nōy illi.
`iṉṟikku uṉakku yātu nērntatu? `eṉatu vīṅkiya vitiyil uḷḷanīri veḷi eṭukkum poruṭṭu imparā maruttuvak kaḻakattiṟku iṉṟu kāliyiṟ ceṉṟēṉ.
`aṅṅaṉamē nīr veḷiyē eṭukkappaṭṭatā? `ām.
`vitiyi aṟutta piṟaku nērntatu niṉivil irukkiṉṟatā? `uṭaṉē yāṉ nallatūkkattiṟkup pōṉēṉ, vēṟoṉṟum eṉakku niṉivil illi.
`aṟutta piṉpu ētāvatu uṇṭaṉiyā allatu parukiṉiyā? `nāṉ viṭāy koṇṭiruntēṉ; āṉāl, nāṭṭu maruttuvar eṉṉi eḻuppukiṟa variyil nāṉ pakarukuvataṟku ētum kiṭikkavilli.
`yārāvatu uṉṉi ūciyāṟ kuttiṉārkaḷā?’ `allatu neruppāṟ cuṭṭārkaḷā? `illi, illi.
`uṉakku veṟuppāṉa etaṉiyēṉum mukantaṉiyā? `illi.
`nī tūṅkumpōtu iṉpamāyt tūṅkiṉiyā? `mikavum iṉpamāyt tūṅkiṉēṉ.
`nī tūṅkiyapōtu ētēṉum ōci kēṭṭaṉiyā? `kural olikaḷ kēṭṭaṉa, āṉāl, avikaḷi nāṉ tiṭṭamāyt terintu koḷḷakkūṭavilli.
`maruttuvak kaḻakattil eṉṉit tavira vēṟu evariyēṉum pārttaṉiyā? `illi.
`nī tūkkattiṟkup pōṉapiṟaku uṉ vīṅkiya vitiyil ētēṉum nōviruntatā? `nāṉ eḻuntirukkum variyil nōvēyilli.
`anta iṭattil ippōtu ētēṉum nōvirukkiṟatā? `mikac ciṟiyanōvutāṉ irukkiṟatu.
`iṉṟikku uṉakku ettaṉimuṟi vayiṟu pōyiṟṟu? `maruttuvak kaḻakattiṟku varummuṉ nāṉku muṟi pōyiṟṟu, ataṉpiṟaku oṉṟumilli. muṉṉē iruntativiṭa ippōtu eṉvayiṟu ilēcāy irukkiṉṟatu.iṅṅaṉam avveḷḷikkāra maruttuvar viti vīṅkiṉa nōyāḷi oruvaṉi niṉivaṟṟa āḻnta tuyiliṟ pōkac ceytu avaṉ vitikaḷi aṟuttu nalappaṭuttiyapiṉ avaṉit takkōr palarmuṉṉiliyil vittu mēṟkāṭṭiyavāṟu kēṭṭup peṟṟa viṭikaḷiliruntu, ittakiya tūkkattiṟ ceṉṟōrkku evvakiyāṉa tuṉpamum terivatilli yeṉṟum, attūkkattiṟ ceṉṟu eḻuntapiṉ nōy nīṅki nalam uṇṭāmeṉṟum avaruṭaṉiruntōrellārum tam kaṇṇetirē kaṇṭu teḷiyap peṟṟārkaḷ. itaṉip pōlavē nikaḻnta iṉṉumpala uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷellām niṉivaṟṟa aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟōrkku uṇṭām. aḷaviṟanta nalaṅkaḷi aiyantiripuk kiṭaṉiṉṟi naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, mēṟkāṭṭiya niṉivaṟṟa tuyiliṉum āḻntu cellum mūṉṟāvatāṉa naṭittuyiliṉip paṟṟic ciṟitu ārāyntu pārppām. niṉivaṟṟa tuyiliṉum āḻntu celvatākiya tuyilil uyiriṉ aṟivik kavintuniṉṟa aṟiyāmiyiruḷ vilakippōka avvuyirkku iyaṟki aṟivuviḷakkam mikuntu tōṉṟā niṟkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu tōṉṟum iyaṟki aṟivukkuk kaṇ kātu mutalāṉa puṟakkaruvikaḷiṉ utavi ciṟitum vēṇṭappaṭuvatilli. kaṇ mutaliya appuṟak karuvikaḷiṉ vāyilāka oruvarkku aṟivu nikaḻumpōtu, avaṟṟiṉ etirē niṟkuṅ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kāṭukaḷ mutaliya maṟippukaḷāl avaṟivu taṭippaṭṭuniṉṟu avaṟṟiṟku appāluḷḷa poruḷkaḷi aṟiyamāṭṭātākum. āṉāl, iviyaṟkiyaṟivu viḷakkattiyō puṟattēyuḷḷa evvakipporuḷum ciṟitum maṟikkavallaṉa alla. iḵtu āyiraṅkātavaḻikku appāl uḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyum aṅkē nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷiyum eḷitilē aṟiyavallatākum. niṉivōṭirukkiyiṟ ceyyamāṭṭāmal muḻutum viṭappaṭṭavikaḷiyum, arikuṟiyāy viṭappaṭṭavi kaḷiyum niṉiviḻanta ivvaṟituyiliṟ ṟōṉṟi viḷaṅkum aṟivu eḷitilē tiṟampaṭacceytu muṭittirukkiṉṟatu. ittuyilil iruppavarkaṭkuk kaṇkaḷ mūṭiyiruntum kaṇṇāṟkaṇṭu naṭappavarkaḷiṉun tiṟamiyāka avarkaḷ naṭantu celkiṉṟaṉar. tāṇṭa iyalāta paḷḷaṅkaḷiyellām tāṇṭiviṭukiṉṟaṉar, ēṟamuṭiyāta cuvarkaḷ kōpuraṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟilellām uṭampiṟ ciṟituṅ kāyampaṭāmal ēṟiyiṟaṅki viṭukiṉṟaṉar. viyappāṉa innaṭittuyiliṉ ittakiya aruñceyalkaḷukku uṇmiyāka nikaḻnta cila nikaḻccikaḷi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām.
ittāliyā nāṭṭiṟ peyar peṟṟa icippāṇar oruvar (Tartini) tūṅkikkoṇṭirukkiyiṟ `pēy nilippāṭṭu eṉṉuñ ciṟantatoru patikatti iyaṟṟiṉār. celva vāḻkkiyiṟ ciṟanta oruvar (Lord Thurlow) tamatu iḷantip paruvattiṟ kallūriyiṟ kalipayiṉṟu koṇṭiruntapōtu orunāḷmālik kālattil ilattīṉ moḻiyiṟ patikam oṉṟu iyaṟṟappukuntu ataṉi muṭittaṟku evvaḷavō muyaṉṟum muṭikkamāṭṭāmal, aṉṟiravu uṟakkattiṟkuc ceṉṟār. uṟakkattiṟ ceṉṟatum, muṭiyātuviṭṭa appatikattit tiṟampaṭa muṭittu, viḻitteḻuntapiṉ ataṉi niṉivu kūrnteḻuti vittaṉar. appatikattip piṟaku kaṇṭa aṟivuṭiyōrellām ataṉ coṟcuvi poruṭṭu cuvikaḷiyum aḻakiya amippiyum mikaviyantu pārāṭṭiṉar. mēlum, iṉṉicik kalviyil tērccipeṟum poruṭṭup payiṉṟa māṇākkar palaril oruvar iravilē tūṅkik koṇṭirukkiyil iṭiyil attūkka mayakkattuṭaṉē eḻuntu icippeṭṭi (Piano) yaṇṭi ceṉṟu ataṉic cevviyāy iyakki atiṟ pāṭutaṟkuk kuṟittuvikkappaṭṭa pāṭṭip piḻipaṭāmal iṉitāyp pāṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
tūṅkik koṇṭirukkum niliyil avaṉpāṟ kāṇappaṭṭa innuṇṇaṟivu viḷakkattip pārttu viyanta avaṉṟaṉ uṭaṉmāṇākkar cilar orunāḷiravu avvicip peṭṭimēṟ kuṟittuvikkappaṭṭa pāṭṭik kīḻatumēlatāyp puraṭṭi vittukkoṇṭu avaṉ yātuceyyap pōkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu viḻittup pārttapaṭiyāy iruntaṉar. āṉāl, tūkkattiṉ iṭiyē naṭakkum ammāṇākkaṉō vaḻakkampōl icippeṭṭiyiṉiṭam ceṉṟavuṭaṉ, icippāṭṭuk kaṭitam puraṭṭivikkap paṭṭiruttali eppaṭiyōterintu, ataṉi yeṭuttu, vikkavēṇṭiya muṟippaṭi vittukkoṇṭu peṭṭiyi iyakkic cevviyāyp pāṭiṉāṉ. iṉṉum, ōr iḷiya peṇpiḷḷi naḷḷiravil tūkkattiṉ naṭuvē eḻuntu, muḻu iruḷil mūṭiyakaṇ mūṭiyapaṭiyāy irukka, aḻakākavum aṟivākavuṅ kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻuti vippāḷ. ivviṭattil viyakkattakkatu maṟṟoṉṟu uṇṭu. naḷḷiraviṉ iruḷil kaṇmūṭiyavāṟē avaḷ eḻutikkoṇṭu pōkiyil ētēṉum ciṟitu veḷiccam avaḷirukkum aṟikkuḷ nuḻintālum avaḷ uṭaṉē eḻuta muṭiyāmal niṉṟuviṭuvāḷ. muḻu iruḷil iruntāl maṭṭum avaḷ toṭarntu eḻutikkoṇṭu pōvāḷ. atuniṟka.
tamatu vīṭu nōkki vaḻicceṉṟa oruvar tām cellum vaḻiyiṉ ōramāy uḷḷa vēlikkuc ciṟitu appāl oru koṭi muntirip pantariṉmēl ucciyiṟ putumi yāṉatoṉṟu amarntirukkak kaṇṭār. appōtu naḷḷiruṭ kālam. uṭaṉē avar aṅkēyē niṉṟukoṇṭu ataṉi uṟṟu nōkkiṉār. aḵtu appantariṉmēl iyaṅkuvatupōṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu. avar ataṉōṭu pēcip pārttār; āṉāl aḵtu ētumē viṭicollavilli. annērattil ūrkāvaṟ cēvakar oruvar avvaḻiyē vantār. uṭaṉē avar accēvakari arukaḻittu avvuruvattik kāṭṭiṉār. ataṉik kaṇṭatum accēvakar `ō! aḵtoru peṇpiḷḷi eṉṟār. mj‰F k‰wt® `m¥goahdhš mtŸ ö¡f¤âš ïU¡fnt©L«? eṉṟu cērntu coṉṉār. iṅṅaṉam avvuruvattik kuṟittu oru muṭivukku vanta piṉ, avarkaḷ attōṭṭattiṉatu katavit tiṟantu koṇṭu uḷnuḻintu akkoṭi muntirip pantaraṇṭi pōy appeṇṇik kūppiṭṭup pārttārkaḷ; āṉāl, viṭi kiṭikkavilli. ataṉmēl avvūr kāvaṟcēvakar appantariṉmēl ēṟalāṉār.
ataṉāl appeṇ avviṭattiviṭṭu mikavuṅ kiḷarcciyōṭum ataṉmēl nīḷa ōṭi appantariṉ ōrattiṟ ceṉṟu, aṅkiruntu eṭṭaṭi vilakiyirunta vēṟoru marappantariṉmēl orē kutiyil tāvi ēṟiṉāḷ. iv aruñceyal avarkaḷip peritum viyappuṟuttiṉamiyāl, avarkaḷ attōṭṭattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa vīṭṭārippōy eḻuppiviṭṭu, avarkaḷatu marappantaliṉmēl tūkkattil naṭappavaḷ orutti irukkiṉṟāḷ eṉat terivittaṉar. avarkaḷellārum vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyē vantu, ellārumāy avaḷip piṉṟoṭarntu ōṭiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam piṉṟoṭarntu varutalum appeṇ piṉṉum virivāy am marap pantaliṉmēl ōṭi, attōṭṭattu māḷikiyiṉ tāḻvārattu uccimēl tāviṉāḷ. piṟaku avaḷ aṅkiruntu ōṭi avvīṭṭiṉ naṭumēlucciyiṟ ceṉṟu, avviṭattirunta (Chimney) pukippōkkiyiṉmēṟ cāyntu koṇṭu niṉṟāḷ. kaṭiciyāka, ōr ēṇiyik koṇarntu cārtti avvīṭṭukkuriyavarkaḷil ōr iḷiñaṉ akkūriyiṉ oru pakkamāy ēṟi ataṉmēṟ cella, aṅkellām paṉikkaṭṭi mikutiyāy mūṭiyiruntamiyiṉ avaṉ appeṇṇaṇṭi pōkakkūṭātavaṉāṉāṉ.
ataṉāl avaṉ kīḻiṟaṅki vantu, ēṇiyi avvīṭṭiṉ mukappiṟ koṇṭu pōyc cārtta, ippōtu accēvakar ataṉmēl ēṟi appeṇṇaṇṭi yiṟ pōka, avar kiṭṭavarum variyil cummā irunta appeṇ avar taṉpāl aṇukak kaṇṭatum maṟupaṭiyum appakkat tāḻvārattu ucciyiṉmēlum, ataṉpiṉ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy avvīṭṭuk kūriyiṉ mēlum ōṭi, aṅkiruntu tirumpavuṅ koṭi muntirippantariṉmēl tāvi ēṟip, piṟaku avvāṇmakkaḷ taṉkiṭṭa varutaṟkumuṉ nilattē kutittu attōṭṭattiṉ piṉ vāyilvaḻiyē nuḻintu veḷiyē puṟappaṭṭu oru perun teruviṉ vaḻiyē ōṭalāṉāḷ. uṭaṉē avarkaḷellārum avaḷ piṉṉē mika virintōṭi ōriṭattē avaḷip piṭittārkaḷ. piṭitta piṉ avaḷi iṉṉāḷeṉṟu terintu avaḷuṟaviṉari varuvittu avarkaḷiṭam avaḷi oppu vittaṉar. appeṇ tūkkattil iṅṅaṉam veḷinaṭantu tirintatu iraṇṭām muṟiyāku meṉṟu avaḷuṟaviṉar terivittaṉar. ivvāṟellām uyaramāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟit tirintum avaḷuṭampil oruciṟu kāyaṅkūṭa illāmiyik kaṇṭu ellārum nirampa viyappaṭintaṉar.
iṉi, innaṭittuyiliṉkaṇ oruvarkku viḷaṅkum aṟivāṉatu neṭuntolivil nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷiyum ētoru karuviyiṉ utaviyiyum vēṇṭātu eḷitil aṟiyavallatā yirukkiṉṟateṉpataṟku uṇmiyāy nikaḻnta maṟṟoru nikaḻcciyiṉi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟām. 1995ām āṇṭil, pampāy nakarattiṟku iraṇṭukal tolivil uḷḷa kolāpā eṉṉum iṭattil ōr āṅkilappaṭit talivarum avartam maṉiviyārum taṅkiyiruntaṉar. avvammiyār yōkanittiriyip paṟṟiya pala nūlkaḷik kaṟṟuvantamiyi liruntu, am muṟiyi ārāyntu pārppatil aḷaviṟanta vēṭkiyuṭiyarāy, ataṟkut takkatōr āḷittēṭat, tam kuḻantikaḷukkuk kittāyāka vikkappaṭṭavaḷ aṟituyilukku mikavum poruttamuṭiyavaḷākak kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭāḷ. aṅṅaṉam kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭa avaḷi avvammiyār aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu, taṇṇīr nirappiya oru kaṇṇāṭik kuvaḷiyi oru mēcimēl vittu, atil uḷḷa nīri uṟṟunōkki aṅkē tōṉṟukiṉṟa tōṟṟaṅkaḷit terivikkumpaṭi ceyvar.
ivvāṟu ceytu, toliviluḷḷa tam uṟṟār uṟaviṉar naṇparpāl nikaḻvaṉavaṟṟiyellān teḷivāka aṟintu vantār. itiṟ paḻakkappaṭuttiya ap peṇpiḷḷi aṟivillātavaḷ allaḷ; āṅkilam naṉṟāyp pēcavum eḻutavum paṭikkavum cevviyāyk kaṟpikkap paṭṭavaḷ. ivaḷ iṅṅaṉam pārttuc colliya toli nikaḻccikaḷellām nāḷvaḻiyē muṟṟum uṇmikaḷāy muṭiyak kaṇṭu avvāṅkila mātarārkku avaḷatu teḷivuk kāṭciyil muḻunampikki uṇṭāyiruntatu. iḵtippaṭiyirukka, orunāṭ kāliyil perumpaṭit talivar oruvari varavēṟṟaṟ poruṭṭu avvammiyāriṉ kaṇavar nērattōṭu uṇa veṭukkiyil, tam ēvalāḷik kūppiṭṭut taṉṉuṭuppukaḷi virinteṭuttu oḻuṅkupaṭutti vikkac coṉṉār. aṅṅaṉamē ceyyac ceṉṟa avaṉ ciṟitu nēraṅkaḻintapiṉ vantu tikippum accamum uṭiyavaṉāyp `perumāṉē! paṇappi uḷḷa arippaṭṭi kiyimaṭṭuṅ kāṇōm eṉṟu vāykuḻaṟik kūṟiṉāṉ. mj‰ft® `kl¤jdkhŒ¥ ngrhnj, Ú btsthiy¥ ngh‰ f© FUlhÆU¡f nt©L«! eṉṟu kūvic collik koṇṭē tam irukkiyiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu uṭuppaṟikkuṭ ceṉṟār.
avar ataṉuṭ ceṉṟu tēṭip pārttum ataṉik kāṇāmiyiṉ mikavuñ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu tam ēvalāṭkaḷi yellām viya, avarkaḷ tām ataṉi eṭukkavilli yeṉṟu mikka accattōṭum keñcik kūṟiṉar. ataṉpiṉ avar uṇavukoḷḷum aṟikkut tirumpi vantu, tam maṉiviyip pārttu `ippōtu uṉ tōḻikkuḷḷa teḷivuk kāṭciyiṉ uṇmiyi nāṉ terintu koḷvataṟkut takka amayam kiṭittirukkiṉṟatu. avaḷi iṅkē varuvittuk kāṇāmaṟ pōṉa eṉatu arippaṭṭikiyik kaṇṭeṭukkum paṭi cey eṉṟār. ataṉ mēl, avvammiyār attōḻiyi aṅkē varuvikka, avaḷ eṅkē taṉmēṟ kuṟṟañcāṭṭap pōkiṉṟārkaḷō veṉṟu naṭukkattōṭum vantāḷ. āṉāl, anta mātarār, ceyyavēṇṭuvatu iṉṉateṉṟu terivittut, tiruṭaṉ iṉṉāṉeṉṟu kaṇṭa piṉṉum avaḷi evarkkuṅ kāṭṭik koṭāmal tāmē ellāvaṟṟiyum amitiyuṟa muṭippatāka uṟuti coṉṉār.
ataṉpiṉ, oru kaṇṇāṭik kuvaḷiyil taṇṇīri nirappi ataṉit tamatu iṭatu kimmēl vittukkoṇṭu, valatu kiyāl ataṉmēl palamuṟi taṭavi atil uḷḷa nīrukkut taṉatu uyirppiṉi ēṟṟiyapiṉ, ataṉi oru mēcimēl vittu attōḻi ataṉi uṟṟup pārttu aṅkē tōṉṟuvatu iṉṉateṉṟu collumpaṭi kaṟpittār. avaḷ aṅṅaṉamē mukattik kuṉintu ataṉi uṟṟunōkkuvāḷāṉāḷ. ciṟitu nēram āṉatum ammātarār avaḷi nōkkip `peṇṇē! ētāvatu kāṇkiṉṟaṉiyā? eṉṟu kēṭka, `oṉṟum illi eṉṟu viṭi kūṟiṉāḷ. ataṉmēl avvammiyār avaḷ taliyiṉmēlum kaḻuttiṉ piṉpuṟattum putitākat taṭavi avaḷi iṉṉum āḻnta tuyiliṟ pōkacceytu kēṭṭum, avaḷ `oṉṟumē tōṉṟavilli eṉṟāḷ. ataṉāl, avaḷ ittaṉi kālamākat tammi ēmāṟṟit tāṉ vantaṉaḷō eṉṟu avvammiyār mutalil aiyuṟavu koṇṭār.
tirumpavum ammātarārkku maṟṟoṉṟu maṉattil teṉpaṭṭatu. paṭavē, vēṟoru muṟiyāl avaḷi muyaṉṟu pārkkat tuvaṅkiṉār. `peṇṇē, kaṭiciyāka nam talivar arippaṭṭiki aṇintirunta nāḷi uṟṟup pār eṉṟu kaṟpittār. avaḷ ciṟitu nēram cummā iruntu, `nāṉ ippōtu nam talivarik kāṇkiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu kaṉavu mayakkattōṭu kūṟiṉāḷ. tirumpavum `avar ippōtu uṭi uṭuttukiṟār; ippōtu paṭikkōlam pūṇkiṉṟār! ippōtu arippaṭṭiki aṇikiṉṟār. ā! ippōtu avar aṟiyi viṭṭup pōkiṉṟār eṉṟāḷ. uṭaṉē ammātarār `viṭātē avarip piṉṟoṭarntu cel eṉṟu uṟutiyāyc coṉṉār. `namtalivar kutirimēl ēṟukiṉṟār; itō ataṉi ūrntu celkiṉṟār! eṉṟu kūviṉāḷ. piṉṉum avvammiyār `ōr imippoḻutum avariviṭātē eṉṟu kūviṉār. `aiyō! avar mikavum virivāyc celkiṉṟārē! eṉakkō kaḷippāy irukkiṉṟatu! eṉṟu appeṇ perumūcceṟintāḷ. `viṭātēcel eṉṟu piṉṉum attaliviyār kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭār. `nam talivar ippōtu iṉṉum maṟṟat talivarkaḷōṭu irukkiṟār. ettaṉiyō paṭiyāṭkaḷum kuṭimakkaḷum niṟintirukkiṟārkaḷ. ētō oru periya vēṭikki naṭakkiṉṟatu. yārō oru periya talivar payaṇamāyp pōkiṟār.avarkaḷ ellārum taṇṇirukku arukē niṟkiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟu appeṇ kūṟiṉāḷ. `appaṭiyāṉāṟ ciṟitunēram ōyvāy iru. āṉāl, niṉ kaṇkaḷ nam talivariviṭṭu ippuṟam tirumpātirukkaṭṭum eṉṟu attaliviyār colliṉar. avvāṟē avaḷ ciṟitu nēram pēcātiruntu piṉṉart `taṇṇīrkku arukiluḷḷa oru periya vīṭṭiṉuḷḷē nam talivar nuḻikiṉṟār. avar ippōtu ōr uṭuppu aṟikkuṭ celkiṉṟār. tammuṭiya uṭikaḷi māṟṟukiṉṟār. avartam paṭikkōla uṭikaḷellām oru takarap peṭṭi yiṉuḷḷē vikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl, paṇappiyuḷḷa avaratu arippaṭṭiki maṭṭum veḷiyē viṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; kaṭalukku aṇittāyuḷḷa avvīṭṭiṉ uṭukkum aṟiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa oru muḷiyilē atu toṅkukiṉṟatu, eṉṟu moḻintāḷ.
ataṉik kēṭṭatum avvammiyiṉ kaṇavar `ō! atu tāṉ yāṭ viṭuti eṉṟurittut tam ēvalāḷi yaḻittu `uṭaṉē ōr āḷiyaṉuppi, av arippaṭṭiki aṅkē viṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatāveṉṟu pārttu varaccey eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭār. avaṉum maṟṟuñ cilarōṭu avviṭattiṟkup pōyiṉāḷ.
appaṭittalivar `uṇmiyākavē yāṉ ataṉi yāṭviṭutiyil viṭṭu viṭṭēṉō veṉṟu viyappuṟu kiṉṟēṉ. yāṉ kaṭiciyāka ataṉi aṇintiruntatu rippaṉperumāṉ iṅkilāntiṟkup puṟappaṭṭuppōṉa aṉṟikkēyām, eṉṟu muṉakiṉār.
ataṟkanta ammiyār `nām virivilataṉ uṇmiyik kāṇpōm. avvarippaṭṭiki aṅkē kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭum eṉpatil eṉakkuc ciṟitum aiyam illi eṉṟār.
ciṟitu nērattilellām pōṉa ēvalāḷum avaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟōrun tirumpivarum aravam kēṭṭatu. avarkaḷ varum ōciyiliruntu apporuḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭateṉṉum uṇmi avvammiyārkkut tiṇṇamāyiṟṟu. avvēvalaṉ anta arip paṭṭikiyit talikkumēṟ piṭittukkoṇṭu, avarkaḷ irunta mēṉmāḷikikku irikka irikka ōṭivantu, attōḻip peṇ coṉṉavāṟē atu kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭateṉat terivittāṉ.
iṉṉōraṉṉa uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷāl, naṭittuyiliṉ kaṇ oruvarkkuḷḷa aṟivu viḷakkamāṉatu ētoru karuvi yiṉutaviyiyum vēṇṭātu tāṉākavē evviṭattiṉum ekkālattiṉum nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷiyellām aṟiyavalla tām uṇmi naṉku viḷaṅkāniṟkum. iṉi, immūvakit tuyiliyum payaṉpaṭuttikkoḷḷum muṟikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām.
14. payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷ
niṉivuttuyiliṉkaṇ niṉivumāṟāmal iruttalāl itaṉāṟcila eḷiya payaṉkaḷittām peṟutalkūṭum. ittuyilil iruppōr tuyiṟṟuvōr pāl niṉivu oṉṟu porunti niṟpar; avar collukiṟapaṭiyē ceyvārallāmal avarkku etiriṭiyāṉatu oṉṟuñceyyār. tuyiṟṟuvōr avar kaṇkaḷimūṭi `ivikaḷi nummāl tiṟakka muṭiyātu eṉṟu coṉṉāl avar aṅṅaṉamē avaṟṟit tiṟakka māṭṭāmal iruppār. avaratu vāyimūṭi `numatu peyarik kēṭṭāl, ataṉic collamāṭṭāmal vaṟitē iruppīr eṉṟu coṉṉāl avar appaṭiyē iruppar. veṅkāyattik kiyiṟkoṭuttu `itu nalla maṇam uḷḷatoru mullippū, itaṉi mukantupārum eṉṟāl avvāṟē ataṉi mukantu makiḻvar. iṉiya maṇaṅ kamaḻuñ ceṅkaḻunīrppū voṉṟik kiyiṟṟantu, `itu maṇam illāta erukkampū eṉṟu moḻintāl, uṭaṉē avar ataṉi mukantupārttu atu maṇamillātateṉṟu aruvaruppuṟuvar. avaratu kiyip piṭittu atil ōriṭattik kuṟittuk kāṭṭi `ivviṭattil iratta ōṭṭam irātu, iṅkē uṇarcci irātu, nōvum irātu eṉṟu uṟutticcolliyapiṉ oru kuṇṭūciyi eṭuttu avviṭattē mēṟcatiyiṟ cerukiṉāl avar ataṉic ciṟitum uṇarār; piṟaku ataṉip piṭuṅkiviṭṭu avviṭatti viralāl tēyttu viṭṭāl aṅkē ōr aṭiyāḷamuṅ kāṇappaṭātu. iṅṅaṉamē, ittuyilil iṉṉum pala vēṭikkikaḷi yellām ceytukāṭṭalām. ittuyilil iruppōrkkuk kaṇkaḷ mūṭiyum irukkalām. tiṟantum irukkalām. tiṟantirukkumāyiṉ tuyiṟṟuvōr kaṭpārviyum ivaratu kaṭpārviyum oṉṟiyoṉṟu uṟṟunōkkiya paṭiyāy iruttal vēṇṭum. mūṭiyirukkumāyiṉ tuyiṟṟuvōr tammuṭiya coṟkaḷik koṇṭu avaratu niṉivit tamvaḻippaṭuttik koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, ittuyil ciṟu nōykaḷit tīrttaṟku ēṟṟatoṉṟāy iruttalāl talivali cevikkuttal kaṇṇōy palvali uṭampunōy kāyccal tēḷkoṭṭiṉa vali mutaliyavaṟṟi itaṉutaviyāṟ pōkkalām. āṉāl, itaṉutaviyāl nōykaḷi nīkka virumpukiṟavar uṇmiyākavē aṉpum aruḷum nalleṇṇamuñ cevviyāṉa uṭampum uṭiyavarāka iruttal vēṇṭum. ivviṉiya iyalpukaḷ uṭiyavar oruvari aṟituyiliṟ pōkacceytu avarit toṭṭu `innōy nīṅkuka eṉṟu coṉṉa aḷavilē, aḵtu avari viṭṭu nīṅkutal tiṇṇam. itaṟkut tāyāṉavaḷ kuḻantiyip pōṟṟum muṟiyē takka cāṉṟām. kīḻē tavaṟiviḻuntu kāyampaṭṭa ciṟumakavit tāy pataṟieṭuttu, akkāyattit tuṭittuk kiyāl mellattaṭavi āṟutal moḻikaḷāl ataṟku attuṉpattip pōkkit taṉmaṭimēl ataṉit tūṅkavittali nām uṟṟunōkkumpōtu, avaḷ ivvaṟituyilukkuriya muṟikaḷit taṉṉiyaṟiyāmalē iṉitu ceykiṉṟāḷ eṉa uṇarap peṟukiṉṟōm allamō? 
aṉpumikutiyum uṭiyārkku iṉiya collum melleṉṟa ceyalun tāmākavē varutal iyaṟki. ātalāṟ, piṟarkkuḷḷa nōy tīrttaṟ poruṭṭu avarpāl aṟituyiliṉi varuvittaṟkup paḻakuvōr malarnta mukamum iṉiya collum iṉiya ceyalum nōyaṟṟa uṭampum uṭiyavarāyirukka mutaliṟ paḻakikkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. ataṉōṭu, nōy koṇṭavarkku avvannōy nīṅkutaṟku ēṟṟa tūya uṇavukaḷi māṟṟi māṟṟik koṭukkum vakikaḷiyum, avar aḻukkāṉa āṭikaḷi aṇiyāmalum nalla kāṟṟum veḷiccamum illāmaṟ kuppi kūḷaṅkaḷ malinta aḻukkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil irāmalum tammit tuppuravāy vittuk koḷḷum muṟikaḷiyum eṭuttuc collutal vēṇṭum. ikkālattu maruttuvarkaḷ kāppi tēyili kōkkō iṟicci mutaliya nañcāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷi avittu iṟutta cāṟṟiyum, muṭṭi oyiṉ mutalāṉa poruḷ kaḷiyum, naccuk kāṟṟu aṭaṅkiya cōṭāt taṇṇīriyum nōyāḷikaḷukku ērāḷamāyk koṭuttu varukiṟārkaḷ.
ivi attaṉiyum nōyi mikutip paṭuttuvaṉavē yallāmal ataṉit taṇippaṉa alla eṉṟu ippaṇṭaṅkaḷiṟ kalantuḷḷa nañci naṉku ārāyntaṟinta pukaḻpeṟṟa āṅkila maruttuvarkaḷē (see for instance Dr. A. Haig’ Uric Acid) kūṟivarutalāl, ivvuṇmiyi aṟiyāmal ivaṟṟi nōyāḷikaṭkuk koṭuttal peruntīmikkē vaḻiyām. ākiyāl, aṟituyil muṟiyāl nōy nīkkap pukuvōr annōyi nīkkukiyil annōykku mēlumēlum iṭañceyyum ittakiya poruḷkaḷi uṇavākak koṭāmal taṭi ceytal vēṇṭum. atu niṟka.
iṉi, nōyāḷiyi inniṉivut tuyiliṟ pōkac ceytavuṭaṉ, nōyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷik kiyāl toṭṭēṉum toṭāmalēṉum kīḻnōkkip palakāl taṭavutal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉan taṭavuṅkāl aintāvatāṉa kaṭic ciṟuviralic ciṟitu akaṟṟi, maṟṟi nāṉku viralkaḷiyum oruṅku cērttu iraṇṭu kikaḷāluṅ kīḻnōkkit taṭavuka. mēliruntu taṭavik kīḻ iṟakkiṉa kiviralkaḷi utaṟiviṭṭut, tirumpavum pakkac cāyvāyk kiviralkaḷi mēl uyartti muṉpōlavē kīḻnōkkit taṭavi viralkaḷi utaṟuka. viralkaḷi iṅṅaṉam utaṟutal ēṉ eṉṟāl, nōyāḷiyiṉ nōyik kīḻ iṟakkiyavuṭaṉ annōy itaṉi nīkkuvōr kiviral nuṉikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē paṟṟik koṇṭu avaratu uṭampil ēṟātiruttaṟ poruṭṭēyām. iṅṅaṉam palakāṟ ṟaṭaviyapiṉ nōyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷiṉmēl vāyiṉāl kīḻnōkki mella ūti melliya vāykkāṟṟu avaṟṟiṟ paṭumpaṭi ceyka. vāyiṉāl ivvāṟu nērē ūtuvatōṭu, melliya tūyatuṇiyi eṭṭu maṭippukaḷāka maṭittu nōyuṟṟa iṭattiṉmēl vittup, piṟaku ittuṇiyiṉmēl vāyi vittuk katakatappu uṇṭākumāṟum ūtutal cila nōykaḷukkuk kaṭṭāyam ceyyappaṭutal vēṇṭum.
kaḻalikaḷ, kaṭṭikaḷ, vīkkaṅkaḷ, narampuc cuḷukkukaḷ, erivukaḷ uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil ippaṭic ceytal iṉṟiyami yātatākum. orukāl vāyi vittu ūtiyapiṉ, maṟupaṭiyuṅ kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttē ūtavēṇṭumātalāl, vāyi annōyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil vittapaṭiyākavē mūcci uḷḷiḻuttal ciṟitum ākātu. mukattit tūyakāṟṟuvarum appakkamāyt tiruppi mūcci uḷḷiḻuttuk koṇṭu maṟupaṭiyum ūtuka. `innōy tīrtalvēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇam muṉittu niṟka iṅṅaṉam ūtuvatāl nōy tīrppōratu eṇṇattāl untappaṭum miṉ āṟṟal avaratu mūcciṉ vaḻiyōṭi nōyāḷikkuḷḷa annōykaḷi vilakac ceyyum. ivaṟṟōṭu `innōy ummiviṭṭu - akaṉṟatu. umatu uṭampiṉuḷḷē - tūya irattam - paravukiṉṟatu. ummuṭiya narampukaḷ - valivaṭikiṉṟaṉa. nīr - uḷḷak kiḷarcci - uṭiyavarākiṉṟīr. nīr - ittuyilil - naṉṟāyt - tūṅki - eḻuntatum - umatu uṭampu - cevviyākavum - umatu uḷḷam - kiḷarcciyākavum - irukkak kāṇpīr, eṉṟu palakāl āḻnta melliya kuralil tiruppit tiruppic colluka. ivvāṟellām ceytu, avari naṉṟāyt tūṅki eḻumpaṭi kaṟpittu viṭṭup pōṉāl, avar avvāṟē ayarntuṟaṅki eḻuntu, tamakkirunta nōy nīṅkippōkak kaṇṭu viyantu makiḻvar.
mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭiya ciṟu nōykaḷellām, mēṟkūṟiya muṟippaṭi oru poḻutu allatu iraṇṭu mūṉṟupoḻutu ceyta aḷavāṉē nīṅkum. nāṭpaṭṭa kuḷir kāyccal, naccuk kāyccal, caḷikkāyccal, vayiṟṟu vali, kuṇṭikkāy vali, īral vali, neñcaṭippu, paṭi, puṇ, toḻunōy, poruttup piṭippu, eṉpurukki, maṇṭikkuṭiccal, piḷavi, irumal mutalāṉa kaṭunōykaḷukkō cilakāl mēṟkūṟiya muṟikaḷic ceytal pōtātu. palanāḷum palakālum mēṟkūṟiya muṟikaḷit toṭarpākac ceytuvaral vēṇṭum. nōy nīkkuvōrkku annōykaḷ toṟṟikkoḷḷātapaṭi avar tammit tūymi ceytu koṇṭu mūccup payiṟci ceytuvaral vēṇṭum. ikkaṭu nōykoṇṭa nōyāḷikaṭkut tīṉikkuṭal, malakkuṭal, viyarvit tuḷikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi aṭikkaṭi kaḻuvit tuppuravu ceytalōṭu, tūyavuṇavum tūyanīruṅ kāṟṟum uṭkoḷḷumpaṭiyum ceytal vēṇṭum. iṉṉum nalla maruntukaḷ uṭkoṭuttaṟkum piṉ vāṅkalākātu. atu niṟka.
iṉi, niṉivaṟṟa āḻnta tuyilil iruppōrkkup puṟattē cuṟṟilum naṭappatu oṉṟun teriyāteṉpati muṉṉarē kāṭṭiṉām. ittūkkattiliruppōrkku itu kaliyāmalē, ivarkaḷi viḻittu uṇarvupeṟumāṟu ceytu pala putumikaḷi viḷivittuk kāṭṭuvatuṭaṉ, tīrttaṟkariya koṭu nōykaḷiyum tīrttuviṭalām. iyaṟkiyāṉa viḻippukku varāmalē kaṇkaḷit tiṟantu, tuyiṟṟuvōr colvaṉavaṟṟi uṇarntu ceyyumpaṭi ittūkkattil oruvaric celuttuki yilēyē kaṟpittu oḻuṅku ceytu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. mēṟkūṟiya niṉivut tuyilil iruppōrip piṉvarumāṟu ceytu inniṉivaṟṟa tuyiliṟ celuttuka. kaṭic ciṟuviral tavira ēṉi viralkaḷic cērttapaṭiyāy iraṇṭu kikaḷiyum nīṭṭi avaratu uccan talikkumēṟ kīḻnōkkip piṭikka. aṅṅaṉam piṭittiruk kiyil avviralkaḷiṉ nuṉivaḻiyē tamatu miṉṉāṟṟal avaratu uccan taliyiṉuḷ iṟaṅki avari niṉivaṟṟa āḻnta tuyiliṉkaṭ koṇṭu celvatākat tuyiṟṟuvōr naṉṟāy uṟuttu niṉittalvēṇṭum.
ippaṭic ciṟitu nēram niṉittapiṉ, paṭitta avviral kaḷāl avvuccan taliyilirunta avaratu muḻaṅkāl variyil taṭavip, piṟaku pakkac cāyvāy akkikaḷip puraṭṭi avartam vilāppakkamāka mēluyartti maṟupaṭiyum taṭavuka. iṅṅaṉam palakāṟ ceytapiṉ `ippōtu - nīr - āḻnta - tūkkattiṟ - cellukiṟīr. ummic cuṟṟilum naṭappatu - iṉṉateṉṟu - umakkut teriyātu. yāṉ - colvaṉavaṟṟit tavira - vēṟoṉṟum - umakkut - teriyātu. eṉ coṟkaḷit tavira - vēṟoṉṟum - umakkuk kēḷātu. nīr - āḻnta - tūkkattil - irukkumpōtu, yāṉ - collukiṟapaṭiyē - ceyvīr - ippōtu - nalla - āḻnta - tūkkattil - irukkiṉṟīr -tūkkam - ayarnta tūkkam - āḻnta tūkkam - āḻnta tūkkam, eṉṟu tiruppittiruppi āḻnta tīrmāṉamāṉa kuraliṟ palakāṟ colli, iṭiyiṭiyē mēṟcolliyapaṭi uccantali yiliruntu taṭavutaluñ ceyka. ivvāṟu kālmaṇi nēram variyiṟ, ceytavuṭaṉē avar āḻnta tuyiliṟ ceṉṟu tūṅkuvar. ittūkkattiṟku aṟikuṟiyāka orē aḷavāka neṭṭuyirp peṟivar; kātaṇṭi evvaḷavu periya ōciyi eḻuppiṉālum avar ataṉi uṇarār; tuyiṟṟuvōr kuraloliyit tavirap piṟitoṉṟum avarukkuk kēḷātu. ivvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, tuyiṟṟuvōr piṉvarumāṟu avarukkuc colluka: `ippōtu - nīr - nirampa - āḻnta tuyilil - iruppiṉum - yāṉ colvaṉavaṟṟi maṭṭum - eṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi - kēṭkiṉṟīr’ eṉṟapiṉ `eṉ coṟkaḷik kēṭkiṉṟīrā? eṉṟu viṉavuka. mutaliṟ ciṟitu cummā iruppar; piṉṉum piṉṉum viṭāmaṟ kēṭṭāl avar `ām, ippōtu kēṭkiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu tūkka mayakkattāl mella mellac colvar. piṟaku `umatu āḻnta tūkkattil iruntapaṭiyē kaṇkaḷit tiṟantupārum eṉṟuiraṇṭu mūṉṟumuṟi colluka. aṅṅaṉamē avar kaṇkaḷic ciṟitu uḻippōṭu arivāci tiṟantu pārppar. appōtu avartam kaṇkaḷ civantirukkum. itaṉāl, avar āḻnta tuyililētāṉ irukkiṉṟār eṉpataṉit tiṇṇamākat terintu koḷḷalām.
niṉivaṟṟa ittuyilil iruppōrkku aimpoṟi yuṇarvukaḷum tam iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭu naṭakkumāṟum ceyyalām. yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, oru tāmpuk kayiṟṟik kiyiṟkoṭuttu `itu melliya oru pūmāli, itaṉi nīr kaḻuttil aṇintu koḷḷalām eṉṟu coṉṉāl, avar ataṉit taṭavip pārttup puṉcirippōṭu ataṉik kaḻuttil aṇintukoṇṭu iruppar. piṟaku `nīr ippōtu kaḻuttil aṇintirukkum inta māliyil toṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷa mullimalarkaḷ evvaḷavu naṟumaṇam uḷḷaṉa vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa! nīr inta māliyi mukantu pārum ataṉ maṇam umakku mikunta makiḻcciyit tarum, eṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ, avar tamatu kaḻuttiṟ pūṇṭirukkum antat tāmpukkayiṟṟip piṭittut tūkki mukantu pārttup puṉcirippukkoṇṭu makiḻvar. `ataṉ maṇam eppaṭi yirukkiṟatu?’ eṉṟu viṉaviṉāl `mikavum naṉṟāyirukkiṟatu! eṉṟu collic colli ataṉi mukantu mukantu pārppar. ataṉpiṟ ciṟitu kaṟkaṇṭi avar kiyiṟ koṭuttu  `ivi kacappāṉa pākaṟkāyviti, ivaṟṟi nāvil iṭṭuc cuvittup pārum. eṉṟu coṉṉāl, avar avaṟṟi vāyiṉuṭpeytu cuvittu ivi kacakkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu mukañcuḷittuc colli avaṟṟik kīḻē umiḻvar. piṉpu, cila kompup pākaṟkāyi avariṭan tantu `ivikaḷ kuḷirnta nalla cuviyuḷḷa veḷḷarikkāy, ivikaḷit tiṉṟu pārum eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl, avar avaṟṟi vāyiliṭṭu meṉṟu naṉṟāyc cuvittu makiḻcciyōṭu uṇpar. `inta veḷḷarikkāykaḷ eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉa? eṉṟu kēṭṭāṟ, `kuḷircciyāy nalla cuviyuḷḷaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu makiḻntu viṭiyaḷippar.
iṉṉum, melliya oru ciṟu kōli avaratu kiyiṟkoṭuttu, `ippōtu nīr muḻaṅkālvariyiṟ cēṟāy uḷḷa iṭattil naṭantu cella vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu; intak kōli ūṉṟikkoṇṭu eṉpiṉṉē karuttāy naṭantuvārum eṉṟu colli, avari yirukkiyiṉiṉṟum eḻacceytu naṭakkaviṭṭāl, avar uḷḷapaṭiyē akkōli ūṉṟik koṇṭu, tām ippōtu naṭakkumiṭam karuṅ kaṟṟariyā yiruppiṉum, ataṉic cēṟṟu nilamākavē karutic, cēṟṟiṉiṉṟum varuttattōṭu kālip piṭuṅkip piṭuṅki vittu naṭappatu pōl naṭappar. ataṉpiṉ, avarukku anniṉivi māṟṟi vēṟoru niṉiviṉi eḻuppuka. `nīr ippōtu accēṟṟu nilatti viṭṭu nallatōr iṭattil vantu amitiyā yirukkiṉṟīr eṉṟu colli avaratu neṟṟiyit toṭṭut taṭaviṉavuṭaṉē avar aṅṅaṉañ coṉṉapaṭiyē amitiyāyirupparākalāṉ appōtu avari nōkki `itō umakketirē aḻakiyatōr ēri tōṉṟukiṉṟatu; atil teḷivāṉa kuḷirnta nīr nirampiyirukkiṉṟatu; atiliruntu vīcuṅ kāṟṟu intak kaṭuveyiṟ kālattil evvaḷavu kuḷircciyā yirukkiṉṟatu! nīr intak kaṭuṅ kōṭiyil nīṇṭavaḻi naṭantu kaḷittup pōyirukkiṉṟīr! inta ēriyil iṟaṅkik kuḷittut talimuḻuki, itaṉ pakkattil uḷḷa antap pūñcōliyiṟ ciṟitu nēram iḷippāṟi irum! eṉṟu tīrmāṉamāṉa kuralil teḷivāyc colluka. aṅṅaṉam colliya aḷavilē avar kuṟippiṭṭamukamāy makiḻcciyōṭu uṟṟunōkkuvar. appōtu piṉṉum `inta ēriyip pārkkiṉṟīrā? eṉṟu viṉaviṉāl `ām, pārkkiṉṟēṉ, eṉpar. `anta ērikkāṟṟu eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟatu? eṉak kēṭṭāl, `ā! aḵtu evvaḷavu kuḷircciyāyirukkiṉṟatu! eṉpar.
ataṉmēl, avari irukkiyiṉiṉṟum eḻacceytu `ippōtu itiṟceṉṟu nīrāṭum eṉṟuviṭa, avaruḷḷapaṭiyē nīril iṟaṅkit talimuḻukuvatupōṟ ceyvar. ataṉpiṟaku `ippōtu nīr īran tuvarttikkoṇṭu intap pūṅkāviliruntu iḷippāṟum eṉṟu kūṟa, uṭaṉē avar aṅṅaṉamē ceyvar. piṉṉar `intap pūṅkāvil umakketirē ōr iḷamāmaram ā! evvaḷavu aḻakāyc ceḻittirukkiṉṟatu! pārttīrā?’ eṉṟu kēṭpiṉ `ām, inta iḷamāmaram mikavuñ ceḻumiyākattāṉ irukkiṉṟatu! eṉṟu viṭi kūṟuvar. uṭaṉē `ippōtu am māmarattiliruntu oru kuyil evvaḷavu iṉitākak kūvukiṉṟatu! ataṉi uṟṟuk kēḷum! eṉṟu colluka. avar akkuyilōciyik kēṭpārpōl kātu koṭuttu uṟṟuk kēṭpar. appōtu `ik kuyilōci eppaṭi irukkiṉṟatu? eṉṟu viṉavuka. ataṟkavar `ō! intak kuyiliciyiṉ iṉimiyi eṉṉeṉṟu colluvēṉ! iḵtu eṉakku aḷaviṟanta makiḻcciyit tarukiṉṟatu! eṉṟu viṭiyaḷippar.
`appaṭiyāṉāl nīrum umakkut terinta ōr iṉiya pāṭṭip pāṭukiṟatutāṉē. avvāṟu pāṭiṉāl aḵtu umakku iṉṉum mikunta makiḻcciyit tarum. ētō ciṟitu pāṭum eṉṟu colli avarit toṭṭu acittu viṭṭāl, uṭaṉē avar tamakkut terinta toru pāṭṭip pāṭāniṟpar. ciṟitunēram pāṭiyapiṉ `pāṭṭu mika naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu. pōtum, eṉṟu colli niṟuttiviṭuka. iṉṉum ivi pōṉṟa ettaṉiyō vakiyāṉa uruveḷit tōṟṟaṅkaḷi yellām avarpāl viḷivikkalām. iṅṅaṉamellāñ ceytu pārttapiṉ, iraṇṭu uḷḷaṅkikaḷiyum mēṉmukamāka virittu nīṭṭi, avarit toṭṭō toṭāmalō muḻaṅkāliliruntu, talivariyil mēlnōkkit taṭavi `nīr ippōtu viḻittuk koḷḷukiṟīr; kaṇṇit tiṟantu viḻittuk koḷḷum eṉṟu collumpōtē kikaḷit taṭṭi avari eḻuppiviṭuka.
iṉi, oṉṟiviṭṭorunāḷō, kiḻamik koru kālō, aṉṟit tiṅkaḷukkorukālō aṭuttaṭuttu varum nāṭpaṭṭa nōy uṭiyārkku, niṉivaṟṟa ittuyili varuvittu, annōy varutaṟkuriya kālamuṟiyi māṟṟi viṭṭāl annōy nīṅkiviṭutal tiṇṇam. eṇṇattiyum narampiyum paṟṟivarum nōykaḷit tīrttaṟku ivvaṟituyil mikavum ēṟṟatoṉṟākum. attakiya nōy koṇṭāri ittuyilil neṭunēram irukkacceytal naṉṟu; orunāḷ muḻutum, allatu orukiḻami muḻutuṅ kūṭa avari ittūkkattil irukkac ceyyalām. narampiṉ taḷarcciyum valiviṉmiyum mikutiyāyirukkuṅkāl, muṭimutal aṭikāṟum iraṇṭu kikaḷālum kīḻnōkki muṉṉum piṉṉum taṭavuka; ataṉpiṉ nōyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilmaṭṭum taṭavuka. neñcap piyiṉ acivu valikuṟintu kāṇappaṭumāṉāl, allatu aḵtu oḻuṅkiṉṟi naṭipeṟumāṉāl avviṭattit taṭaviyapiṉ vāyiṉāl ūtuka. ataṉāl nōyāḷiyiṉ uṟuppukaḷukkuk katakatappum iṉiyatōr uṇarcciyun tōṉṟak kāṇalām. mikak koṭiya irippirumaliyum, tōṭpaṭṭikaṭku naṭuvē piṭariyiliruntu mutukuneṭukak kīḻnōkki mella orē oḻuṅkāy ūtutalāl taṇiyac ceyyalām. āṉāl oṉṟu; nōyāḷi tamatu uṭampiṉmēṟ paṭṭuttuṇi aṇintiruppārāṉāl ataṉmēl vāyiṉāl ūtiṟ payaṉ uṇṭākātu; āṉatupaṟṟi avar pañcunūṟṟuṇi aṇiyumāṟu ceyvittu immuṟiyic ceyka. allatu tuṇiyi vilakkic ceyyattakkavarāyiruntāl avvāṟu ceytalum ām. iṅṅaṉam vāyiṉāl ūtuṅkāl maṟṟoṉṟum uṉṉittal vēṇṭum. neñciṉuḷ mūccuppi naṉku niṟiyak kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttu, vāyi nōykoṇṭa iṭattiṉmēl neruṅka vittu, uramākavum orē oḻuṅkākavum ūtit, tirumpavum mūccu iḻukkum poruṭṭu, mukatti appuṟamākat tiruppukiyil, ūtiṉa iṭattiṉiṉṟum vetuvetuppu nīṅkāmip poruṭṭu valatu kiyi ataṉmēl vittu ataṉip pottik koḷka. mūcciḻukkuṅkāl ataṉip pottiyum, tirumpa ūtuṅkāṟ kiyi appāl eṭuttum iṅṅaṉamē palakāṟceyka.
iṉip, palvaliyāl varuntuvōri niṉivaṟṟa tuyiliṟ pōkacceytu, piṉvarumāṟu ceytāl anta nōyi uṭaṉē taṇiyac ceyyalām. palvali mukattil entap pakkattil irukkiṟatō anta iṭattiṉmēl valatu kiyic ciṟitunēram vittal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṟaku, valiyirukkum pakkamāyk kātiṉmēl melliyatoru tuṇṭuk kampaḷiyi maṭittup pōṭṭu, ataṉ mēl maṟupaṭiyuṅ kiyi vikka vēṇṭum; maṟṟakkiyit talimēl vittu akkampaḷiyiṉ mēṉ muṉiyip piṭittukkoṇṭu, ataṉ ūṭē kātukkuḷ valuvāy ūtuka. annōy uṭaṉē nīṅkavēṇṭumeṉṉum muṉippōṭu ivvāṟu mūṉṟu, nāṉku muṟi ceyka. vetuvetuppāṉa ōr uṇarcci valiyuḷḷa palliṉkaṇ uṇṭāka, uṭaṉē avvali taṇiyum. kaṭiciyāka ūtiyapiṉ, kātiṉmēliṭṭa kampaḷiyi akaṟṟiviṭuka. immuṟiyāl innōyi uṭaṉē pōkkalāmēṉum, itaṉi vērōṭu kaḷital iḵtoṉṟāl maṭṭum muṭiyātu. malakkuṭaril aḻukkuc cērāmal nāṭōṟuṅ kaḻuviviṭutalālum, kālimāli iraṇṭuvēḷi nīri naṉṟāyk kotikka vittup, palvaliyuḷḷa pakkattiṉ uḷḷum puṟampum av āviyiṟ paṭumpaṭi ceytu cevviyāy ataṉi viyarkka vittalālum annōyi aṭiyōṭu tolikkalām.
iṉik koṭumiyāṉa talivali koṇṭavarkaḷukkum ataṉi uṭaṉē nīṅkac ceyyalām. nōyāḷikkup piṉṉē niṉṟukoṇṭu, iraṇṭu kikaḷiyum nōyāḷiyiṉ neṟṟi mēṟ ciṟitunēram vittu, aṅkiruntu kaṉṉappoṭṭukaḷic ciṟitu aḻuttiyapaṭiyāy mellap piṉṉukkut taṭavip, piṟaku kikaḷi uccantalikkuk koṇṭupōy, aṅkiruntu avaṟṟi eṭuttu utaṟuka. tirumpavum iṅṅaṉamē ceyka. ippaṭiyāka aintumutaṟ patiṉintu nimiṭaṅkaḷ ceytāl, talivaliyiṉ koṭumi taṇiyum. piṉṉum atu ciṟitiruntāl, valinīkkun tilam iṭṭuc ciṟitu tēyttāl atu muṟṟum nīṅkum. maṟupaṭiyun talinōy varātapaṭi malakkuṭarit tuppuravu ceytu, tīṉippi cevviyākum poruṭṭuc cūṭāṉa nalla vennīr aruntuka.
iṉip poruttup piṭippu mutalāṉa nōyāl mika varuntuvōkkum immuṟiyil attuṉpam eḷitil nīṅkum. nōy koṇṭa poruttiṉmēṟ, kaṭivirali akaṟṟi maṟṟi viralkaḷic cērttu nērokkappiṭittu, aṅkiruntu kīḻnōkkit taṭavik kīḻē avvuṟuppiṉ muṉikku vantatum iraṇṭu kikaḷiyum utaṟiviṭṭuk kikaḷip pakkaccāyvāy mēluyartti, maṟupaṭiyum aṅkiruntu avvāṟē taṭavi utaṟuka. iṅṅaṉam palamuṟi ceytāl avvali irunta iṭatti viṭṭuk kīḻ iṟaṅkum; appuṟam ataṉi aṟavē nīkkutal eḷitu. immuṟiyōṭu, poruttukkaḷil uḷḷa piṭippi iḷakac ceytaṟkuriya tilaṅkaḷ iṭṭut tēyttalum, aṅṅaṉan tēyttaṟkumuṉ piṭippuḷḷa iṭatti nīrāviyiṟ kāṭṭutalum peritum naṉmi payakkum.
iṅṅaṉamē veppunōy, kātuvali, kaṇvali, nīrkkōvi, narampuvali mutalāṉavikaḷiyum niṉivaṟṟa tuyiliṉ utaviyāl mēṟkūṟiya muṟippaṭi ceytu eḷitiṟ pōkkalām. immuṟikaḷi oruvar evvaḷavukkuc ceytu ceytu paḻakukiṉṟārō, avvaḷavukku avar annōykaḷi yellām oru noṭippoḻutil nīkkavallunar āvar. immuṟikaḷi vaḻuvāmaṟ paḻakum paḻakkamē innōykaḷit tīrttaṟkuc ciṟanta maruntākum. immuṟikaḷi naṉku paḻakit tērcci peṟṟa oruvar (Dr. Herbert. A. Parkin, M.D. See New Thought Dec No. 1903.) maruntukaḷiṟ ṟīrāta cuvācakācam eṉṉum īḷi nōyāl varuntiṉa orumātarārkku ataṉit tīrtta uṇmi varalāṟṟiṉi iṅkē moḻipeyart teḻutukiṉṟām:-
orunāḷ mālik kālattil, muppattiraṇṭu āṇṭuḷḷa oru mātaric ceṉṟupārkkumpaṭi aḻikkappaṭṭēṉ. ammātar patiṉintu āṇṭukaḷāka nirampavuṅ koṭumiyāṉa cuvācakācattāl nirampat tuṉpuṟṟu vantaṉar. mutaṉmutal avvammiyārkku annōy vantapōtu ataṉi ārāyntu pārttavar aḵtu elumpurukki nōy eṉṟaṉar. piṉṉarp perukkak kaṇṇāṭi (Microscope) vaḻakkattukku vanta cila āṇṭukaḷil annōyi ārāyntu kāṇa atu cuvācakācam eṉṉum īḷi nōyāmeṉṟu uṟutippaṭuttiṉar. yāṉ anta ammiyip pārkkac ceṉṟapōtu, avar teṟkēyuḷḷa ōr ūriliruntu appōtutāṉ aṅkē vantucērntaṉar; avvūriṟ pallāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟu taṅkiyum avarkku ētum naṉmi viḷintilatu. paṭukkiyiṟ pala taliyaṇikaḷi aṭukki avaṟṟiṉmēṟ cāynta paṭiyāy iḷippiṉāl tuṉpuṟṟuk koṇṭu avar uṭkārntirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. avartam vāyitaḻkaḷ nīlaniṟamāyum kikālkaḷ cilleṉṟum iruntaṉa. palamaṇi nēraṅkaḷāy nōy nirampa mummuramāka iruntatu; aṉṟik kālimutal avvammi ētōr uṇavum eṭukkavilli. eṉṉiṭam avar kucukucuveṉṟu pēcutaṟkumēṟ pēca iyalavilli; appaṭip pēcuvatum mikka varuttamākavē iruntatu; ēṉeṉil avar muṟṟuṅ kaḷippaṭintiruntār.
`yāṉ appaṭukkiyiṉ pakkattē amarntu, avvammiyār kikaḷil oṉṟi eṉ kiyiṟ ṟūkki vittuk koṇṭu `ippōtu nīṅkaḷ paṭuntuṉpatti nīkkum poruṭṭu yāṉ iṅku varuvikkappaṭṭēṉ; ciṟitu nērattil nīṅkaḷ mūccut taṭiyiṉṟi viṭuvīrkaḷeṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ. ippōtu uṅkaḷiṭam yāṉ ētoṉṟuṅ kēḷēṉ; nāṉ uṅkaḷi nōkkic colvaṉavaṟṟil uṅkaḷ karuttic ciṟitu nēram patiyavikka vēṇṭuvatutāṉ eṉakku vēṇṭum. uṅkaḷ kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoḷḷuṅkaḷ, ciṟitu nērattil ellām ōyvāyirukkum ōr uṇarcci uṅkaḷ uṭampeṅkum varak kāṇpīrkaḷ; mūccu ōṭṭamum varavara nīṇṭu āḻntu cellum. uṅkaḷ mārpuk kūṭṭiṉ mēlellām yāṉ kiyāl tēykkap pōkiṉṟēṉ; appaṭic ceykiyil mūcciṉ ōṭṭam varavarat taṭiyiṉṟic cellum, eṉṟu colliyapaṭiyē mārpiyum nuriyīralukku nērēyuḷḷa mutukiyum valuvāyt tēykkat tuvaṅkiṉēṉ; atē nērattil, nuriyīral varavarat taṭi tīrkiṉṟateṉavum, mūccōṭṭam āḻamāy nīṇṭu celkiṉṟa teṉavum, narampu valiyum accamuṅ kavaliyum akalukiṉṟaṉa veṉavum, varavarat tūkka mayakkamum uṇṭākiṉṟateṉavum, avarukku vēṇṭuvaṉa nalla uṇavun tūkkamumē yeṉavum iṉṉuñ ciṟitu nērattil ciṟitu cāṟu parukat takkavaḷavu mūccōṭṭam taṭiyiṉṟi nikaḻum eṉavum, ataṉ piṟṟūkkam varumeṉavum kaṭṭuri kūṟikkoṇṭē vantēṉ.
kuṟippiṭṭa inta muṟiyākap pattu nimiṭaṅkaḷ variyil tēytta piṟakuṅ, kaṭṭuri kūṟiya piṟakum nōy koṇṭa avvammiyār mika eḷitāy mūccu viṭutaliyum iratta ōṭṭam cīrppaṭṭuc cellutaliyuṅ kaṇṭu uvappuṟṟēṉ. ataṉpiṉ, mūccōṭṭam eḷitāy naṭipeṟa naṭipeṟac cāyntu koḷḷa vittirukkun taliyaṇikaḷi oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy eṭuttukkoṇṭē vantu kaṭiciyāka avarip paṭukki maṭṭattiṟ kiṭattuvōm eṉṟuṅ kaṭṭurittēṉ. mutal taliyaṇiyi eṭuttuviṭṭavuṭaṉ avvammi paṭippaṭiyāy ataṟku aṭutta taliyaṇiyiṟ cāyalāṉār. annēramellām, `taṭiyillā mūccōṭṭat tip paṟṟiyum, `tūkkattip paṟṟiyum, `paciyip paṟṟiyuṅ kaṭṭurikaḷit toṭarpākac colli vantēṉ. avvammiyār iraṇṭān taliyaṇimēṟ cevviyāyc cāyntu koṇṭirukkiyil naṉṟāyt tāḷitta cūṭāṉa cāṟu ciṟitu avar aruntak koṭuttōm. piṉṉuñ ciṟutunērañ ceṉṟapiṉ, maṟṟat taliyaṇikaḷiyum eṭukkalāṉēṉ; iṅṅaṉamāka irupatu nimiṭaṅkaḷil ellāt taliyaṇikaḷiyum eṭuttuviṭṭu, ayarntu uṟaṅkum avvammiyip paṭukki maṭṭattil kiṭakkaviṭṭōm. avar iraṇṭumaṇinēran tūṅkiṉār. ataṉpiṉ iṉṉuñ ciṟitu uṇavu koṭukkap paṭṭatu. tirumpavum avar tūkkaṅkoṇṭu paṭuttār. aṭutta irupatumaṇinērattil iraṇṭumaṇi nērattiṟku oru muṟiyāka avarukku uṇavu tarappaṭṭatu.
malaccikkal, ceriyāmi, tūkkamiṉmi, paciyiṉmi mutalāṉavi nīṅkum poruṭṭuk kaṭṭuri moḻikaḷ kūṟiya paṭiyāy, ovvoru nāḷuṅ kāli māli iraṇṭu poḻutukaḷāka iraṇṭu kiḻamikaḷ variyil avvammi yārukku immuṟiyic ceytu vantēṉ. uṭaṉē avar nalampeṟalāṉār. mutaṟṟaram immuṟiyic ceyta vaḷavāṉē avvīḷi nōy tirumpa varum aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭavilli. avaruṭampiṉ ovvoru nikaḻcciyun tiruttappaṭṭatu. iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷil ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya ēḻuvīci eṭi mikutiyākap peṟṟār. avvīḷi nōy avari viṭṭakaṉṟu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. avartam vāḻnāḷellām varuntik koṇṭirunta pala vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa nōykaḷi akalac ceytamiyē avvammiyār annōy muṟṟuntīrap peṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu yāṉ karutukiṉṟēṉ.
mikak koṭiya kācanōyit tīrtta amerikka maruttuvariṉ ivvuṇmi varalāṟu koṇṭu, maruntukaḷiṉ utavi vēṇṭāmalē ettakiya nōyiyum ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟiyāl tīrttu viṭalāmeṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅku kiṉṟataṉṟō?
15. taṇṇīr mantirittuk koṭuttal
iṉi, uḷḷē parukutaṟkāvatu, veḷiyē uṭampiṟ ṟeḷittuk koḷḷutaṟkāvatu, allatu talimuḻukutaṟkāvatu taṇṇīr mantirittuk koṭukkum vaḻakkam nammaṉōruḷḷuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ayalnāṭukaḷiluḷḷa veṇmakkaḷ immuṟi nōy nīkkutaṟkup peritum utavutali naṉku ārāyntu pārttu itaṉiyuṅ kikkoṇṭu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. aṟituyil muṟikaḷiṟ paḻakit tērcci peṟṟa oruvarkku niṉivāṟṟal nirampa valivēṟi niṟkumātalāl, an niṉivāṟṟalāl untappaṭum avaratuṭampiluḷḷa miṉcattu avartaṅ kaṇkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavum coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavum viralnuṉikaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavum puṟattē ōṭip puṟattuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyum uyirkaḷiyum iyaṅkumāṟum iyaṅkāmal vaṟitē irukkumāṟum ceytu viṭukiṉṟatu. innāvalantīvilum niṉivāṟṟal mikuntuḷḷōr palaruḷar.
avar tammi vaḻipaṭṭāri vāḻavum, tammip puṟampaḻittāric cāvavuñ ceyta uṇmi varalāṟukaḷ āṅkāṅkuḷḷārkkut teriyāmal irā. toḻu nōy koṇṭum, īḷi mutaliya koṭunōykaḷāl varuntiyum vanta cilar attakiya periyāri etirppaṭṭu avar tan tirunōkkan tammēṟ paṭṭa aḷavāṉē annōy tīrappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. vēṟucilar avarip paḻittut tītu ceytamiyāṉē avarāl viyappaṭṭut tāmum taṅkuṭumpamum vērōṭu aḻintu pōyiṉar. nīrāvi vaṇṭiyiṟ ceṉṟa attakiya periyāri aṟiyāmal avarik kīḻiṟakki viṭṭamiyāṉē, avar avvaṇṭi ōṭāmiyil niṟkacceyta nikaḻccikaḷum piṉṉar avari vēṇṭi irantamiyāṉē avar iraṅki avaṟṟi ōṭacceyta nikaḻccikaḷum innāṭṭiṉ kaṇuḷḷār palar nērē kaṇṭumiruppar. veṟikoṇṭu evarkkum etaṟkum aṭaṅkāmal ōṭit tirintu uḻakkiya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉikaḷiyuṅ kutirikaḷ āṉēṟukaḷ naccuppāmpukaḷ varippulikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum tamatu kaṭpārviyālum oru collālum aṭakkiya periyōriyum palar pārttiruppar. amerikkāvil immuṟikaḷil naṉku tērcci peṟṟa oruvar oru maṇikku nūṟṟirupatu mil vaḻiyōṭum oru nīrāvi vaṇṭiyi oru noṭiyil ōṭāmal niṟutti viṭṭaṉareṉṟu aṅkiruntuvarum veḷiyīṭukaḷiṟ paṭittuṇarn tirukkiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉamellāṅ kaṇkūṭāka aṟiyappaṭum uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷiliruntu, niṉivāṟṟal mikap peṟṟōrāl untappaṭum miṉṉāṟṟal avartaṅ kaṇkaḷ coṟkaḷ viral nuṉikaḷ eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ vaḻicceṉṟu arumperum putumikaḷi viḷikka vallatātali iṉituṇarkiṉṟēm allamō?
ivvāṟu untappaṭum miṉ āṟṟali nōyāḷiyiṉ uṭampiṟ celuttiṉāl aḵtu ettuṇi naṉmiyi evvaḷavu viriviṟ ceyyumeṉpati nām eṭuttuc collutalum vēṇṭumō? nōy tīrkkum niṉiviṉ muṉippiṉāl untappaṭum miṉcattit taṉṉiṭattu vāṅki ataṉi nōyāḷiyiṉ uṭampiṟ celuttutaṟku iṭiniṉṟu utavum poruḷkaḷuḷ taṇṇīrē mikac ciṟantatāyirukkiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, namatuṭampil mukkāṟpaṅku nīrpporuḷāṉa irattattāl niṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; avvirattamē miṉcattit taṉkaṇ ēṟṟu uṭampiṉuḷ eṅkum ōṭi ataṉi vaḷarttu varukiṉṟatu; nuṇṇiya miṉ cattōṭu kalantu ulāvutaṟku atupōl ōṭavalla nekiḻccipporuḷ oṉṟē payaṉpaṭuvatallatu, ēṉik kaṭṭip poruḷkaḷ attuṇip payaṉpaṭā. ātalāl, tūyataṇṇīril miṉcatti ēṟṟi ataṉi nōyāḷi parukutaṟkuk koṭuttalē naṉṟu. tūya kuḷirnta ūṟṟut taṇṇīr kiṇaṟṟut taṇṇīr kiṭiyātapōtu, paṉivellatti mantirittuc ciṟu māttirikaḷākki oru ciṟu puṭṭilil aṭittu vittuk koṇṭu, avaṟṟiyum nōyāḷikkuk koṭuttunōy tīrkkalām. allatu, uṇṇum uṇaviyum, uṭukkum uṭiyiyuṅkūṭa mantirittuk koṭukkalām.
