iṉit, taṇṇīril nōy tīrkkum miṉcatti ēṟṟumāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum; nallatōr ūṟṟilēṉuṅ kiṇaṟṟilēṉum tūya maṭpāṇṭattil mukanteṭuttuvanta tūya kuḷirnta nīri oru maṭkuṭuviyiṟ peytu, ataṉi iṭatu kiyil vittukkoḷka. piṉpu, oḷivaṭivākiya iṟivaṉuruvi niṉintu uḷḷatti avvoḷivaṭiviṟ porutti, avvoḷi numatu uḷḷattiṟ pāyntu uṭampeṅkum niṟintu niṟpatāka naṉṟāy uṟutti niṉikka. aṅṅaṉañ ciṟitu nēram niṉintapiṉ, valatu kiyiṉ kaṭic ciṟuviral oḻiya ēṉiviralkaḷi oṉṟaṉmēloṉṟu paṭāmaṟ kuvittu, iṭatu kiyil uḷḷa kuṭuvit taṇṇīrkkumēṟ piṭikka; kaṇkaḷiyum imiyāmal niṟutti, avvāṟu kuvitta viral nuṉikaḷil avaṟṟiṉ pārviyiyum niṟuttuka; ataṉpiṉ,uṭampil niṟinta avvoḷi nummuṭiya kaṇkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāyuṅ kuvitta viral nuṉikaḷiṉ vaḻiyāyum ōṭi akkuṭuvit taṇṇīriṟ pāyvatāka uṟutti niṉikka; aṅṅaṉam niṉikkiyilēyē avviralkaḷi naṭuṅkuvatu pōl acikka; ataṉpiṉ `nōy nīkkum miṉcattāl niṟinta ittaṇṇīr innōyāḷiyiṉ innōyi uṭaṉē nīkkuka! eṉṟum aṭuttaṭuttu uḷḷē collik koḷka. ivvāṟu ceytāṉapiṉ, akkuṭuvit taṇṇīri orumaṇi nērattiṟku orukāl oru vāykoṇṭa vaḷavu parukak koṭuttu varuka.
akkuṭuvit taṇṇīri mēṉmūṭikoṇṭu iṟuka mūṭivikka vēṇṭum. uḷḷukkup parukak koṭuttalēyaṉṟi, orukuṭan taṇṇīri iṅṅaṉamē mantirittuk koṭuttu nōyāḷi ataṉit taliyil viṭṭu muḻukavuñ ceyyalām. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷuṅ kaṇṇāṭi ēṉaṅkaḷuṅ kaḷimpu turu mutaliya naccupporuḷ illātaṉa vākiyāl, ivikaḷē immuṟikkup payaṉpaṭuttat takkavi. celvamuḷḷavarkaḷ poṉ veḷḷikaḷāṟ ceyta ēṉaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttalām. ivvāṟu niṉivu muṉippāl miṉcattu ēṟṟappaṭṭa taṇṇīr potuvāṉa taṇṇīriṉum vēṟupāṭuṭiya teṉpataṟku, miṉcattu ēṟṟiya taṇṇīriyum aḵtēṟṟāta taṇṇīriyum vēṟṟumi teriviyāmalē nuṇṇuṇarvu mikka oru ciṟumi kiyiṟ koṭuttuc cuvittup pārkkumpaṭi ceytāl, avaḷ avviraṇṭaṟkumuḷḷa cuvi vēṟupāṭṭi naṉkaṟintu urippaḷ.
iṉṉum, iṭatu kiyāl miṉ cattu ēṟṟappeṟṟa taṇṇīrkkum, valatu kiyāl miṉcattu ēṟṟappeṟṟa taṇṇīrkkuṅkūṭa vēṟupāṭu peritu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. im muṟikaḷil naṉṟāyp paḻakit tērccipeṟṟa oruvar tam iṭatu kiyi oru kiṇṇan taṇṇīriṉmēṟ kaviḻttup piṭitta paṭiyāyt tamatu miṉṉāṟṟal atiṟ pāyvatāka ōr aintu nimiṭa nēram niṉittirukka; piṉṉar vēṟoru kiṇṇan taṇṇīriṉ mēl aṅṅaṉamē tamatu valatu kiyip piṭittapaṭiyāy avvaḷavu nēramē niṉittirukka. piṟaku, nuṇṇuṇarvu mikka oru ciṟumi kiyiṟ koṭuttu avviraṇṭiyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyc cuvittu avaṟṟiṟkuḷḷa cuvi vēṟupāṭṭit terivikkumpaṭi kaṟpittāl, iṭatu kivaḻiyē miṉcattēṟiya taṇṇīr vetuvetuppākavum veṟuppāṉa cuviyuṭiya tākavum irukkiṉṟateṉavum, valatuki vaḻiyē aḵtēṟiya taṇṇīr iṉitākavum paḷapaḷappākavum irukkiṉṟateṉavum avaḷ kūṟuvaḷ. iṉṉum pala kiṇṇan taṇṇīrkaḷukku iṭiyē iṅṅaṉam miṉcattēṟṟiya kiṇṇam oṉṟaṉiyuṅ kalantu vittu, avi tammi ovvoṉṟāyc cuvittup pārttu, avaṟṟiṉ cuvi vēṟupāṭu terivikkumpaṭi nōyāḷi oruvarkku moḻintāl, avar miṉcattēṟiya nīriṉcuvi maṟṟiyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiruttali naṉkuṇarntu ataṉit terintu cuṭṭuvar.
iṉi, nīril miṉcattu ēṟṟikkoṭuttu nōytīrkkum muṟiyip paḻakavēṇṭupavar nōyaṟṟa tūya vuṭampum ellārkkum naṉmi ceyya virumpun tūya vuḷḷamuṅ kaṭavuḷiṭattuk kaṉinta aṉpum uṭiyavarāy iruttal iṉṟiyamiyāta tākum.
attakiyōr iḷippuk kaḷippiṉṟik kiḷarcci yōṭu irukkuṅkālaṅ kālippoḻutē yākiyāl, annēramē im muṟiyāl nōy tīrttaṟku ēṟṟa kālamām. mikavum ayarcci yaṭiyattakka uḻippiṉi nāḷ muḻutuñ ceyyāmaliruntāl, ellām amitiyuṟṟirukkum mālik kālamum itaṉic ceytaṟku ēṟṟatēyākum. nōyāḷi amiti koṇṭirukkuṅ kālamum itaṟku icintateṉṟuṇartal vēṇṭum. atu niṟka.
16. tīyapaḻakkaṅkaḷi oḻikkummuṟi
iṉi, mēṟpiṟavikaḷil iṭiyaṟātu paḻakiya paḻakkattāṟ palar piṟakkumpōtē appaḻakkattiṉ payaṉākac cila tīya taṉmikaḷi yuṭiyarāyp piṟantu, vaḷarntu, nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟa, attaṉmikaḷukku otta tīya ceyalkaḷic ceytu, tammiyun tuṉpuṟuttip piṟariyun tuṉpuṟuttip piṟavippayaṉi vīṇākki māyntu pōkiṉṟaṉar. ivar piṟakkumpōtē ittakiya tīya iyaṟkikaḷi uṭaṉ koṇṭuvantu, piṉṉar avi vērūṉṟi vaḷartaṟkēṟṟa tīyōr cērkkiyāl uraṅkoṇṭu muṟṟa, ataṉpiṉ evarāṉum māṟṟutaṟku ēlāta koṭiyōrāy ulakiṟkup pala perun tīṅkukaḷi iḻittu māḷkiṉṟaṉar. ittaṉmiyōrit tiruttutaṟku aracaṉum avaṉāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa ciṟikkaḷam mutalāṉa karuvikaḷum paṇṭu toṭṭu ulakiṉkaṇ mikuntiruntum, ivarit tirutti nalvaḻippaṭuttuvatu muṭintapāṭāyilli. caṭṭattukku māṟākavē tuṇintu naṭappōr palarākac, caṭṭattiṉ piṭikku akappaṭāta tīneṟikaḷil oḷintum oḷiyāmalum naṭappōr palappalar. uḷḷaṭaṅki eriyun tīyi oru pakkattil mūṭiṉāl, atu maṟṟoru pakkattil erintu veḷippaṭutal pōlavum, nilattiṉuḷḷē vēriṉ uram nirampa uḷḷa oru muṇmaratti mēlē evvaḷavutāṉ veṭṭiṉālum atu mēlum mēlum taḷirttal pōlavum, makkaḷ uḷḷattē paṭintu mēṉmēl valuvāy vaḷarum pollāṅkic cuvaṭaṟat tuṭittaṟku icinta vaḻit tuṟikaḷit tēṭāmaṟ, puṟattē evvaḷavu koṭumiyāṉa caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷic ceytu vittālum, avaṟṟāl, tīya iyalpuḷḷōr tiruntuvar eṉṟu kaṉavilum niṉittal vēṇṭām. akattē ūṟiya nañci, akattē ceṉṟu uriñcum maruntē mikkavallatākum. atupōṟ, puṟattuc cellum uṇarvukaḷi maṭakki, akattucceṉṟu putiyum `aṟituyiṟ kaṭṭurikaḷ maṭṭumē, oruvar piṟaviyilēyē tammuṭaṉ koṇṭuvanta tīya iyaṟkikaḷi māṟṟa vallatākumallāmal, vēṟupuṟattu muṟikaḷ eviyum avaṟṟi māṟṟa māṭṭāveṉṟu tiṇṇamāy uṇarntukoḷka. ciṟupiḷḷikaḷā yirukkiyilēyē ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟikaḷāl makkaḷic cīrtiruttutal eḷitu. avarkaḷ vaḷarntu periyarāṉapiṉ avarkaḷit tiruttutal aritu. ēṉeṉṟāl, avarkaḷ tammi aṟituyiliṟ celuttuvōrpāl aṇukavum māṭṭār, avar coṟkaḷukku iṇaṅkavum māṭṭār, āṉatupaṟṟip, periyōrkku aṭaṅki naṭattaṟkuriya piḷḷimip paruvattilēyē im muṟikaḷiṉutaviyāl avarkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttutaṟku. peṟṟōrkaḷ aṉivarum muyalvārkaḷāyiṉ ulakattil tīmikaḷ aṟṟuppōm. ellārum nallavarkaḷāyt tattam āṟṟalukkēṟṟa pukaḻ puṇṇiyaṅkaḷic ceytu nīṇṭa kālam immiyilum iṉpavāḻkkiyil vāḻntu, maṟumiyilum aḻiyā vīṭṭiṉpattip peṟuvar.
tāy tantiyarkkum uvāttiyāyarkkuṅ kīḻppaṭiyāmal naṭantu kalvi payilāmaṟ kuṟumpaṉāyt tiriyum oru piyaṉit tiruttuvatu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum: mēlē kāṭṭiya `aṟituyiliṟ celuttum muṟikaḷil oṉṟāl avaṉit tūṅkac ceytu, avaṉi nōkkip piṉvarumāṟu colluka: ippōtu yāṉ collukīṟapaṭi yellām nī kīḻppaṭintu naṭakkakkaṭavāy. uṉṉi nāṉ ittūkkattiṉiṉṟum eḻuppiya piṟaku nī uṉ peṟṟōrkkum uvāttiyāyarkkuṅ kīḻppaṭintu naṭakkakkaṭavāy. uṉakkuk koṭukkappaṭum pāṭaṅkaḷi nī karuttāyp paṭittu avaṟṟil tērcciyaṭiyak kaṭavāy. cuṟucuṟuppāy viḷiyāṭa vēṇṭum nērattil viḷiyāṭik karuttāyp paṭikka vēṇṭum nērattiṟ paṭittu oḻuṅkāka naṭakkak kaṭavāy. nī kaṟpavikaḷi yellāñ cevviyāka niṉivil vikkakkaṭavāy. ellārkkum iṉiya nalla piyaṉāynaṭantu mēṉmēṟ ceḻippuṭaṉ vaḷarak kaṭavāy. keṭṭa piḷḷikaḷōṭuṅ keṭṭa peṇkaḷōṭum nī kalantu paḻakalākātu. yāṉ uṉṉi eḻuppiyapiṉ, yāṉ kūṟiya ivikaḷi yellām niṉivukūra māṭṭāy; āyiṉum; ippōtu yāṉ colliyapaṭiyē nī eppōtuma naṭakkak kaṭavāy. eṉṟu ikkaṭṭurikaḷit tiruppit tiruppi mella aḻuttamāyt tīrmāṉamāṉa kuraliṟ palakāṟ colluka; orukāṟ colliyapiṉ ciṟitu nēram cummā iruntu piṟaku maṟṟorukāṟ colluka. ēṉeṉṟāl, aṟituyilil nuṇṇiya vuṭampiṉkaṇ niṟkum appiyaṉuṭiya aṟivil aṅṅaṉañ colliyavi naṉṟāyc ceṉṟu patitaṟkuc ciṟitu nēram vēṇṭum. immuṟi vaḻuvāmaṟ kāli māli iraṇṭu vēḷiyum oru kiḻami  variyil avaṉi aṟituyiliṟ celuttik kaṭṭuri kūṟi eḻuppi vantāl, avaṉ tīya taṉmikaḷellām māṟi muṟṟum nalla piyaṉāyk kalviyilum naṭakkiyilum maṟṟa ellā nalvakikaḷilum mēmpaṭuvāṉ.
iṅṅaṉamē, pukiyilic curuṭṭup piṭippōṉiyum, kaḷ, cārāyaṅ kuṭippōṉiyum, mūkkuppoṭi yiṭuvōṉiyum, iṉṉum poy cūtu kaḷavu uyirkkoli mutalāka ākātaṉa pala ceyvōṉiyum mēṟcolliya paṭiyākavē aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu, at tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷi viṭum paṭiyākak kaṭṭuri kūṟit, tirutti, nalvaḻippaṭuttalām.
iṉi, nōykaḷiṟ cērāta oru kuṟipāṭu cilariṭaṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu `teṟṟuvāy eṉpatēyām. oru collic coṉṉapiṉ ataṟkaṭutta collit toṭarntu collutaṟku iṭaṅkoṭāmal vāy mikat teṟṟutaliṉ, akkuṟipāṭuṭiyōr ataṉāl mikat tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. ākiyāl, ataṉi nīkkutaṟkuriya oru muṟiyi maṭṭum iṅkē viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟōm.
teṟṟit teṟṟip pēcun tikkuvāy uṭiya oruvari varuvittu, avari āḻnta tūkkattiṟ pōkacceyka. ataṉpiṉ avarinōkki `nīr ellārum pēcuvatip pōṟ cevviyāyp pēcuvīr; umakku atil ētōr iṭiñcalum irātu; taṅkutaṭi illāmal umakkup pēcavarum; eppōtum naṉṟāyp pēcuvīr, naṉṟāyp paṭippīr; coṟkaḷit toṭarpāka eḷitākac colluvīr, paṭikkum pōtum ovvoṉṟiyun toṭarpāka naṉṟāyp paṭippīr eṉṟu palamuṟi tiruppit tiruppi āḻnta aḻuttamāṉa kuralil viriyāmal metuvāyc colluka. iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭṭuri kūṟiyapiṉ, avari avvaṟituyilil vittukkoṇṭē, avartaṅ kaṇkaḷimaṭṭun tiṟakkum paṭi kaṟpikka; uṭaṉē avartaṅ kaṇkaḷit tiṟappar; avar paṭikkattakka oru puttakatti avaratu kiyiṟ koṭuttu atil oru pakutiyip paṭikkumpaṭi kaṟpikka; aṟituyilil iruntapaṭiyākavē avar ataṉip paṭikkiyil vāytikkāmaṟ paṭippar. evvaḷavu tikkip pēcukiṟavarum aṟituyilil irukkiyil teṟṟup paṭāmaṟ paṭippati appōtu kāṇalām. piṟaku avar tam kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoḷḷumpaṭi ceytu, iṅṅaṉamē avar eppōtun tikkāmaṟ cevviyāyp paṭippareṉa nālintu muṟi tiruppit tiruppic collip, piṉṉum ōr aintu allatu pattu nimiṭam variyil avari amitiyāyt tūṅka viṭṭu, appuṟam avari eḻuppi viṭuka.
iṉic, ciṟuvarkaḷiyē yaṉṟi āṇṭil mutirnta āṭavariyum makaḷiriyuṅkūṭa, mēṟkāṭṭiyavāṟē aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu nām vēṇṭiyapaṭiyellām naṭakkumpaṭi kaṟpikkalām. ivvāṟellāñ ceytu payaṉpeṟutaṟku niṉivōṭu kūṭiya aṟituyil ākātu; niṉivu aṟṟa āḻnta aṟituyilē ivikaḷic ceytaṟku ēṟṟatoṉṟātalāl, ataṉi varuvitta piṟakē ikkaṭṭurikaḷic collutaṟku muyalal vēṇṭum. it tuyiliṉiṉṟu eḻuppappaṭṭapiṉ, tuyiṟṟuvōr colliyavikaḷi viḻippu niliyil teriyāmalirukkumpaṭikkum, āṉālum avar appōtu colliyapaṭiyē eppōtum naṭakkumpaṭikkum vaṟpuṟuttum urittal iṉṟiyami yātatākum.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṟituyilukkuc cellamāṭṭāta allatu aṟituyiliṟcella viruppamillāta nōyāḷikaḷi allatu maṟṟavarkaḷi ataṉkaṭ celuttumāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ; oruvarkku nālintu muṟi muyaṉṟum aṟituyili varuvittal kūṭātāyiṉ, orukiḻami aṉṟi iraṇṭukiḻami variyil viṭāmal nāṭōṟum muyaṉṟuvariṉ avarkku ataṉi varuvittu viṭalām. aṟituyiliṟ cella māṭṭātavar oruvarum illiyākiyāl, ellārkkum ataṉi varuvikkalām eṉpatu tiṇṇam. āṉāl, oru cilarkku uṭaṉēyum, vēṟu cilarkku iraṇṭu nāḷiṉum, maṟṟuñ cilarkku oru kiḻamiyilum piṉṉuñ cilarkku iraṇṭu kiḻamiyilum ataṉit toṭarpāka muyaṉṟu varuvikkalām mēlum, tuyiṟṟuvōr aṟituyil varuvikkum muṟikaḷil naṉku paḻakip paḻakit tērntiruppārāyiṉ, avarkku appaḻakkattāl avvāṟṟal mika muṟuki niṟkum. attakiyavar oruvariyō aṉṟi ōr āyiravariyō ōriṭattil oru noṭippoḻutil aṟituyiliṟ celuttamāṭṭuvar.
aṅṅaṉam atu kūṭumāyiṉum, aṟituyiliṟ cellac ciṟitum viruppamillātavarkku ataṉi varuvittu avarit tamvaḻippaṭuttumāṟu eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ; ovvoruvarkkum nāṭōṟum iyaṟkiyāṉa tūkkam varuvatu uṇṭākiyāl, avar aṟiyāmalēyē avariṭañ ceṉṟu, atiliruntu avari aṟituyilukkup pōkac ceyyalām. ataṟkuriya muṟi yāteṉiṉ; ataṉiyuṅ kāṭṭuvām; aṟituyiliṟ cella viruppamillāta oruvar iyaṟkiyāṉa tūkkattil naṉṟāy ayarntuṟaṅkumpōtu, tuyiṟṟuvōr ētōr aravamuñ ceyyāmal avar pakkattil amitiyāyc cellal vēṇṭum. ceṉṟu mikavun tāḻnta kuralilē avari nōkkit tūṅkum, naṉṟāy ayarntu tūṅkum, añcavēṇṭām, nīr viḻikkakkūṭātu, āḻnta ayarnta tūkkattiṟ cellum, tūkkam, tūkkam, ayarnta tūkkam, āḻnta tūkkam eṉat tiruppit tiruppip palakāl uṟutti aḻuttamāy metuvākac colluka. iṅṅaṉañ collukiyil, avar viḻitteḻum aṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷ kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ, appōtu ataṉi niṟuttiviṭuka. appaṭi yallāviṭṭāl, umatu valatu kiyāl avaratu taliyit toṭṭu `eṉatu kural oliyit tavira vēṟu enta oliyiyum nīr kēṭkamāṭṭīr; nīr mikavum ayarntu āḻntu uṟaṅkukiṉṟīr eṉṟu colluka. ataṉ piṟaku avari nōkki ētēṉuṅ kēḷvi kēṭkalām. `ï¥nghJ kÂ v‹d? eṉṟāyiṉum, `ippōtu nīr oru mullippūvi mukarukiṉṟīr, ataṉ maṇam eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟatu? eṉṟāyiṉum, viṉavuka. avaṟṟiṟku avar viṭi collāma lirupparāyiṉ, viṭi kūṟumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttu urikka. piṉṉar viṭikūṟuvar. maṇi iṉṉateṉṟāvatu, mullippū naṉṟāy maṇakkiṉṟa teṉṟāvatu avar moḻikuvarāyiṉ, avar viḻittuk koḷḷāmal niṉivaṟṟa aṟituyililētāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu uṟutippaṭuttikkoḷka. ataṉ piṉṉum āḻnta tūkkattiṟkuc cellumpaṭiyākac ciṟitu nēraṅkaṭṭuri kūṟi, appuṟam avari numvaḻippaṭuttik koḷvataṟku evvevaṟṟic collal vēṇṭumō, avikaḷi ellām avari nōkkik kaṭṭurittuk kūṟuka. ataṉ piṟaku, avari eḻupputaṟkumuṉ `nīr viḻittuk koṇṭapiṉ yāṉ ippōtu colliyavikaḷi aṟiyamāṭṭīr; āyiṉum, ippōtu yāṉ colliyapaṭiyē nīr eppōtum naṭappīr eṉṟu uṟuttic colliviṭṭu, iṉi, nīr iyaṟkiyāṉa tūkkattil tūṅki viḻikka vēṇṭum nērattil viḻittuk koḷvīr eṉṟu muṭittuk kūṟi appuṟam akalka.
ivvāṟaṉṟi maṟṟoru muṟiyālum, iyaṟkit tūkkattiliruppōri, aṟituyiliṟ celuttalām. aḵtāvatu: mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭi avar arukiṟ ceṉṟavuṭaṉ vāyāṟ kaṭṭuri kūṟuvati niṟuttik kikaḷāl avarit toṭāmalē mukkāl viraṟkaṭi akalaniṟuttit taṭavutalālēyām. iru kikaḷiṉ kaṭic ciṟuviralkaḷiyum akala niṟutti, maṟṟa viralkaḷic ciṟitu neruṅka vaḷittuk kīḻnōkkip piṭittu, uccantaliyi liruntu mukattiṉ mēlākak kīḻiṟaṅki neñcuvariyiṟ kīḻnōkkit taṭavip, piṉṉarun taṭavutaṟkuk kikaḷi uyarttuṅkāl appaṭiyē uyarttāmaṟ pakkac cāyvāyt tiruppi uyartti, maṟupaṭiyum uccan taliyiliruntu neñcu variyil toṭāmal mukkāl viraṟkaṭi kikaḷi eṭṭa vittut taṭavuka; iṅṅaṉam pattu allatu patiṉintu nimiṭaṅkaḷ variyil taṭaviyapiṉ, mika melliya kuralil `tūkkam, tūkkam, āḻnta tūkkam, ayarnta tūkkam, nīr mikavum ayarntu tūṅkukiṉṟīr eṉṟu kaṭṭurikaḷip palakāl metuvākac colluka. itu muṭintapiṉ, metuvāka avaratu neṟṟiyit toṭṭu avari numvaḻippaṭuttutaṟkuc colla vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭiyē ceytu appāṟcelka. ivvāṟu ceytalāl, tammōṭu iṇaṅkāmal tamakku evvaḷavu māṟupaṭṭu niṟpōriyun, tam viruppappaṭi naṭakkac ceyyalām.
immuṟikaḷāl, nāṭpaṭṭa koṭiya nōykaḷiyun tīrkkalām; piḷavik kaṭṭikaḷālum āṟāta puṇkaḷālum allum pakalun tuṉpuṟuvōrkku, attuṉpañ ciṟitun teriyātapaṭi avikaḷi aṟuttu āṟṟiyum viṭalām. ivaṟṟiṟku uṇmiyāka nikaḻnta cila nikaḻccikaḷi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām. aṟituyili varuvittu, ataṉāl oruvarit tam viruppappaṭi naṭakkac ceyyalām eṉpati, im muṟikaḷiṟ peritun tērccipeṟṟu, ivaṟṟik kāḷikaṭṭattil (Calcutta) uḷḷa āṅkila maruttuvak kaḻakattil nōyāḷikaḷpāṟ ceytu pārttu, ulakiṟ paravacceyta āṅkila maruttuvar oruvar (Dr. James Esdile, M.D.) tām nērē kaṇṭu eḻutiya oru nikaḻcciyiliruntu naṉku aṟiyalāmātalāl, avareḻutiya appakutiyi iṅkē moḻi peyarttu varikiṉṟām:-
iraṇṭu kiḻamikaḷukkumuṉ ūkuḷik kaṭit teruviṉūṭē (Hooghly bazaar) vaṇṭiyiṟ celkiyil, aṅkuḷḷa ūr kāvaṟ cāvaṭikku (Police Office) etirē oru tiraṇṭa kūṭṭamāṉa āṭkaḷ niṟkak kaṇṭēṉ. aṅkē naṭappatu eṉṉaveṉṟu yāṉ kēṭka, oru periya āḷ oru ciṟuvaṉit tiruṭikkoṇṭu pōkiyiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭāṉeṉṟum, kāvaṟkūṭattiṉuḷḷē avviruvarum irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum cilar coṉṉārkaḷ. itik kēṭṭatum, yāṉ akkūṭattiṉuṭ ceṉṟu, pattu allatu paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭuḷḷa oru ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉi mīṭṭavaṉākac collap paṭṭa ōr āṇmakaṉ maṭimītu amarntirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. antap piyaṉ arivāci mayakka muṭiyavaṉāyum, avaṉ kaṇkaḷil oṉṟu vīkkamuṭiyatāyum iruntamiyāl avaṉi maruttuvak kaḻakattiṟku eṭuttuc cellumpaṭi ēviṉēṉ. ataṉ piṟaku avaṉit tiruṭicceṉṟa kaḷvaṉi eṉṉiṭaṅ kāṭṭiṉar.
avaṉ tāṉ ōr ampaṭṭaṉ eṉṟāṉ; avaṉ toḻilukkuriya karuvikaḷ aṭaṅkiya aṭippum oṉṟu eṉ etirē koṇarntu vikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉi nāṉ karuttāy ārāyntu pārttēṉ; āṉāl, avi ampaṭṭarkkuriya vaḻakkamāṉa karuvikaḷākavē iruntaṉa. antap piyaṉō virivil taṉ uṇarvu taṉakku varappeṟṟavaṉākip piṉvaruṅ katiyi eṉṉiṭañ coṉṉatum allāmal, tirumpa naṭunili maṉṟattilum naṭuvarkku etirilum aṅṅaṉamē ciṟitum picakāmaṟ coṉṉāṉ. acciṟuvaṉ, tāṉ aṉṟu viṭiyaṟkāliyil oru vīṭṭiṟku arukiluḷḷa vayalaṇṭi ceṉṟa ciṟitu nērattiṟkup piṟaku, pāṭṭiyiṟ ceṉṟa oru putumiyāṉa āṇmakaṉ atiliruntu taṉkiṭṭa vantaṉareṉṟum, kiṭṭavantatum cilar mantira moḻikaḷic collat toṭaṅkiṉa ṉeṉṟum, ataṉpiṉ avaṉ taṉ kikaḷip piṭittukkoṇṭu taṉ kaṇkaḷiṉ mēṟ kuṟukkākak kiyit taṭavat tāṉ uṇarvu mayaṅki viṭa avaṉ taṉṉi vaḻinaṭattic ceṉṟatumaṭṭum niṉivilirukkiṉṟa teṉṟum, avaṉatu valukkaṭṭāyam iṉṟiyētāṉ avaṉip piṉpaṟṟic cellavēṇṭiyatāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum pukaṉṟāṉ. maṟupaṭiyum taṉ uṇarvu taṉakku varappeṟṟatu, tāṉ avvāṇmakaṉi etirppaṭṭa iṭattiṟku iraṇṭu mil toliviluḷḷa cantiranākūrk kōṭṭi vāyilaṇṭiyēyām eṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ.
ivvaḷavutāṉ avaṉ colliyatu. anta āḷōṭukūṭa varukiyil acciṟuvaṉ ētoṉṟum uṇṇavumilli, parukavumilli; antap piyaṉukku uṭiyavaṉum nēcarkaḷum acciṟuvaṉ oḻuṅkāṉa naṭakkiyun tiṟamiyum uḷḷavaṉeṉavum, icippāvatu tūkkattil naṭakkum vaḻakkamāvatu avaṉukku orukālum illiyeṉavum moḻintārkaḷ. antap piyaṉi mīṭṭavaṉeṉṟu collappaṭṭa maṟṟōr āṭavaṉi ārāyntēṉ; avaṉ acciṟuvaṉi aṉṟu kāliyil tāṉ pārkkiyil avaṉ oru putumiyāṉa āḷip piṉ ṟoṭarntu cella, ataṟku muṉṉamē tāṉ ap piyaṉi naṉṟāyt terintavaṉātalāl, avaṉi vaḻiyil niṟutti `nī eṉṉa ceykiṟāy? eṉṟu kēṭṭum acciṟuvaṉ ētoru viṭiyum pakarāmal mayaṅkiyirukkak kaṇṭu, tāṉ veruṇṭu, taṇṇīrik koṇṭuvantu avaṉatu mukattiṉ mēṟ ṟeḷittum, vēṟucila māṟṟukaḷic ceytum avaṉukku ammayakkan teḷiya vittatākavum, maṟupaṭiyun tāṉ appoṭiyaṉi viṉāva, avaṉ tāṉ aṅkuvantatu ēṉ eṉṟu taṉakkut teriyavilli. tāṉ muṉṉaṟiyāta apputiyavaṉi eppaṭiyō piṉṟoṭarntu cellalāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟiyavuṭaṉ nilattiṉmēl viḻuntu kaṇṇik kāyappaṭuttik koṇṭa tākavum eṭutturittāṉ. itaṟkiṭiyil, apputiyaṉ avviṭattiviṭṭu ōṭukiyiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu ūkuḷikkuk koṇarappaṭṭāṉ.
ataṉ piṟaku avvampaṭṭaṉi uḷḷē kūppiṭṭēṉ; avaṉ coṉṉatāvatu; antap piyaṉ aḻutukoṇṭu mayakkattō ṭirukkiyil avaṉit tāṉ pāṭṭiyil etirppaṭṭatākavum, avaṉukku yātu tuṉpam nērntateṉṟu tāṉ viṉava avaṉtāṉ vaḻitappi vantu viṭṭatic coṉṉatākavum, atu kēṭṭu ūrkkāvaṟ cāvaṭikkut taṉṉōṭu kūṭavantāl aṅkiruntu oru kāvaṟkāraṉ avaṉi vīṭṭiṟ koṇṭupōyc cērppaṉeṉa moḻintatākavum pukaṉṟāṉ.
entap pakkam uṇmiyō aḵtirukkaṭṭum; viyappāṉa icceyti eṉkaruttik kavarntatu; av āḷiṉatu toḻilum eṉakku aiyatti eḻuppi viṭṭatu. inta nāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa ampaṭṭarkaḷ tamatu toḻili metuvākac ceykiyil āṭkaḷit tūṅkacceyva tuṇṭeṉṟu yāṉ kēḷvip paṭṭiruntēṉ.
tammi mayakkiviṭṭa āṇpālāriṉ piṉṉē mayakkuṇṭa makkaḷ toṭarntuceṉṟa ceytikaḷ innāṭeṅkum paraviyirukkiṉṟaṉa; aṅṅaṉam mayaṅkic ceṉṟavarkaḷ perumpālum peṇmakkaḷēyām. ulaka meṅkumuḷḷa ampaṭṭarkaḷ kūrtta aṟivum etaṉiyum uṟṟunōkkum iyalpum uḷḷavarkaḷ. avarkaḷatu toḻilāṉatu aṟituyil varuvittaṟku ēṟṟa uṇarcciyuṭiya uṭampiṉ mēṟpuṟatti avarkaḷ toṭutaṟku iṭañ ceykiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ mikap paḻiya kālattilēyē avarkaḷ aṟituyilākiya maṟi poruḷit terintiruttal vēṇṭum; avarkaḷiṉ kittoḻilukkuriya putiporuḷāy aḵtu avarkaṭku vantirukkavēṇṭum. ippōtu nērnta im muṭṭup pāṭṭiṉiṉṟu veḷippaṭutaṟku iraṇṭu vaḻikaḷtām eṉakkut teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa; appiyaṉiṭattuk kāṇappaṭṭa mayakkam avaṉukku iyaṟkiyāka varum naṭittuyi (Somnambulism) lākavātal, aṉṟic ceyaṟkiyāka varuvikkappaṭṭa naṭit tuyilākavātal iruttal vēṇṭum.
atu, piṟkūṟiyatāka iruntāl aṟituyiliṉ muṟikaḷāl aṉṟi vēṟeṅṅaṉam atu varuvikkappaṭum?  taṟceyalāka yāṉ innikaḻcciyik kāṇa nērntamiyāl, iṅṅaṉam ōr āḷik kavarntu koṇṭu cellum muṟi irukkakkūṭumeṉpatip paṟṟic cāṉṟupakara yāṉ aḻikkappaṭuvēṉeṉa muṉṉatākavē aṟintukoṇṭēṉ; naṭittuyilip paṟṟiyatoṉṟum yāṉ aṟintatillāmiyāl, mutalil eṉakkuḷḷa kuṟiyit tīrttukkoḷḷumporuṭṭu yāṉ ataṉi ārāya vēṇṭiṉēṉ. iḵtu aṟituyiṉ muṟikaḷāl varuvikkak kūṭiyatāyiṟ periyatōr āṟṟal ciṟiyataṉit taṉṉuḷ aṭakki niṟṟalpaṟṟi, ataṉip pōṟ ceyaṟkiyāṉa naṭittuyili yāṉ varuvikka vallaṉāvēṉ eṉa eṇṇiṉēṉ. immuṟiyāl aṭiyakkūṭumāyiṉ, iyaṟki naṭittuyilippōṟ ceyaṟkiyāṉa toṉṟi varuvittaṟporuṭṭu, uṇarvaṟṟa niliyil oruvarik koṇṭu cellutaṟku iṭiyē yāṉ niṉṟuviṭal vēṇṭum.
ākavē, yāṉ ciṟikkōṭṭattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa maruttuvak kaḻakattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa oruvaṉi aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytēṉ. ivaṉukkuk kaṇṇerivu tīrttal vēṇṭi ivaṉi itaṟkumuṟ palakāl niṉivaṟṟa aṟituyiliṟ celuttiyirukkiṉṟēṉ; akkālaṅkaḷiṉellām uṇarvaṟṟa niliyiṉaḷavukku avaṉi aṟituyiliṟ koṇṭu ceṉṟu, mikak kuṟivāyk kātukēṭkavum uṭampaciyavum maṭṭum avaṉukku vallami ciṟitu viṭṭuk koṭuttēṉ. ippōtu avaṉukku niṉivaṟṟa aṟituyili varuvikkiyiṟ, ciṟituṇarvukku iṭam viṭṭa inniliyil vantu avaṉi viṭalāyiṉēṉ. avaṉum annaṭittuyilil naṭakkat tuvaṅki,akkaḻakattiṉ maṟṟoru muṉivariyiṟ ceṉṟu cuvaraṇṭipōy niṉṟāṉ; piṟaku aṅkiruntu tirumpi orē nērākac ceṉṟu maṟupaṭiyum maṟṟoru taṭiyaṇṭi vantu muṭṭuppaṭṭu niṉṟāṉ. ciṟitu nēram yāṉ avaṉi aciviṉṟi niṟkac ceyyavē, avaṉukku attuyil iṉṉum mikutiyāyiṟṟu; avaṉ ōcikaḷi uṇaramāṭṭāvaṉāyiṉāṉ. avaṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷiṉ mēl ūtiṉamiyiṉālum, neṭuka avaṉuṭaṉ pēciṉamiyiṉālum avaṉ attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuntu āṅkilam intuttāṉam eṉṉum irumoḻikaḷiyum yāṉ kūṟiyavāṟē tiruttamākat tiruppic coṉṉāṉ. avaṉi nāṉ eḻuppiyapiṟaku, tūkkattiltāṉ ceytavikaḷic ciṟitum aṟiyavilli yeṉṟum, avviṭattiviṭṭut tāṉ appuṟam peyaravilli yeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ; āṉāl, akkaḻakattil yāṉ avaṉi aṟituyiliṟ celuttat toṭaṅkiya iṭattiṟku nēretirē ippōtiruntāṉ. piṟaku, nāṉ naṭunili maṉṟattiṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarumāṟu aḻikkappaṭṭēṉ.
“aṅkē ceṉṟatum, naṭuvar eṉṉi nōkkic `cāṉṟu kūṟiṉōr pakarntapaṭi oruvarik kavarntu koṇṭu pōtal naṭakkakkūṭiyatutāṉ eṉṟu tāṅkaḷ eṇṇukiṉṟīrkaḷā? eṉṟu kēṭṭār.`atu naṭakkakkūṭiyatutāṉ eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ; ēṉeṉṟāl, atippōṉṟa toṉṟi ippōtu tāṉ ceytu vantēṉ; taṉṉiyaṟiyāmalē oru ciṟiyāḷi eṉṉip piṉṟoṭāntu maruttuvak kaḻakattic cuṟṟivarac ceytēṉ. eṉṟu viṭi pakarntēṉ.
atu kēṭṭu naṭuvar avvaḻakkil akappaṭṭavaṉic ciṟikku aṉuppiṉār. piṉṉar avvaḻakku mēlnaṭumaṉṟattil tirumpavum ārāyccikkuvara, appōtu ataṉi ārāynta caṭṭanūl vallārkku yāṉ karutiya poruḷit tiṉiyaḷavukūṭa viḷaṅkappaṭuttal iyalavilli. ataṉāl, am mēl naṭuniliyar, yāṉ moḻintapaṭi, oruvar tammi yaṟiyāmalē maṟṟoruvarip piṉṟoṭarntu cellumāṟu ceyyakkūṭumeṉpati ac caṭṭanūl vallārkku nērē ceytu kāṭṭuvatil ētēṉun taṭiyuṇṭōveṉak kēṭṭār. ataṉi ārāyntu kāṭṭa viruppam uṭiyēṉ eṉṟālum, ataṉi muṭittuc ceyvēṉeṉṟu uṟuti pukala māṭṭēṉeṉṟu viṭi kūṟiṉēṉ. nammuṭiya nōkkaṅkaḷ iṉṉaveṉṟu teriviyāmal, yāṉ peyar kūṟiya mūvar āṇpālāri ammaṉṟattiṟku varuvikka am mēlnaṭuvar kaṭṭaḷi tantāl, etu ceyyakkūṭiyatō ataṉi yāṉ ceytu pārppēṉ eṉṟēṉ.
iraṇṭoru nāḷil am mēlnaṭuvariṉ maṉṟattiṟku varumāṟu vēṇṭikkoḷḷap paṭṭēṉ; appōtu aḵtu airōppiyarālum uḷnāṭṭavarālum niṟintiruntatu. nācir makamatu eṉpavaṉ uḷḷē koṇṭuvantu aṭippiṉuḷḷē niṟuttappaṭṭāṉ; ciṟitu nērattil yāṉ avaṉukku aṟituyili varuvittu viṟikkacceyta kikaḷōṭu avaṉi ammaṉṟattiṟku veḷiyē naṭattic ceṉṟu, ciṟitu tolivākap pāṭṭiyiṉmēl avaṉi naṭakkavuñ ceytēṉ; mēlum, yāṉvēṇṭiya nēram variyil avaṉuṭiya kikaḷip pala vakiyāṉa nilikaḷil viṟittu niṟkavuñ ceytēṉ. maṟupaṭiyum avaṉik koṇṭuvantu ammaṉṟattiṉ aṭippiṉkaṇ niṟuttiṉēṉ; appōtu, mēlnaṭuvarum caṭṭanūl vallunarum avaṉōṭu evvaḷavō pēcippārttum avaṉ avarkaḷi eḷḷaḷavum uṉṉikkavē yilli; ataṉāl avaṉi eḻuppi viṭumpaṭi avarkaḷ eṉṉik kēṭkavēṇṭiyavarāṉārkaḷ. yāṉum aṅṅaṉamē ceytēṉ. `m›Él¤ijÉ£L btËna br‹widah? eṉṟu aṅkirunta caṭṭanūlār viṉava, `illi eṉṟu uṟutiyākac coṉṉāṉ. avarkaḷ avaṉōṭu uriyāṭik koṇṭu irukkiyil, avaṉ kāṇātapaṭi yāṉ avaṉukkuppiṉṉē ceṉṟu, avaṉ pēcikkoṇṭu cellum niliyilēyē avaṉukku aṟituyili varuvittēṉ. appuṟam avaṉ pēcamāṭṭāta vaṉāyiṉāṉ; evaruṭiya kuralum avaṉukkuk kēṭkavilli; maṟupaṭiyum yāṉ avaṉ kaṇkaḷiṉmēl ūti avaṉi viḻikkacceytēṉ.
ataṉpiṉ  mātūp eṉpavaṉik koṇarntu aṭippiṉkaṇ niṟuttiṉar. nāṉ uḷnuḻintati avaṉ pārkkavilli. mēl naṭuvaruñ caṭṭanūlārum avaṉit tammukappaṭuttip pēcikkoṇṭiruntaṉar; avaṉ aṟivōṭu kiḷarcciyākap pēcukiyil yāṉ avaṉukkup piṉṉē ceṉṟu avaṉi viṟikkac ceytēṉ; kaṭavuḷi nōkki āṇiyiṭṭuc collum ciṟiyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻakkappaṭi, avaṉ collik koṇṭu varukiyil oru noṭip poḻutil pēcavum colvatik kātāṟ kēṭkavum māṭṭātavaṉāy iṭiyē niṉṟu pōṉāṉ. kātu kēṭka māṭṭātavaṉāy niṉṟa piṟakum, avaṉ vāyitaḻkaḷ pēcutaṟku acivaṉapōṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu avaṉukku etiriliruntōr eṉakkuc colliṉar. aciyum āṟṟal muḻutum aṟṟuppōka avaṉ avvaḷavu viṟittup pōṉamiyāl, avaṉi naṭakkumpaṭi ceytaṟkākap piṉṉēyiruntu eṉ viralkaḷāl yāṉ avaṉit taḷḷa vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu; iṭarppāṭṭōṭu avaṉ ciṟitu vaḻi naṭantāṉ; tirumpavum avaṉ muṭiyiliruntu aṭivariyil caṭutiyil viṟittamiyāl yāṉ avaṉi mella muṉṉukku unta, mikavun tiṭukkiṭattakka vakiyāy avaṉ nilattiṉmītu talikīḻāy viḻuntuviṭṭāṉ; mikavirivil avaṉ viṟittati yāṉ aṟiyāmaṟ pōṉēṉ. ciṟitu varuttappaṭṭu yāṉ avaṉi eḻuppa vēṇṭuva tāyiṟṟu; nallakālamāy avaṉ kāyam aṭiyavilli.
ataṉpiṟaku corūpacantaṉ eṉpavaṉ koṇarap paṭṭāṉ. yāṉ avaṉi oru tiṅkaḷākap pārāmiyāl, avaṉatu nalattip paṟṟiyum piṟavaṟṟippaṟṟiyum ucāviṉēṉ; avvāṟu ucāvukiyilēyē avaṉukku aṟituyil varuvikkum muṟiyic ceytēṉ. ciṟitu nērattilellām avaṉ viṭikūṟa iyalātavaṉāṉāṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉi aṭippi ṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭutti, viṟitta kikaḷōṭu pamparampōl avaṉ cuḻaṉṟuvaracceytu, muḻutum uṇarvaṟṟa niliyilēyē avaṉi muṉṉirunta iṭattil vantirukkappaṇṇiṉēṉ. avaṉ kātiṟ kēṭkumpaṭiyākavāvatu, uyirōṭirukkum aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi avaṉ kāṭṭumpaṭiyākavāvatu, ceyya evarālum iyalavilli. āyiṉum, yāṉ avaṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷiṉmēl ūtiya vuṭaṉē, avaṉ taṉ aimpoṟiyuṇarvu kaḷum varappeṟṟut tāṉ avviṭatti viṭṭu akaṉṟa tilliyeṉak kūṟiṉāṉ.
anta ampaṭṭaṉ aṟituyil muṟiyāl acciṟuvaṉik kavarntukoṇṭu pōyiṉaṉō allatappaṭiyilliyō yāṉ ataṉit tīrmāṉikkap pukavilli; āyiṉum, avvāṟu ceyyakkūṭum eṉpataṉip palarum naṉku aṟiyumāṟu meyppaṭuttik kāṭṭutaṟku eṉakku aḵtu ōr amayantantatu; yāṉ mūṉṟu peyarip pala muṉṉiliyiṟ kavarntu ceṉṟataṉi evarum maṟukkat tuṇiyavilli. nāṭṭupparikāri oruvaṉ evvaḷavu eḷitāy ataṉic ceytalkūṭumō, avvaḷavukku yāṉum ōr āṭavaṉiyāvatu allatoru peṇmakaḷi yāvatu allatu oru ciṟupiḷḷiyiyāvatu kavarntu cellakkūṭum. aṟituyiliṉ āḻatti yāṉ nērē kaṇṭaṟinta kālam mutal ataṉ vallamiyāl tīmiyum viḷiyum naṉmiyum viḷiyumeṉṟu tiṭṭamāy uṇarappeṟṟēṉ. ataṉāl uṇṭām naṉmi tīmikaḷiṉ uṇarcciyi ellār maṉattilum paṭumpaṭi ceyyum nōkkattiṉālēyē ataṉi avvaḷavu āḻamākac ceṉṟu ārāyntu pārttēṉ. aṟivu viḷakkattiṉ poruṭṭākavum nōytīrkkum poruṭṭākavum allāmal, immuṟiyi vēṟu tīyavakikaḷiṟ payaṉ paṭuttuvāri ellāruṅ kaṭintu collum nāḷiṉvaravu tolivil illiyeṉṟu nāṉ nampukiṉṟēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam uṇmiyāka nikaḻnta innikaḻcciyik koṇṭu aṟituyiṉ muṟikaḷiṟ paḻakit tērccipeṟṟa oruvar tām vēṇṭiyavāṟellām piṟari naṭakkac ceyya vallunar āvar eṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō? mēlē kāṭṭiya turimakaṉār terivittapaṭi, koṭiyōrit tiruttit tām virumpiya naṉmikaḷukkut tiruppik koḷḷavum, palavēṟu piḻipāṭāṉa vaḻikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu aṟivilarāy oḻukit tuṉpuṟuvārkku aṟivuteruṭṭi avari attuṉpattiṉiṉṟu viṭuvittuk koḷḷavum, maruntukaḷiṉun tīrāk kaṭunōykaḷit tīrttuk koḷḷavum ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟiyiṉip payaṉpaṭuttuvatu mika naṉṟu. in naṉṉeṟi yallāta piṟavaṟṟil itaṉip payaṉpaṭuttaṟku oru ciṟitum niṉittal ākātu.
maṟṟōr airōppiya turimakaṉār (Carl Sextus, see his admirable book on hypnotism, p. 241) ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟikaḷil mikavun tērcci peṟṟiruntār. avar vaṭa amerikkāviluḷḷa cikkākō nakarattiṟ ceṉṟu palari aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu viyappāṉa palakāṭcikaḷik kāṭṭiṉār. annakarattil oru pattirikikku āciriyarāṉa paṇṭi eṉpavar tam ēvaṟkāraṉ oruvaṉ poruttup piṭippālum kāyccalālum varun tiṉamiyiṉ, annōykaḷit tīrkkumporuṭṭu ak kaliñarit tamatu illattiṟku varuvittār. avarum ataṟkicintu orunāḷ muṟpakaliṟ pattumaṇikku aṅkē ceṉṟār. piṟaku tām aṅkē ceytataṉit tāmē eḻutukiṉṟār; atu varumāṟu: nāṉ avaṉatu paṭukkiyaṟikkuṭ pukuntatum avaṉ paṭukkiyiṉ talimāṭṭiṉkaṭ ceṉṟu niṉṟu, eṉ kaṇkaḷi uṟṟunōkkumpaṭi avaṉukkuk kaṟpittēṉ. nāṉku nimiṭaṅkaḷ kaḻintatum, avaṉ eṉṉuṭiya niṉivāṟṟaluḷ muḻutum akappaṭṭu niṉṟāṉ. piṟaku avaṉuṭiya uṟuppukaḷip pala nilikaḷil niṟuttiṉēṉ; annilikaḷi avaṉ māṟṟa muṭiyātavaṉāṉāṉ. iḵtu, avaṉ viṟippu niliyiliruk kiṉṟāṉ eṉpatit teḷivuṟak kāṭṭiṟku. iraṇṭumaṇi nērattiṟkuḷ avaṉ mikavum nalamaṭiyap peṟuvāṉeṉṟum, paṭukkiyiṉiṉṟum eḻa virumpuvā ṉeṉṟum, ataṉpiṉ nallavuṇavi uṭkoḷvāṉeṉṟum, vaḻakkampōla avaṉ taṉ vēliyic ceyyap pukuvāṉeṉṟuṅ kaṭṭuri moḻikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. piṟpakal iraṇṭu maṇikku avaṉ kiḷarcciyāyt taṉ paṭukkiyi viṭṭeḻuntu, nalattōṭum viriviṟ paciyeṭuttut taṉatu pakaluṇavi uṇṭāṉ. piṉṉar nāṉku maṇikkellām avaṉ muṟṟilum nalamaṭintamiyiṉ pāṭikkoṇṭuñ cīḻkki aṭittukkoṇṭum taṉakku vaḻakkamāyuḷḷa vēliyic ceyyavallaṉ āṉāṉ.
iṉi, itaṟku muṟkāṭṭiya eṭēl, eṉṉum āṅkila maruttuvar, koṭiya piḷavik kaṭṭikaḷālum āṟāp puṇkaḷālum neṭunāḷ tuṉpuṟṟa nōyāḷikaḷ palarkku aṟituyili varuvittu avarkaṭku nōy ciṟitun teriyāmalē avaṟṟi yaṟuttu nalappaṭuttiṉa varalāṟukaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi iṅṅaṉē moḻipeyarttu varikiṉṟām: - aimpatu āṇṭuḷḷa muralitā eṉṉum oru kuṭiyāṉavaṉ ceṉṟa ōr āṇṭākak kuruttelumpip paṟṟiya oru koṭumpuṇṇāl tuṉpuṟṟukkoṇṭiruntāṉ; appuṇ avaṉatu iṭatu kāliṉ keṇṭic catiyil tōlukkumēl ariviraṟkaṭi uyaramākavum āṟuviral nīḷamākavum iruntatōṭu, avaṉatu antak kāliyum toṭikku nērkōṇamāy niṟkumpaṭi muṭakkiviṭṭatu. mutaṉmutalāka avaṉ iṉṟikkuttāṉ aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭṭāṉ; avaṉi attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuppāmal vittuk koṇṭē, paḻukkak kāycciyatōr irumpi appuṇṇiṉuḷ āḻa nuḻittu ataṉit tīyttēṉ. maṟunāḷ ataṉippārkka ataṉ mēṟpuṭippu nirampat taṭippāyum aḻuttamāyum iruntamiyāl, ataṉi akkeṇṭic catiyiṉiṉṟum aṟutteṭukka vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu; ākavē, avaṉukkut tirumpavum aṟituyil varuvikkappaṭṭatu. avaṉ uṇarātapaṭi cuṟṟilum uḷḷa tōliṉ maṭṭattiṟku appuṇṇiṉ puṭippu cīvit taḷḷappaṭṭatu. itaṟkaṭuttanāḷ tirumpavum avaṉi aṟituyiliṟ kiṭattiyapōtu, muṭaṅkiya avaṉatu kāli muṟṟilum nimirttuc cirāy vittuk kaṭṭiṉēṉ; avaṉ viḻikkavilli; piṉṉar viḻittatum avaṉukku nōvilli; eṉ muḻuvaliyum niṟiyuṅkoṇṭu am muṭakkiṉi nimirttu kiyil eṉ kiyiṉkīḻ narampiṉ curuṭṭu neṟuneṟu veṉṟu piṭippu viṭuvati yuṇarntēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam nōytīrtta āṅkila maruttuvar tamakku utaviyāṉa maṟṟoru maruttuvari ēvi maṟṟumoru nāṭpaṭṭa nōyip pōkkiṉa varalāṟum iṅku moḻipeyart teḻutuvām: - intiyāvil irupatu āṇṭukaḷāka irukkum kālṭar eṉṉun turi iṉṟikku eṉṉiṭam vantu tām poruttup piṭippiṉālum kikāl naṭukkattiṉālum taḷarcciyiṉālun tuṉpuṟuvatāka muṟiyiṭṭukakoṇṭār. mikunta varuttattōṭu oru taṭiyi ūṉṟikkoṇṭu naṭakkiṉṟār; mettippaṭiyiṉmēl avarāl ēṟa iyalātu. avaruṭiya narampukaḷ nuṟuṅki viṭṭaṉa, avaratu kaṭpārvi maṅkippōy viṭṭatu, eḻutukiyil avaratu ki naṭuṅkukiṉṟatu. maṭṭamāṉa tariyiṉmēl naṭakkuṅkāl kīḻē vaḻukkuvatāka avarukku ōr eṇṇan tōṉṟukiṉṟatu; ataṉāl avar tammik kāttukkoḷḷumpaṭi ceyyum muyaṟciyē avarik kīḻē taḷḷiviṭukiṉṟatu. cilakālamāka avar tamatu vaṇṭimēliruntum ataṉic celutta muṭivatilli; aḵtēṉ eṉṟāl, tamakketirēvarum vaṇṭiyōṭu tamatu kutiripōymuṭṭikkoḷḷātapaṭi ceyyat tammāl ākāteṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. avar inta nilimiyiṟ ceṉṟa iraṇṭari āṇṭukaḷāka iruntu varukiṟār; akkāla mellām avar ettaṉiyō maruttuvariṭañ ceṉṟu, ēṟakkuṟiya oru maruttuvak kaḻakattil uḷḷa maruntavvaḷavuñ cāppiṭṭār; ētoru payaṉum uṇṭākavilli. eṉ maṉaccāṉṟukku māṟāka iṉi avarukku maruntu koṭukka muṭiyāteṉṟu kūṟi, avarukku naṉmiyi uṇṭākka nīṇṭa nāḷum poṟumiyum vēṇṭumēṉum, avaratu nilimi aṟituyiṉ muṟiyāl nōytīrttaṟku ēṟṟa toṉṟākumeṉa eṇṇi, iyaṟki nikaḻcci avarkku yātu ceyyakkūṭu meṉpati āṇṭu pārkkumpaṭi avarkku eṭuttuc coṉṉēṉ. nōytīrttaṟku itaṟku muṟceytu pārātatoṉṟip piṭittup pārppatil avarukku mikunta makiḻcci uṇṭāyiṟṟu; ovvōr iravum paṭukkiyi liruntapaṭiyākavē varukku orumaṇi nēram variyil aṟituyil varuvikkumpaṭi eṉ tuṇiyāṭkaḷil oruvarkkuk kaṟpittēṉ.
kālṭar eṉṉum atturimakaṉār aṟituyiliṟ kiṭantapōtu yāṉ avarkiṭṭa orukālum irunta tillāmiyāl, avar tamatu nilimiyip paṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu eṉakku eḻutiyaṉuppiya kuṟippukkāka avarukku nāṉ kaṭamip paṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ceṉṟa cepṭampart tiṅkaḷ ki.pi. 1845ām āṇṭik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār 25-ām nāḷ, taṅkaṭku utaviyāyuḷḷa nāṭṭu maruttuvar, tāṅkaḷkaṟpitta paṭiyākavē, eṉakku mutaṉmutal aṟituyili varuvikka muyaṉṟār; āṉāl, aṉṟikku atu kikūṭavilli; ammuyaṟci oṉṟarimaṇi nēram naṭipeṟṟatu. 26, 27 ām nāṭkaḷilum aṅṅaṉamē; āṉāl, 28ām nāḷ atē nēram variyil viṭāppiṭiyāymuyaṉṟatil, yāṉ āḻntaṟi tuyiliṟ celuttappaṭṭiruntu, aintumaṇi nērattiṟku mēṟṟāṉ viḻitteḻuntēṉ. eṉ kaṇṇiṟippikaḷi mēl nimirttuvatilum, eṉ kaṇkaḷit tiṟantu vippatilum yāṉ peritum iṭarppaṭṭēṉ. yāṉ eṉatu cāymaṇikkaṭṭili viṭṭiṟaṅki, eṉ paṭukkikkup pōṉēṉ; maṟunāṭ kāli āṟumaṇi variyil yāṉ iyaṟkiyāṉa tuyiliṟ kiṭantēṉ. oru kiḻamikkuppiṉ, eṉṉi aṟituyiliṟ celuttum muyaṟci nāṭōṟum oru maṇi nēram variyil naṭipeṟṟatu; āṉāṟ cila nimiṭam variyiṟ ṟūṅkac ceytaṟku mēṟ piṟitoṉṟum viḷiyavilli. eṉṟālum, iraviṉkaṇ iyaṟkiyāṉa tūkkattil iṉitākat tuyiṉṟu varalāṉēṉ; eṉatu nōyiṉ tuṉpam nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟak kuṟintu varalāyiṟṟu; eṉṉuṭiya narampukaḷ nirampavum iṟukkamuṟṟaṉa; ippōtu nāṉ utavi vēṇṭāmalē mettip paṭikkaṭṭiṉmēl mēluṅ kīḻumāy naṭakkak kūṭiyavaṉāṉēṉ; vaṇṭiyiyum yāṉ maṉat tiṭpattōṭu celuttak kūṭum; āṉāl iraṇṭari āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ yāṉ iṅṅaṉañ ceyya muyaṉṟirukka muṭiyātu. akṭōpar, 17ām nāḷ variyil, ovvoru mūṉṟallatu nāṉkāmnāḷ iravum yāṉ iraṇṭumaṇi nēram variyil aṟituyiliṟ celuttap paṭṭuk kiṭantēṉ; ataṉāl viḷinta naṉmiyi, nēṟṟuk kāliyil yāṉ nāṉku milukku mēlum, iṉṟik kāliyil āṟu milukku mēlum naṭakka vallaṉāyiruppatu koṇṭu tāṅkaḷē terintu koḷḷalām.
itukāṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷik koṇṭu oruvarit tam vaḻippaṭuttalum, avarkkuḷḷa nāṭpaṭṭa koṭiya nōykaḷi nīkkutalum, piḷavikkaṭṭikaḷ puṇkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi avar aṟiyāmalē aṟuttut tīrttalum niṉivaṟṟa āḻnta aṟituyiliṉkaṇ avaric celuttutalāl eḷitil muṭikkappaṭum eṉpati naṉkuṇarntu koḷka.
17. teḷivukkāṭci
iṉi naṭittuyiliṉ payaṉkaḷippaṟṟic ciṟitu pēcuvām. naṭittuyiliṉkaṭ celuttappaṭṭa oru tūya uyir iṟantakāla nikaḻkāla etirkāla nikaḻccikaḷit terinturikka valla tātali mēlē viḷakkip pōntām. innaṭit tuyilāṉatu niṉivaṟṟa āḻnta aṟituyiliṉum āḻamāyc ceṉṟapiṉ varuvatākum. ittuyilil iruppōr atilirukkum variyil, iyaṟkiyāka viḻittiruppōrip pōṟ pēcutalum naṭattaluñ ceyvarāyiṉum, avaratu niṉivu appōtu mika nuṇṇiya aṟivuṭampip paṟṟikkoṇṭu iyaṅkutalāl, avar iyaṟkiyāka viḻittirukkum niliyiṟ kāṇamāṭṭāta, maṟinta arumperuṅ kāṭcikaḷi yellāṅ kāṇamāṭṭuvar. itu tavavoḻukkattil niṟpōrkkut tāṉākavē varuva toṉṟākiyāl, tavakkāṭci yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ aruntavattōrkkum, ivvaṟituyilil iruppōrkkum vēṟupāṭeṉṉi yeṉiṉ; aruntavattōr tāmākavē iṭiyaṟātu ceytu pōtarun tavamuyaṟciyāl, tamatuṇarviṉi aṟiyāmiyiṉiṉṟum pirittu viḷaṅkacceytu, tām vēṇṭiyapōtu ivvuṇavuṭampu, uyirppuṭampu, maṉavuṭampu, aṟivuṭampu, iṉpavuṭampu  (vaṭanūlār ivaṟṟi muṟiyē aṉaṉamayakōcam, pirāṇamayakōcam, maṉōmayakōcam, viññāṉamayakōcam, āṉantamayakōcam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar) eṉṉum aivaki yuṭampukaḷiṉum naṭipeṟacceytu āṇṭāṇṭu nikaḻum maṟiporuṇikaḻccikaḷi yellān tāmākavē eḷitiluṇarap peṟuvar; avarkaṭku maṟatiyum aṟiyāmiyum uḷavākā. maṟṟup piṟar oruvaratu utaviyāl naṭittuyiliṉkaṭ celuttappaṭuvārō tāmākavē aṟivuṭampiṟ cellamāṭṭāmiyōṭu, āṇṭu nikaḻcci kaḷiyun tāmākavē aṟinturikka māṭṭuvār allar; atu vallāmalum, tām viḻittirukkum niliyil uṇarntavaṟṟi naṭittuyiliṉkaṭ ceṉṟavuṭaṉē maṟantum, naṭittuyiliṉkaṇ uṇarntavaṟṟi viḻippu nilikku vantavuṭaṉ maṟantum aṟiyāmiyiṟ paṟṟappaṭṭavarāyiruppar; ovvōruṭampilum vevvēṟu uyirpōl naṭappar; aruntavattiṉarō avvellā vuṭampukaḷilum ōruyirākavē oru toṭarpupaṭṭa vuṇarvuṭiyarāyiruppar. ituvē naṭittuyiliṉkaṭ celuttap paṭuvōrkkum, aruntavat tōrkkum uḷatākiya vēṟṟumiyām. ātalāl, aruntavattōrkku nikaḻvatit tavakkāṭci yeṉavum, naṭittuyilil iruppōrkku nikaḻvatit teḷivuk kāṭci yeṉavum avvēṟṟumi tōṉṟak kūṟiṉām.
iṉi, innaṭittuyilil iruppōrkkuk kaṇkaḷ mūṭiyiruppiṉun tiṟantiruppiṉum avar kāṇpaṉa nam ūṉa viḻikaḷukku nērē pulappaṭum poruḷkaḷ alla; maṟṟu naṅ kaṇkaḷukku nērēyillāmal tolivilum aṇimiyilum maṟintiruppavikaḷēyām. maṉattūymiyum nuṇṇaṟivu muṭiya nam māṇavar oruvari orukāl nām āḻnta aṟituyiliṟ celutta avaruṭaṉē naṭittuyiliṟ ceṉṟu teḷivukkāṭci yuṭiyarāṉār. appōtavar kaṇkaḷ mūṭiyē iruntaṉa. anniliyil avar maṟinta poruḷkaḷik kāṇa vallunarāy irupparā eṉat terital vēṇṭi, ematu kikkaṭikāratti eṭuttu avaratu piṉṟalippuṟattē piṭittu ippōtu nīr ikkaṭikārattik kāṇkiṉṟīr; itaṉ mukattaṭṭiṉmēl maṇi muḷḷum nimiṭamuḷḷum iyaṅkuvatiyun teḷivāyk kāṇkiṉṟīr. ām, ivaṟṟik kāṇkiṉṟīrallavā? eṉṟu yām viṉāva, avartam kaṇkaḷ naṉṟāy mūṭiyiruntum ām, oru kik kaṭikārattik kāṇkiṉṟēṉ; ataṉ mukattaṭṭiṉmēl muṭkaḷ  acikiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟu avar annaṭittuyiliruntapaṭiyē viṭi kūṟiṉār. ataṉpiṉ appaṭiyāṉāl ippōtu maṇi eṉ? collum eṉṟēm. ataṟkavar ippōtu āṟaṭikka aintu nimiṭam eṉṟu picakāmaṟ kūṟiṉār. aṉṟu mālikkālam, avar colliyapaṭiyē oru nimiṭamum picakavilli; āṟaṭikka aintu nimiṭaṅkaḷē iruntaṉa. itukaṇṭu viyappuṟṟēm.
orukāl yām tirunelvēlikkuc ceṉṟirukkiyil, iv vaṟituyiṉmuṟikaḷil tērccipeṟṟa ōr aṉpar tammāl aṟituyiliṟ palakāṟ celuttappaṭṭa oru pārppaṉa iḷiñari emmiṭam aḻittu vantār. atu mālik kālam, appōtutāṉ viḷakkēṟṟappaṭṭatu. avar avviḷiñari oru cāymāṉak kuṟicciyil amarttic ciṟitu nērattil āḻnta naṭittuyiliṟ pōkacceytu, ippōtu ivar teḷivukkāṭci yuṭiyavarāyirukkiṉṟār. tāṅkaḷ ētēṉum oru poruḷik kiyil maṟittu vittuk koṇṭāl, ataṉi ivar uṭaṉē aṟintu colvar eṉṟār. yām accamayattiṟ kiṭitta oru cāviyi atu kōkkappaṭṭa kayiṟṟōṭum eṭuttu aṅkiruntār evarkkun teriyāmal ematu valatu kiyiṉuḷ maṟittu vittuk koṇṭēm. piṟaku avar aṟituyililirukkum avviḷiñari nōkki ippōtu nīr viḷakkukaḷik kāṇkiṉṟīr, ellām orē veḷiccamāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. umakkup pakkattē niṟkum ivarkaḷiyuṅ kāṇkiṉṟīr eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
avviḷiñarum ataṟkiṇaṅki ām, orē oḷiyā yirukkiṉṟatu. nīṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭavarkaḷiyum pārkkiṉṟēṉ. eṉṟāl. appaṭiyāṉāl, ivarkaḷiṉ valatu kiyiṉuḷḷē eṉṉa irukkiṉṟatu? eṉṟu av aṉpar viṉaviṉār. ataṟkavar mutalil avar ki mūṭiyirukkiṉṟatu, yāṉ uḷḷē yiruppatip pārkkakkūṭavilli. eṉṟu viṭikūṟiṉār. maṟupaṭiyum avar, ippōtu naṉṟāy uṟṟuppārum, kiyiṉuḷḷē yiruppatu naṉṟāyt teriyum, veḷiccam naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu. eṉṟār. avviḷiñar ām, avar kiyiṉuḷḷē nīḷamāy oṉṟirukkiṉṟatu; ō, aḵtu oru ciṟu kayiṟu. eṉṟār. tirumpavum avar piṉṉum eṉṉa irukkiṉṟatu? naṉṟāy uṟṟuppārum. eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttuc coṉṉār. atil ētō oṉṟu ciṟitāyirukkiṉṟatu; āṉāl aḵtu iṉṉateṉṟu teriyavilli. eṉa moḻintār. avar illi. ippōtu atu naṉṟāyt teriyum. veḷiccam iṉṉum naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu; naṉṟāyp pārum eṉṟār. ām, ippōtu mikunta oḷi vīcukiṉṟatu; ō, aḵtu oru cāvi eṉṟu kaṭiciyāka uṇmiyic colliviṭṭār. ataṉpiṉṉum ematu pakkattilirunta iraṇṭoruvar vēṟu cilavaṟṟi maṟittu vittukkoḷḷa avikaḷiyum avar picakāmaṟ colliviṭṭār.
aṟituyiṉ muṟiyil mikavun tērnta oru turimakaṉ (Carl Sextus) oru ciṟu peṇṇikkoṇṭu neṭuntoliyil nikaḻntatoru nikaḻcciyic colluvittaṉar. ataṉi avar collukiṟapaṭiyē iṅku moḻipeyart teḻukiṉṟām: ēḻu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ cuvīṭaṉil uḷḷa kātaṉparkkil yāṉ iruntapōtu, pukaḻpeṟṟa celvar oruvar illattil aṟituyiluṇmiyippaṟṟi oru virivuri nikaḻtti, attuyilic cilarkku varuvittuṅ kāṭṭiṉēṉ. iṅṅaṉañ ceykiyil, mantirakkaṇṇāṭi (Magic mirror)Æš nōkkutalik kuṟittu yāṉ pēca, accelvar ataṉiyuñ ceytukāṭṭumpaṭi eṉṉik kēṭṭār. appōtu avaratu kuṭumpattiṟku aṟimukamāṉavarkaḷum naṇparkaḷumāka aṅkē kūṭiyirunta irupattintu muppatu peyaril, ammuṟiyic ceytu pārttaṟkut takkavar oruvarum illiyeṉa oru noṭippoḻutiṟ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ aṉṟumāli pattumaṇi nēram irukkum.
itaṟkut takkavar ivvivṟu irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu yāṉ kuṟittuk kāṭṭiyapaṭi, mikavum poruttamākap pattup patiṉōrāṇṭuḷḷa orumakaḷ tammiṭam aluval pārkkum oruvarukku iruppatāka akkūṭṭattil irunta tarakar oruvar kūṟiṉār. acciṟumiyiṉ peyarum iruppiṭamum avar collavē, avaḷ arukāmiyiliruntamiyāl avaḷi aḻittuvarumpaṭi virivāka ōr āḷ aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. avaḷ peṟṟōrkaḷ ammuṟiyit tērntu pārttaṟkāka ētoru taṭiyuñ collāmiyāl, pōṉavar acciṟumiyi aḻittu vantār. acciṟumiyi aṟituyiliṟ celutti, aṅku vikkappaṭṭa mantirak kaṇṇāṭiyi uṟṟunōkkumpaṭi ceytōm; appeṇ viyakkattakka iyaṟki nuṇuṇarvum ciṟanta teḷivuk kāṭciyum uṭiyaḷāyiruntāḷ. ṭōk kōmil uḷḷa ciṟṟaraci oruttiyik kāṇumpaṭi yāṉ avaḷukkuc coṉṉēṉ.
avaḷum ciṟitum malippaṭiyāmal `appaṭiyē eṉṟu uṭampaṭṭuc colli, acciṟṟaraciyiṉ vaṭivattiyum avaḷ aṉṟu māliyil amarntirukkum aṟiyiyum nirampa naṉṟāy viritturittāḷ. (cuvīṭaṉukkut talinakarāṉa ṭākkōm eṉpatu ippōtu yāṅkaḷirukkuṅ kātaṉpark eṉṉum nakarukku munnūṟṟimpatu mil tolivil uḷḷatu; icciṟumi acciṟṟaraciyi oru kālum pārttavaḷum allaḷ.) ataṉ piṟaku, acciṟumi, avvaṟiyil avvaraciyōṭu uṭaṉirukkum ōr iḷiñaṉiyuṅ kaṇṭa avaṉṟaṉ uruvattiṉ taṉmi kaḷiyum nuṭpamāka viritturikkap pukuntāḷ.
atik kēṭṭu akkūṭṭattil iruntavarkaḷellām nakiyāṭiṉar. ceṉṟa āṟu tiṅkaḷāka avvaraciyiṉpāl mikunta kātalaṉpu uṭiyaṉāy avaḷip pārāṭṭik koṇṭu oḻukum av varaciḷiñaṉippaṟṟi acciṟumi paṭam eḻutik kāṭṭiṉāṟpōṟ colliya viḷakkavuri avvaḷavum ottiruntaṉa. āṉālum avaḷatu viḷakkavuri varavara aḷavukku miñcic ceṉṟamiyālum, pulliya taṉmiyuṭiya tāyiruntamiyālum yāṉ attaṉiyōṭu ataṉi niṟutta vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. acciṟṟaraciyiyum avaḷ kātalaṉiyum paṟṟiya oru kuṟippu aṅkiruntavar kēṭkat takkatāyilli; mēlum. avvillattiṟkuriya celvarkkum atu tuṉpan taruvatāyiruntatu. tāṉum taṉ naṭakkiyum ippōtu palaruṅ kāṇattakka niliyiliruppatic ciṟitum uṇarātu, katavukaḷ tāḻiṭṭuc cāttappaṭṭa aṟikkuḷ tāṉ pattiramāyiruppa taṉāl, tāṉ ūrār paḻiccollukkut tappik koṇṭatāka acciṟṟaraci eṇṇiyiruntāḷ. aṟituyilippaṟṟi āṅkāṅku virivuri nikaḻttum poruṭṭu nārvē muḻutum nāṉku tiṅkaḷ variyiṟ cuṟṟivarutaṟkāka aṭutta iraviṟ kiṟittiyāṉiyā nakarattiṟkuc ceṉṟēṉ. kātaṉpark nakarattiṟku yāṉ maṟupaṭiyum tirumpivanta poḻutu muṉṉē eṉakku viruntāṟṟiya ac celvarik kaṇṭēṉ.
naṭittuyiliṟ ceṉṟa ciṟumi ac ciṟṟaraciyip paṟṟit teḷivuk kāṭciyiṟ kaṇṭu kūṟiyavikaḷik kuṟittut tām iṭiyē ārāyntu pārkka avi yavvaḷavum uṇmiyā yirukkat teḷintatāka eṉakku eṭutturittār. ataṉōṭu, aṉṟu māliyil ac ciṟṟaraciyiṉ naṭakkiyip paṟṟi ellārum naṭiyāṭat takkatāka avaḷ muṉṉaṟintu colliya nikaḻcci oṉṟum nirampa uṇmiyāyiruntateṉṟum avar eṉakkaṟivittār. ivvāṟu aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟa ciṟcilar teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭiyarāyt tolivil nikaḻum nikaḻcci kaḷiyum nērē kāṇpatupōl teḷḷat teḷiyak kaṇṭurikkum viyappiṉi itiṟ ciṟitu muyaṟciyeṭuppā revarun tāmēyum nēriluṇarntu viyakkalām.
iṅṅaṉam nilapparappiṉmēl aṇmiyiluñ cēymiyilum maṟiviliruppaṉavaṟṟiyum naṭappaṉavaṟṟiyun teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭiyār kāṇavallarātal pōlavē, innilattiṉuḷḷē mikavum āḻamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyum avarkaḷ teḷiyak kaṇṭurikka vallavarkaḷā yirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. itaṉiyum uṇmiyā nikaḻnta oru nikaḻcciyiṉāl viḷakkik kāṭṭuvām. iḵtu amerikkāvilmeyyāka naṭantu, kalviyiṟ perumpukaḻ peṟṟa īṭaṉ (See the article by Dr. Samuel Eadonï M.A., M.D., LL.D. Ph. D., in “The Phrenological Annual” 1892) eṉpavarāl ōr itaḻil variyappaṭṭatu. ataṉi aṅkiruntapaṭiyē yeṭuttu iṅku moḻipeyart teḻutukiṉṟām: - cikkākō nakaramāṉatu, ulakattil nāṭōṟum virintu muṉṉēṟṟam aṭiyum nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟeṉpatu yāvarum naṉkaṟintatēyām. yōṉā eṉpavariṉ curikkoṭi orē iravil muḷittup paṭarntatu pōla, atuvum ōr iravil vaḷarkiṉṟatu. ataṉ kuṭimakkaḷ viriviṟ perukiviṭṭamiyāl, uyirvāḻkkikkuñ celvavāḻkkikkun taṇṇīr iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu. iyaṟkip poruṇūl (Science) picakip pōyiṟṟu; nilanūlvallār (Geologists) attakiya nilappaṭiyiṉ kīḻt taṇṇīr iruttal kūṭāteṉṟu colliviṭṭārkaḷ. mēṟparappilētāṉ taṇṇīrkkākap pārkkavēṇṭu vatāyiṟṟu; aḻukkāṉa taṇṇīriyē payaṉpaṭuttak koṭuttaṟku oru kūṭṭuc cērntatu.
inta nāḷil ac cikkākō nakarattil āpirakām jēm eṉṉum peyaruṭiya oruvaṉ iruntāṉ; avaṉ kaḷaṅkam aṟṟavaṉ, kiṟittumatattiṟkup puṟampāṉavar kuṭiyiṟ piṟantavaṉ, kalviyaṟivillātavaṉ, uṇmiyil oṉṟumēyaṟiyāta ōr āḷ. anta jēm eṉpavaṉ iyaṟkiyākavē teḷivukkāṭci vāynta ōr uṇmiyāṉa karuviyeṉṟum, avaṉ naṭit tuyiliṉkaṭ celuttappaṭṭapōtu annakarattiṟku aṇṭiyiṟ kīḻē kuṟippāṉa oru nilappāṅkil ērāḷamāṉa taṇṇīrum maṇṇeṇṇeyum irukkiṉṟaṉavāka aṟivittaṉaṉeṉṟum kāroliṉ jārṭaṉ eṉṉum mātar oruvar veḷiyaṟi vittaṉar. nīṇṭakālam variyil avaṉ kūṟiyati evarumē uṉṉittup pārkkavilli. kaṭiciyākac cikkākō nakarattiṟku meyiṉ eṉṉum ūriliruntu aluvalmēlvanta uviṭheṭ, kāṭ eṉṉum iruvar, āpirakām jēm eṉpavaṉ kūṟiya ceytiyik kēṭṭut tōṇṭiṉāl ērāḷamāṉa taṇṇīr kiṭikkum iṭamāka avaṉāṟ collappaṭṭa nilattaṇṭi avaṉi aḻittuk koṇṭu ceṉṟārkaḷ. naṭittuyiliṟ ceṉṟatum, jēm avviṭatti uṭaṉē cuṭṭikkāṭṭiṉāṉ. tāṉ attaṇṇīrip pārppatōṭu, aḵtu, avarkaḷ appōtu niṟkum iṭattiṟku iraṇṭāyiram miltoli vil uḷḷa malippāṟi kaḷiliruntu tōṉṟip pala nila aṭukkukaḷiṉūṭum nilavaṟi kaḷiṉūṭum ōṭivaruvatiyun tāṉ orupaṭattileḻutik kāṭṭa kkūṭumeṉṟum avarkaṭkuc coṉṉāṉ. uṭaṉē annilatti vilikoṭuttu vāṅkum ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytu, ataṉit tōṇṭutaṟkut tuvaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉan tuvaṅkiyatu ki.pi 1864ām āṇṭu pipravarit tiṅkaḷilēyākum; navampart tiṅkaḷ variyil nāṭōṟum nilattit tōṇṭikkoṇṭē pōka, eḻunūṟṟup patiṉōraṭi āḻattiṟ ceṉṟatun taṇṇīr vantu taṭṭiyatu; uṭaṉē irupattunāṉku maṇi nērattiṟku munnūṟāyiram marakkāl taṇṇir veḷikkiḷampi ōṭalāyiṟṟu.
tamarūci tuḷittuc ceṉṟa pottalāṉatu kīḻuḷḷa nilappaṭikaḷiṉ vakikaḷik kāṭṭiyatu; ippaṭikaḷ attaṉiyum muṉṉamē teḷivukkāṭciyuṭiya avaṉ kaṇṭu coṉṉamiyāl, nērē kāṇum maṟṟavar kaṇkaḷukku avi ataṉ uṇmiyi meypeṟak kāṭṭiṉa . mutal nūṟu aṭivariyil tamarūci tuḷittuc ceṉṟatu aṭimaṇ paṭiyāyiruntatu; ataṟkuk kīḻē muppattintu aṭivariyil eṇṇeyōṭu ūṟiya cukkāṉkaṟ paṭiyāyiruntatu, antac cukkāṉkal evvakiyāṉa nilakkariyiyumpōl naṉṟāy eriyum; ataṟkuṅ kīḻē nūṟu aṭivariyil calavikkaṟpaṭi; ataṟkuṅkīḻē nūṟṟirupattintu aṭivariyiṟ cempup poṭiyum irumpup poṭiyuṅ kalantu cakkimukkik kallum maṇalum oruṅku tiraṇṭa paṭi; ataṟkuṅ kīḻē nūṟṟim pattāṟu aṭikāṟum maṇṇeṇṇey kalantu koḻuppup pōl taṭippāyum nuṇṇiya cuṇṇāmpu pōl melliya tāyumuḷḷa vaṇṭal mēlē varappeṟṟa kaṟpāṟippaṭi; ataṉiyaṭuttu ainnūṟṟu muppatu aṭikāṟum īyaṅkalanta cukkāṉkaṟpaṭi; piṟaku aṟunūṟṟu muppattoṉpataṭi variyil kantakattōṭu kalanta irumpum cakkimukkik kallum uḷḷa cukkāṉkaṟpaṭi mikavum aḻuttamāyiruntamiyāl ataṉit turuvikkoṇṭu cellum vēli metuvāy naṭipeṟṟatu. intanērattil nilattiṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa āvikaḷ mēleḻumpu taliṉāl oru kontaḷippu aṭikkaṭi uṇṭāyiṟṟu; taṇṇīr caṭutiyil muppatu aṭiyiliruntu aṟupatu aṭi kīḻēpōyt tamarūciyiṉ ciṟutuṇṭukaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum eṭuttuk koṇṭu maṟupaṭiyuñ caṭutiyil mēlēvantatu. iṉṉun, tuḷikkum vēli naṭantu koṇṭē pōyiṟṟu; piṉṉar eḻunūṟṟup patiṉōraṭi āḻamuḷḷa oru malippāṟiyiṉ vaḷivu muḻutun tuḷikkap paṭṭavuṭaṉē, aṭiyil nālari viraṟkaṭi akalamuḷḷa ōr uruvutuḷiyi liruntu mikak kuḷirnta taṇṇīrāṉatu paḷiṅkupōṟ ṟeḷivuṭiyatāy, virampōṟ ṟūyatāy, ūṉkaḻivu puṟpūṇṭukaḷiṉ kaḻivu etuvum oruciṟituṅ kalavātatāyp, parukutaṟkum uṭampiṉ nalam pēṇutaṟkum ituvariyiṟ kiṭitta entat taṇṇīriṉum mikac ciṟantatāy, iṉi varuṅkālamellām ēḻi makkaḷukku  uṟṟatoru naṭpeṉap payaṉpaṭuvatāyc caṭutiyil mēṟ kiḷampiṟṟu.
itō nampikkiyaṟṟavarkaḷukkup peṉṉam periya tōr uṇmiyāṉa ceyti! kaṭpulaṉākāta oru viṉimutalāl veḷik koṇarappeṟṟu, ettakiyōrāṉum etirmaṟukkak kūṭāta ōr uṇmi nikaḻcci! iyaṟkipporuṇūl Natural Science. taṇṇīr kaṇṭeṭukka muṭiyāteṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu; āṉāl maṉanūlō `nī moḻintatu poy, ēṉeṉṟāl, inta maṇṇulakamāṉatu cuḻaṉṟucelluṅ kālamvariyum ciṟanta veḷḷamāyp puraṇṭōṭuñ ceḻumpuṉal uḷḷa iṭam ituveṉṟu nāṉ kuṟittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ eṉak kūṟiṟṟu. ki.pi. 1864ām āṇṭumutal; cikkākō nakarattil eṭukkappaṭṭa intat tuḷikkiṇaṟṟiliruntu nāḷ oṉṟukku ēḻari ilakkam marakkāl taṇṇīr viḻukkāṭu mēlōṭi varukiṉṟatu. mēlum, ittaṇṇīrik kuḷaṅkaḷiṉum mikap periya toṭṭikaḷiṉuṅ koṇṭu pōy nirappik kaṭṭivittu, maḻi kālattil ciṟitēṟak kuṟiya nāṉūṟṟeḻupattāṟu maṇaṅku paṉikkaṭṭi ceytu viṟṟuc ceṭṭup piṭikkiṉṟārkaḷ. iṉṉum ippaṉikkaṭṭiyi vēṇṭumpōtu nāṉku maṭaṅkākkiyum viṟkalām. evarum maṟuttaṟku iyalātu, amerikka makkaḷiṉ kaṇṇetirē nikaḻnta ivvuṇmi nikaḻcciyiṉāl, makkaḷiṉ mukattēyuḷḷa puṟakkaṇkaḷukkup pulaṉākāmal in nilapparappiṉ mēluṅ kīḻum neṭuntolivil maṟintu kiṭakkum evvakip poruḷkaḷum avar tam akakkaṇṇiṉ etirē iṉitu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutalum, at tōṟṟat tiṉutaviyāl muyaṉṟu avaṟṟip puṟakkaṇkaḷuṅ kāṇumpaṭi ceytu akkāṭciyiṉ uṇmiyit teḷintu payaṉ peṟutalum uṇṭeṉpatu naṉku tuṇiyappaṭu kiṉṟataṉṟō?
iṉi, it teḷivukkāṭciyiṉ utaviyāṟ kāṇāmaṟ pōṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum kaṇṭeṭukkalām eṉpataṟku uṇmiyāy nikaḻnta maṟṟoru nikaḻcciyi, ittuṟiyil mikavallarāṉa maṟṟōr āciriyaratu nūliliruntu (How to thought - read by Dr. James Coates, Ph.D., F.A.S.) eṭuttu īṇṭu moḻipeyarttu varikiṉṟām: - tiyaṭōr eṉṉum oru nīrāvik kappal piḷāṇṭu eṉṉum maṟṟoru kappal ilivarpūl eṉṉuṅ kaṭaṟṟuṟi paṭṭiṉattic cērnta aṉṟikkē vantu cērntatu. aḵtu eṅkaḷuṭiya kappalip pārkkilum periyatu; eṉṟālum ataṉ kaṇ uḷḷa carakkum eṅkaḷ kappalil uḷḷatip pōṉṟatē. `ellām cevviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu terivikkum poruṭṭāka nāṉ uṭiyavarkaḷiṭam ceṉṟa aṉṟikku, mīkāṉ mārṭṭaṉ eṉpavari mikavuṅ kalavaramāṉa nilimiyil etirppaṭṭēṉ; ēṉeṉṟāl, tamatu campaḷamum tammōṭu vēlipārppavar campaḷamuñ cērnta tokikku īṭāṉa viliyuḷḷa muppatu pañcup potikaḷi avar iḻantu viṭṭār. avar ataṉporuṭṭu mikavuṅ kalaṅkiṉamiyāl, avartam maṉiviyum āṟṟāmi mikkavaḷākit, teḷivukkāṭciyil tiṟamivāynta ōr iḷam peṇṇi yuṭiyarāṉa haṭcaṉ eṉṉuṅ kappaṟṟalivaṉiṭañ ceṉṟu ucāvumpaṭi avarit tūṇṭiṉāḷ. nāṅkaḷ iruvēmum teḷivukkāṭciyip paṟṟiya ceytiyil aiyuṟavu koṇṭavarkaḷāka iruntōm. `ikkālattiya mantira mūṭṭiyip pōṉṟavaṟṟil tām taliyiṭṭukkoḷḷa viruppam illiyeṉṟu mārṭṭaṉ mutalil terivittār; āṉāl, muṭivākat tam maṉiviyiṉ vēṇṭukōḷukku iṇaṅkippōka uṭaṉpaṭṭār.
mutalil mālumi haṭcaṉukku oṉṟēkāl rūpā kaṭṭaṇañ celuttiyapiṉ, teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭiya avviḷam peṇṇik kaṇṭupēca iṭampeṟṟōm. teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭiya aṟituyilukku av viḷampeṇ celuttap paṭṭapiṉ, mārṭṭaṉ ap peṇṇiṉ valatu kiyit tamatu valakkiyiṟ piṭittuk koṭu, tām kēṭka virumpiyavikaḷik kēṭkalām eṉṟu kaṟpikkappaṭṭār.
avaḷ kūṟiya viṭikaḷ piṉvarumāṟu iruntaṉa:  - tiyaṭōr eṉṉuṅ kappal mutaliṟ puṟappaṭṭa tuṟimukattiṟkut tirumpa niṉiviṉāṟ ceṉṟāḷ. ak kappalāṉatu ilivarppūli viṭṭu aṭlāṇṭik kaṭalik kuṟukkē kaṭantu, mēlintiya tīvukaḷiṉ ūṭē maṟupaṭiyum putu ārliyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattiṟkup pōṉavaḻiyit tirumpac collik kāṭṭiyatōṭu, taṉkikaḷālun toṭarntu cuṭṭikkoṇṭu vantāḷ. kaṭiciyāka, `ām, pañcup poti kāṇāmaṟpōṉatu ivviṭattilētāṉ. cuṟṟiluñ civappuk kuṟiyuḷḷa oru periya kaṟuppuk kappaliṉ mēṟṟaṭṭilē avikaḷ vikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟu avaḷ moḻintāḷ, ataṉ piṟaku, ak kappal taṉ iruppiṭattiṟkut tirumpi vanta vaḻiyit taṉ coṟkaḷālēyaṉṟik kiyiṉāluṅ kuṟippiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvā ḷāṉāḷ; āṉāl, aṭlāṇṭik kaṭalit tirumpak kuṟukkiṭṭu vantapiṉ, ilivarppūli nōkki airiṣ kālvāyil varuvativiṭṭup pirañcuk kariyi nōkkic celvatu pōl iṅkiliṣ kālvāyiṉ pakkamāyt tirumpiṉāḷ. ataṉpiṉ, taṉ kiyi nīṭṭi `ō, itō pañcup potikaḷ; aṅkuḷḷavarkaḷ ā, evvaḷavu pakaṭiyāṉa makkaḷāyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ; avarkaḷ iṅkilīṣ pēcavilli. uṭaṉē, mārṭṭaṉ mīkāmaṉ `nallatu, atutāṉ tāma mīkāmaṉiṉ piraṉvik, eṉṟār. niyūārliyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattiṉ tuṟimukattil tamatu kappalōṭu niṉṟu koṇṭirunta avvamerikkar kappal taṉakkuriya pañcuc carakki ēṟṟik koṇṭiruntatu.
appōtu tiyaṭōr tāṉuñ carakkēṟṟikkoṇṭu pirañcut tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamākiya hāvarukkup pōvatāyiruntatu. iṅṅaṉam ap peṇmakaḷ taṉatu teḷivuk kāṭciyāl, aṟintu coṉṉavaṟṟik kēṭṭu maṉa niṟivu uṭiyavarāy, mārṭṭaṉ mīkāmaṉ tamatu illattiṟkuc ceṉṟu, uṭaṉē tāma mīkāmaṉukku eḻutik kāṇāmaṟpōṉa tamatu carakkip paṟṟik kēṭṭār. ciṟitu kālaṅkaḻittup, pañcuppoti, uṇmiyākavē aṅkē uḷaveṉṟum, avi tuṟimukattiṉiṉṟum picakāka eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum, avi evarkku uriyaṉavāy iruntālum viṟpaṉi ceytaṟku āyattamāyiruntaṉa veṉṟum avarkku viṭivantatu. āṉāl, mārṭṭaṉ mīkāmaṉ, avi tirumpa aṉuppappaṭutaṟku ām celavu toki yaṉuppiṉāl, appañcuppotikaḷ uṭaṉē avarkku aṉuppappaṭumeṉpatun terivikkappaṭṭatu. avar aṅṅaṉamē ceyya pirañcu nāṭṭu hāvar tuṟimukattiliruntu iṅkilāntilirukkum ilivarppūl tuṟimukattiṟku varutaṟkuriya celavu maṭṭum uṭiyaṉavāy appañcup potikaḷ uriya kālattē avariṭam vantu cērntaṉa. ivi tām uṇmiyāka nikaḻntavi; āṉāl, annikaḻcci yiṉiyalpi viḷakkutaṟku yāṉ pukavilli. eṉṉum immeyc ceytiyāl, paṉṉāṭkaḷukkumuṉ neṭuntoliviṟ ṟavaṟip pōṉa poruḷkaḷum teḷivukkāṭci yuṭiyārāl eḷitiṟ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭum uṇmi naṉku nilipeṟukiṉṟataṉṟō?
iṉi, nōyāl varuntuvōriṉ nōyiṉ iyalpi aṟiyamāṭṭāmal tavikkum maruttuvar ataṉit tiṭṭamāy aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkun teḷivukkāṭci peritum utavi purivatākum. uṭampiṉ uḷḷē aminta karuvikaḷiṟṟōṉṟiya nōykaḷip puṟakkaṇkaḷ maṭṭum uṭiya maruttuvar yāṅṅaṉam uṇaravallār? akattuḷḷa nōyit tiṭṭamāka aṟiya māṭṭāmiyāl, anta nōykkuttakka maruntik koṭukka ēlāmal, takāta maruntikkoṭuttup piḻipaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. nōyāḷikaḷum poruṭcelavu ceytum maruntuṇṭum payaṉ peṟāmal neṭukat tuṉpuṟṟu uyiriyun tuṟakkiṉṟaṉar. āṅkila maruttuvariṟ palar nōyāḷikaḷiṉ akattē nōyirukku miṭanteriyāmal nōyillā vēṟiṭaṅkaḷi aṟuttup pārttu ēmāṟṟam aṭikiṉṟaṉar.
avartam aṟiyāmiyāl, annōyāḷikaḷum āṟṟutaṟkariya tuṉpameytic cākiṉṟaṉar. ātalāl, maruttuvap payiṟciyōṭu, teḷivuk kāṭciyāl nōyiṉ taṉmiyik kaṇṭaṟiyum muṟikaḷilum paḻakit tērvarāyiṉ attakiya maruttuvarāl ulakiṟku viḷiyum naṉmikaḷ aḷaviṭappaṭā. nōyiṉ iyalpu teḷiyappaṭāmiyāl oru nōyāḷiyum avarukku maruttuvañ ceyta āṅkila maruttuvarun teḷivuk kāṭciyiṉ utaviyāl attuṉpaṅkaḷ nīṅkappeṟṟamikku uṇmiyāy nikaḻnta maṟṟoru varalāṟṟiṉi mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa aṟituyiṟ pulami āciriyar (Dr. James Coates How to thought - Read) nūlilirunteṭuttu moḻipeyart teḻutukiṉṟām: - `iyaṟkiyākavē teḷivukkāṭci eṉṉum oru perum pēṟṟiṉi aṭintu taṉ maṭṭil uyir vāḻum oru perumāṭṭikku aṟimukamākum nalviṉiyi āciriyar uvilṭ eṉpavar peṟṟār. tamakku naṇpaṉāyuḷḷavaṉ oruvaṉ pala āṇṭukaḷāka ovvōr iravum pala nāḻiki nēram variyil mutukilum mārpilum koṭiyatoru nōyuṟṟu vantaṉaṉeṉṟum, nāṭcellac cellap piṟaku avaṉ iravuḻuḻutum oru nāṟkāliyil uṭkārntirukka vēṇṭiyavaṉ āyiṉā ṉeṉṟum, ataṉāl avaṉ kālkaḷ vīṅkat tuvaṅkiṉa veṉṟum avvāciriyar apperumāṭṭikkuc colliṉar.
inta nōyāḷi ceṉṟa mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷāka ilaṇṭaṉmā nakarattiluḷḷa palarāl muṟiyāka maruttuvañ ceyyappaṭṭu vantaṉar. neñcappiyil iṉṉateṉṟu viḷaṅkāta ētō oru picaku irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu cilar coṉṉārkaḷ; vēṟucilar atu narampunōy eṉṟu kūṟiṉar; oruvar ataṉip poruttup piṭippu eṉṟār; kaṭiciyāka oruvar mutukelumpu mikavum aḻukip pōyiṟṟu eṉṟār.
uvilṭ eṉpavariṉ nēyar, muṉṉatākac ceytu koṇṭa ēṟpāṭṭiṉpaṭi avari vantu kaṇṭu, mēṟcolliya perumāṭṭiyāriyum pārttār. inta ammiyār cummā avari uṟṟu nōkkiṉār. ataṉ piṉ, avar avvaṟiyi viṭṭuc ceṉṟapiṉ, anta ammiyār am maruttuvari nōkkip piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiṉār: aḵtu ettakiya nōy eṉpati yāṉ kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ. avaruṭampu paḷiṅkupōṟ ṟeḷivāy iruntateṉṉa yāṉ ataṉi avvaḷavu viḷakkamāyk kaṇṭēṉ. avaratu neñcappikkuppiṉṉē kaṭukkāy aḷaviṉatākiya oru kaṭṭi puṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; aḵtu aḻukku niṟamuṭiyatā yirukkiṉṟatu; atu uṭintu pōkumpōl āṭukiṉṟatu. muṟṟilum ōyvāyiruppatit tavira vēṟetuvum avarkku naṉmi ceyyātu.
ataṉmēl uvilṭ eṉpavar kūṟiyatāvatu: `avvammiyār karutic coṉṉatu ituveṉa yāṉ uṭaṉē kaṇṭēṉ. ākavē ataṉip piṉvarumāṟu eṉ naṇpariṉ maruttuvarukku eḻuta uṭkārntēṉ: eṉ naṇparukkuḷḷa nōyiṉ taṉmi iṉṉateṉṟu kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉeṉa nampukiṉṟēṉ iṟakkattil uḷḷa cennīrppiyil avarkku oru vīkkam oru kaṭukkāyaḷavu irukkiṉṟatu. itutāṉ neñcappiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa ciṟitu iṭappeyarcciyi uṇṭākkiyatākum; ikkaṭṭiyāṉatu vilāvelumpu kaḷiṉ iṭiyiluḷḷa narampukaḷi nerukkutalāl mutukiṟ perunōyum mārpiṉ muṉpuṟattuḷḷa tōliṉkaṇ nōyum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. nāḷikku nīṅkaḷ pārkkaccellum maruttuvarukku, yāmaṟinta innōyiṉ niliyit terivittu, avar colvatu iṉṉateṉṟu eṉakku aṟiviyuṅkaḷ.
aṅṅaṉamē annōyāḷi anta maruttuvariṭan terivittatil apperumāṭṭi annōyippaṟṟik kūṟiyatu tāṉ uṇmiyeṉa uṟutip paṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉōṭu annōyi nīkkutaṟkuc ceyya vēṇṭuvatum muṟṟum ōyntiruttalēyā meṉpatum maruttuvar ellārāṉum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. avvāṟē annōyāḷi ōyntirukka aḵtu avariviṭṭu akaṉṟatu. ivvuṇmi varalāṟṟiṉāl, nōyiṉ iyalpi uḷḷavāṟu uṇarntu, ataṉi nīkkutaṟkuc ceyyum muṟiyun tiṭṭamākat teḷintu koḷḷutaṟkut teḷivukkāṭci ettuṇi yutavi ceykiṉṟa teṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō?
18. cila eccarippukaḷ
mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavāṟu aṟituyilip piṟarpāl varuvittu, ataṉutaviyāṟ pala uyarnta naṉmikaḷi viḷivikka vēṇṭupavarkaḷ, itaṉip payiṟci ceykiṉṟa pōtu kaṭṭāyamāyk karuttiṟpatittu naṭakkavēṇṭiya kuṟippukaḷ cila uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ, mutalāvatu, oruvari aṟituyiliṟ celuttukiṉṟapōtu tuvakkattilēyē piṉvarumāṟu colli avarit tamatu kaṭṭaḷikku uṭaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷakkaṭavar: ippōtu ummi aṟituyilukkuc celuttap pōkiṉṟēṉ. ummi aṟituyiliṟ celuttuvatu umakkum eṉakkum ulakattiṟkum naṉmiyi uṇṭākkutaṟ poruṭṭēyām. ātalāl, eṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi nīr aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟu, attuyilil irukkiyil, yāṉ collukiṟapaṭiyē ceytu, tirumpa yāṉ ataṉiṉiṉṟum ummi viḻikkac collukiyil eṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭiyē uṭaṉē viḻittu eḻakkaṭavīr. eṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi yellām uḷḷaṭaṅki naṭakkakkaṭavīr. eṉṟu valiyuṟuttic colli, ataṉpiṟaku avar aṟituyiliṟ celkiyilum iṭiyiṭiyē mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu kaṭṭuṟuttic colluka.
iraṇṭāvatu: avar aṟituyilil irukkiyil, naṉmiyāṉa coṟkaḷiyē teḷivāy valiyuṟuttic colluka. maṟantun tīmiyāṉa coṟkaḷic collaṟka. umatu uṭampu mikavum naṉṟāy irukkiṉṟatu. umatu aṟivu mikavum teḷivāyirukkiṉṟatu. umatu mūḷiyum nuriyīral neñcappi tīṉippi malakkuṭar mutalāṉa akakkaruvikaḷum mikavuñ cevviyāṉa niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. nīr eppōtum nalla niṉivum nalla eṇṇamum uṭiyavarāyiruppīr. nīr eṉṟum nalla ceykikaḷiyē ceyvīr eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttic colluka. avar attūkkattilirukkiyil nōyippaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ ētēṉun tappittavaṟik kūṭavarāmal viḻippāyp pēcuka.
mūṉṟāvatu: aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟavar ataṉiṉiṉṟum eḻuntapiṉ iṉṉiṉṉa nāḷil iṉṉiṉṉa nērattil iṉṉiṉṉavaṟṟic ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟu avar attuyilil irukkiyil valiyuṟuttic coṉṉāl, avar aṅṅaṉamē tām viḻittiruntāluṅ kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭapaṭiyēceyvar.ākiyāṟ, piṉ naṭakka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟic colli avarukkuk kaṟpikkumpōtu, cevviyāy ārāyntu collat takkavaṟṟit tiruttamākac collal vēṇṭum. oruvarit tuyiṟṟukiṉṟa maṟṟoruvar tammittavira vēṟevarum avarit tuyiṟṟutal ākātu eṉṉum eṇṇam uṭiya varāyiruntāl, av eṇṇatti aṟituyilil iruppavarkkut tiṭṭamāyk kūṟuka. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, cilamuṟi aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭṭa oruvar piṟaku evarāṉum eḷitilē aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭutaṟku icintavar āvar. inta uḷavit terinta tīyōr evarēṉum ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟiyit terintukoṇṭu avarukku eḷitilē aṟituyili varuvittu avarit tam vaḻippaṭuttikkoṇṭu avarip palavakiyilum ēmāṟṟavuṅkūṭum. ātalāl, nallōrāṉa tuyiṟṟuvōr tammāl tuyiṟṟappaṭuvōr piṟar evarāṉum iṅṅaṉan tuyiṟṟappaṭutal ākātu eṉṟu kaṭṭurittuc colliviṭṭāṟ, piṉpu immuṟiyiṟ paḻakiya maṟṟevarum avarit tuyilacceytal iyalātu. iṉṉum avar aṟituyilil irukkiyil avarukkut tām kaṭṭuṟuttic colliyavikaḷi avar attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuntapiṉ niṉittalākātu eṉṟu collavēṇṭiṉāl, aṅṅaṉamē colluka; avar viḻittapiṉ attūkkattil nikaḻntavi kaḷi oru ciṟitum uṇarār.
nāṉkāvatu: mūvakippaṭṭa aṟituyiliṟ celuttap paṭṭavarkaḷum anniliyilirukkuṅkāl, avarkaḷukkut tōṉṟumāṟu viḷivitta uruveḷit tōṟṟaṅkaḷi maṟiyumāṟuceytu, avaratu uṇarvu eppōtumpōla oḻuṅkāṉa niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa teṉavuṅ kaṟpittu, avari viḻikkac ceyvataṟku maṟantuviṭalākātu.
aintāvatu: tuyiṟṟappaṭṭavar aṟituyilil irukkum pōtu avariṭam ētēṉuṅ kēṭkavirumpiṉāl `nīr ippōtu cevviyāka irukkiṉṟīrā? nīr ētāvatu immuṟikku eṉakku aṟivikkakkūṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭka vēṇṭumē yallāl `nīr cevviyā yirukkiṉṟīr eṉṟu niṉikkiṉṟēṉ, yāṉ ceyyavēṇṭiyamuṟi iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiviyum eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭuk kēṭṭalākātu. ēṉeṉil, aṟituyilil irukkiyil tuyiṟṟuvōr ētu kaṭṭaḷi yiṭukiṉṟārō akkaṭṭaḷippaṭiyē tuyilvōr ceyvar; kaṭṭaḷi yiṭāmaṟ kēṭṭāl avaratu uḷḷattil appōtu iyaṟkiyākat tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟi urippar. avaṟṟik kēṭṭu uṇmi yiṉṉa teṉṟu uṇarntu ataṟkēṟpac ceyvaṉavaṟṟic ceyyalām.
āṟāvatu: mūvakiyāṉa aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭ ṭōriyum attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuppa vēṇṭuṅ kālattil eḻuppiviṭutaṟkuc ciṟitum maṟantu pōkalākātu. oruvarukku varum aṟituyilāṉatu iṟivaṉatu tiruvaruḷiṉpattil nikaḻvatātalāl, avar avviṉpat tuyiliṉiṉṟuñ caṭutiyil viḻitteḻa viruppam illātavarāy iruppar. atupaṟṟi maṉantaṭumāṟi uḷḷaṅ kalaṅkal vēṇṭām. nīvir vēṇṭum pōtu viḻitteḻa avarukku viruppam illiyāṉāl `evvaḷavu nēran tuyila virumpukiṉṟīr? eṉṟu avariyē viṉāvuka. iraṇṭumaṇi nēram mūṉṟumaṇi nēran tūṅkal vēṇṭumeṉa virumpuvarāyiṉ aṅṅaṉamē avarit tūṅkaviṭuka. iṉṉum pala maṇinēran tūṅkal vēṇṭum eṉparāyiṉ, ataṟku iciya iyalāviṭṭāl `avvaḷavu nēraṅ kūṭātu, iṉṉuṅ kuṟiyac collum, iṉṉuṅ kuṟiyac collum eṉṟu colli avari varavara iṇakkik koṇṭuvantu ari, allatu oṉṟu, allatu oṉṟari, allatu iraṇṭu maṇi nēram variyil ēṟkumāṟu tūṅkaviṭṭu, ataṉpiṉ iraṇṭu kikaḷiyum nīṭṭi, uḷḷaṅkikaḷiyum viralkaḷiyum mēl nōkkit tirumpik, kaṭic ciṟuviral tavira, ēṉiviralkaḷic cērttapaṭiyāy avaratu aṭiyilirun tāyiṉum muḻaṅkāliliruntāyiṉum mēlnōkki muṭivariyil toṭṭēṉun toṭāmalēṉum palakāl taṭavi, avartam neṟṟimēluṅ kaṇṇiṟippikaḷiṉ mēlum vāyiṉāl meṉkāṟṟuṇṭāka ūti `viḻittuk koḷka eṉṟāl viḻittukkoḷvar. aṅṅaṉam viḻikkiyiṟ kaṇṇiṟippi kaḷit tiṟakka māṭṭāmaṟ ciṟitu varuntu varāyiṉ, avari iraṇṭoru nimiṭam cummā irukkaviṭṭu ataṉpiṉ `kaṇṇit tiṟantupārum eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāl avar viḻittukkoḷvar. allatu, yāṉ irupatu eṇṇiyavuṭaṉ viḻittukkoḷvīr eṉṟu collik kikaḷi mēṉōkkit taṭavukiyil `oṉṟu - iraṇṭu - mūṉṟu eṉṟu āḻnta kuraliṟ coṟlik koṇṭē ceṉṟu irupatu muṭintatum `ippōtu nīr viḻittukkoḷḷak kaṭavīr eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭuk kiyit taṭṭiṉāl uṭaṉē viḻittuk koḷvar.eṉṟu itukāṟum yōkanittiri eṉṉum aṟituyilākiya aruṅkalittiṟam iṉitu viḷakkappaṭṭatu. ivvarumaruntiṉum uyarnta kaliyip paḻaki ulakattavar nalam peṟuka!
